Abstract: Autoregressive generation is a powerful approach for high-fidelity image synthesis, but it remains computationally demanding and slow even on the most advanced accelerators. While speculative decoding has been explored to mitigate this bottleneck, existing approaches fail to achieve efficiency gains comparable to those observed in text generation. A key limitation is the target model's high uncertainty during image generation, which leads to high draft token rejection rates. In this work, we identify previously overlooked patterns in the target model's behavior that emerge naturally in tree-based speculative decoding. Specifically, we formalize two properties, semantic interchangeability and convergence, arising from the redundancies in the target model's hidden state representations. By capturing these redundancies across the depth and breadth of the predicted token tree, our method identifies principled opportunities for acceptance relaxation without requiring additional training. Additionally, we enhance standalone drafter performance by injecting the redundancy signals from the target model into drafter training with minimal modification. We evaluate our approach across multiple text-to-image models and drafter architectures. Results show that CASCADE achieves state-of-the-art speedups for drafter-based speculative decoding, with up to 3.6x acceleration, while maintaining image quality and text-prompt fidelity.

</details>


### [37] [Towards multi-modal forgery representation learning for AI-generated video detection and localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07232)
*Dat Le,Khoa Nguyen,Xin Wang,Shu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态架构，联合大语言模型语义分支、时空视觉分支和多尺度部分伪造音频分支，实现AI生成视频的检测与细粒度时间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成视频检测方法受限于单模态或部分模态数据建模，且缺乏细粒度的时序伪造定位能力，难以应对多模态、部分篡改的AI生成视频带来的语义扭曲与滥用风险。

Method: 提出核心架构，联合三个分支：LMM语义分支（大语言模型）、时空视觉分支（ST）和多尺度部分伪造音频分支（PS audio），实现多模态融合的同步检测与细粒度时序定位。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在AI生成视频检测与定位任务上优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该多模态联合架构有效弥补了现有方法在模态覆盖和时序定位粒度上的不足，为AI生成视频的可靠检测提供了新方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI have democratized video creation at scale. AI-generated videos, including partially manipulated clips across visual and audio channels, pose escalating risks of semantic distortion and misuse, which motivates the need for reliable detection tools. Most existing AI-generated video detectors remain limited by single- or partial-modality of data modeling and the lack of fine-grained temporal forgery localization. To address these challenges, our primary novelty introduces a core architecture that jointly integrates an LMM semantic branch with a spatio-temporal (ST) visual branch and a multi-scale partial-spoof (PS) audio branch. This multi-modal approach enables simultaneous detection and fine-grained temporal localization of partially manipulated AI-generated video forgeries. Extensive experiments show that this approach outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [38] [Hard to Read, Easy to Jailbreak: How Visual Degradation Bypasses MLLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07250)
*Zhixue Song,Boyan Han,Yiwei Wang,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态大模型（MLLMs）在视觉上下文压缩范式中的一个关键安全漏洞：降低图像分辨率会意外催化越狱攻击，即使文本仍可辨认，模型的防御能力也急剧下降。作者将这种现象归因于“认知超载”，并提出“结构化认知卸载”策略来缓解风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上下文压缩技术让MLLMs能够高效处理超长上下文，但该范式存在一个被忽视的安全隐患——降低分辨率会意外引发越狱攻击。作者旨在揭示这一漏洞并探索其根本原因与缓解方法。

Method: 通过实验验证不同分辨率下SOTA模型的安全防御性能变化；提出“认知超载”假说解释该现象；设计“结构化认知卸载”策略，通过序列化流水线将视觉转录与安全评估解耦来缓解风险。

Result: SOTA模型的安全防御随分辨率下降而急剧恶化，且该现象在噪声和几何失真等多种视觉扰动下一致存在。所提出的结构化认知卸载策略能够有效缓解这一安全风险。

Conclusion: 视觉上下文压缩范式中存在显著安全风险，降低分辨率会因认知超载而削弱模型的安全审计能力。结构化认知卸载为未来MLLMs的安全设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Recent advancements in visual context compression enable MLLMs to process ultra-long contexts efficiently by rendering text into images. However, we identify a critical vulnerability inherent to this paradigm: lowering image resolution inadvertently catalyzes jailbreaking. Our experiments reveal that the safety defenses of SOTA models deteriorate sharply as resolution degrades, surprisingly persisting even when text remains legible. We attribute this to ``Cognitive Overload'', hypothesizing that the effort required to decipher degraded inputs diverts attentional resources from safety auditing. This phenomenon is consistent across various visual perturbations, including noise and geometric distortion. To address this, we propose a simple ``Structured Cognitive Offloading'' strategy that mitigates these risks by enforcing a serialized pipeline to decouple visual transcription from safety assessment. Our work exposes a significant risk in vision-based compression and provides critical insights for the secure design of future MLLMs.

</details>


### [39] [High-Fidelity Surface Splatting-Based 3D Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07254)
*Nandhana Sunil,Abhirami R Iyer,Avirup Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多视角网格重建中高频几何细节难以恢复的问题，本文提出一种基于紧支多项式核的隐式移动最小二乘法（IMLS），替代传统指数核以实现更好的频率控制，并结合随机正则化与拉普拉斯滤波，在表面重建与渲染任务上均取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如3DGS、NeRF）需后处理提取网格，限制了几何与外观的联合优化；而现有IMLS方法使用指数核难以恢复高频细节。

Method: 1) 提出紧支多项式核替代传统指数核，具有局部支撑性和更高灵活性，增强对频率内容的控制；2) 引入随机正则化与拉普拉斯滤波，进一步提升高频结构的保真度并保持优化稳定性。

Result: 在表面重建和渲染任务上均达到最优性能，几何更精确，视觉效果更锐利。

Conclusion: 紧支多项式核与随机正则化结合，能有效提升IMLS对高频几何的恢复能力，实现端到端的多视角网格重建与渲染。

Abstract: Multi-view mesh reconstruction remains a core challenge in computer graphics and vision, especially for recovering high-frequency geometry from sparse observations. Recent methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) and Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF) rely on post-processing for mesh extraction, thereby limiting joint optimization of geometry and appearance. Implicit Moving Least Squares (IMLS) instead enables direct conversion of point clouds into signed distance and texture fields, supporting end-to-end reconstruction and rendering. However, existing IMLS formulations use exponential kernels that struggle with high-frequency detail. We introduce a compact polynomial kernel with local support and greater flexibility, allowing better control over frequency content and improved geometric fidelity. To further enhance fine details, we incorporate stochastic regularization with Laplacian filtering. Together, these improve the preservation of high-frequency structure while maintaining stable optimization. Experiments show state-of-the-art performance in both surface reconstruction and rendering, yielding more accurate geometry and sharper visuals from multi-view data.

</details>


### [40] [TAS-LoRA: Transformer Architecture Search with Mixture-of-LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07256)
*Jeimin Jeon,Hyunju Lee,Bumsub Ham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对Transformer架构搜索中因超网权重共享导致的特征坍缩问题，本文提出TAS-LoRA方法，通过参数高效的LoRA和混合LoRA专家策略，实现子网特定特征学习，在ImageNet等多个数据集上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TAS方法因超网内权重共享导致子网无法学习特定特征，即特征坍缩问题，限制了子网性能。

Method: 提出TAS-LoRA方法，引入LoRA实现子网特定特征学习；采用MoLE策略，通过轻量级路由器根据子网架构动态分配LoRA专家；提出分组路由器初始化技术，在训练早期促进专家多样化特征学习。

Result: 在ImageNet及CIFAR-10/100、Flowers、CARS、INAT-19等迁移学习基准上，TAS-LoRA有效缓解特征坍缩，性能显著优于当前最优TAS方法。

Conclusion: TAS-LoRA通过引入LoRA和MoLE策略，在保持计算效率的同时有效解决了特征坍缩问题，为TAS提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Transformer architecture search (TAS) discovers optimal vision transformer (ViT) architectures automatically, reducing human effort to manually design ViTs. However, existing TAS methods suffer from the feature collapse problem, where subnets within a supernet fail to learn subnet-specific features, mainly due to the shared weights in a supernet, limiting the performance of individual subnets. To address this, we propose TAS-LoRA, a novel method that introduces parameter-efficient low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to enable subnet-specific feature learning, while maintaining computational efficiency. TAS-LoRA incorporates a Mixture-of-LoRAExperts (MoLE) strategy, where a lightweight router dynamically assigns LoRA experts based on subnet architectures, and introduces a group-wise router initialization technique to encourage diverse feature learning across experts early in training. Extensive experiments on ImageNet and several transfer learning benchmarks, including CIFAR-10/100, Flowers, CARS, and INAT-19, demonstrate that TAS-LoRA mitigates feature collapse effectively, improving performance over state-of-the-art TAS methods significantly.

</details>


### [41] [Adaptive Subspace Projection for Generative Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07257)
*Van-Anh Nguyen,Anh Tuan Bui,Tamas Abraham,Junae Kim,Amardeep Kaur,Rollin Omari,Thuy-Trang Vu,Dinh Phung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptSP方法，通过将语义漂移投影到低维子空间进行精确调整，在不重新训练的情况下解决生成式个性化中的语义崩溃问题（SCP），显著提升提示保真度和上下文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式个性化中，学习到的个性化概念会压制文本提示中的其他上下文细节，导致语义崩溃问题（SCP）。现有方法缺乏对SCP根本原因的分析和有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先分析发现SCP的语义漂移集中在特定低维子空间中，且个性化过程扰动原始概念嵌入使其不稳定。基于此，提出AdaptSP：利用稳定的预训练嵌入作为锚点，隔离语义漂移并将其投影到识别出的子空间中进行精确调整，无需训练。

Result: 实验表明，AdaptSP在保持主体身份的同时，显著提升了提示保真度和上下文对齐能力。

Conclusion: AdaptSP通过低维子空间投影实现训练自由的精准语义调整，有效缓解了生成式个性化中的语义崩溃问题。

Abstract: Generative personalization often suffers from the semantic collapsing problem (SCP), where a learned personalized concept overpowers the rest of the text prompt, causing the model to ignore important contextual details. To address this, we first analyze the underlying cause, revealing that the semantic drift responsible for SCP is not random but is concentrated within a specific low-dimensional subspace. We also discover that the personalization process perturbs the embedding of the original base concept, making it an unstable reference point. Based on these insights, we introduce Test-time Embedding Adjustment with Adaptive Subspace Projection (AdaptSP), a training-free method that uses the stable, pre-trained embedding as an anchor. AdaptSP isolates the semantic drift and projects it onto the identified subspace, performing a precise adjustment that mitigates SCP while maintaining the subject identity. Our experiments show that this targeted approach significantly improves prompt fidelity and contextual alignment.

</details>


### [42] [From Clouds to Hallucinations: Atmospheric Retrieval Hijacking in Remote Sensing Vision-Language RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07273)
*Jiaju Han,Chao Li,Chengyin Hu,Qike Zhang,Xuemeng Sun,Xin Wang,Fengyu Zhang,Xiang Chen,Yiwei Wei,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CloudWeb攻击，通过向遥感图像叠加参数化云/雾图案并在检索阶段优化，实现对多模态RAG系统的检索劫持，使攻击者控制返回的天气相关证据，进而影响下游生成结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗研究主要攻击RAG的检索语料库或视觉模型的最终任务预测，而针对遥感多模态RAG中证据检索阶段的输入空间攻击尚未被探索。

Method: 在遥感图像上叠加参数化的云/雾图案，通过检索导向目标函数进行优化，该函数包括：拉近对抗嵌入与目标天气证据的距离、抑制源场景证据、强制排序分离、正则化自然性和覆盖度。

Result: 在7个数据集、5种CLIP风格检索器上，CloudWeb一致优于基线方法；GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32上Weather@5从0.71%提升至43.29%，下游生成出现可测量的天气幻觉和语义偏移。

Conclusion: 自然外观的大气变化可在生成开始前损害证据检索，暴露了遥感多模态RAG在输入空间的实用故障模式。

Abstract: Multimodal RAG systems increasingly rely on vision-language retrievers to ground visual queries in external textual evidence. Existing adversarial studies on RAG mainly manipulate the retrieval corpus or memory, while attacks on vision-language and remote sensing models typically target end-task predictions. Input-space threats to the evidence retrieval stage of remote sensing multimodal RAG remain underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce CloudWeb, an atmospheric retrieval hijacking attack that modifies only the input image while keeping the retriever, generator, and knowledge base fixed at deployment. CloudWeb overlays parameterized cloud- and haze-like patterns on remote sensing images and optimizes them with a retrieval-oriented objective that pulls adversarial image embeddings toward target atmospheric evidence, suppresses source-scene evidence, enforces rank separation, and regularizes naturalness and coverage. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study of retrieval-stage atmospheric evidence hijacking in remote sensing multimodal RAG. We evaluate CloudWeb on a seven-dataset remote sensing RAG benchmark with five CLIP-style retrievers, including GeoRSCLIP, RemoteCLIP, OpenAI CLIP, and OpenCLIP, together with downstream vision-language generators. Across retrievers, CloudWeb consistently outperforms clean retrieval, handcrafted atmospheric baselines, random cloud perturbations, and fixed variants in injecting weather-related evidence into top-ranked results. On GeoRSCLIP ViT-B/32, Weather@5 increases from 0.71\% to 43.29\%. Downstream generation further shows measurable weather hallucination and semantic shift, indicating that retrieval-stage hijacking can propagate to the final RAG response. These findings reveal a practical failure mode: natural-looking atmospheric changes can compromise evidence retrieval before generation begins.

</details>


### [43] [SplatWeaver: Learning to Allocate Gaussian Primitives for Generalizable Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07287)
*Yecong Wan,Fan Li,Mingwen Shao,Wangmeng Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatWeaver提出了一种可泛化的新视角合成框架，通过引入基数高斯专家和像素级路由机制，能够动态分配高斯原语数量（0到M个），并利用高频先验和路由正则化实现区域自适应的复杂度感知分配，在减少高斯原语数量的同时提升了渲染质量，超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于3D高斯泼溅的可泛化前馈方法为每个像素或体素分配固定数量的高斯原语，忽略了真实场景中空间变化的复杂度，导致在平滑区域浪费原语而在精细结构区域容量不足。

Method: 提出SplatWeaver框架，包含：(1) 基数高斯专家，每个专家专门生成0到M个特定数量的原语；(2) 像素级路由方案，协调专家自适应决定每个空间位置分配的高斯原语数量；(3) 高频先验及相关引导模块和路由正则化，稳定专家选择并促进复杂度感知分配。

Result: 在多种场景的广泛实验中，SplatWeaver在更少高斯原语的情况下，始终优于现有最先进方法，生成了更忠实的新视角渲染结果。

Conclusion: 通过动态分配高斯原语数量并利用高频结构线索引导分配，SplatWeaver实现了更具表现力且更紧凑的3D场景表示，验证了区域自适应原语分配策略的有效性。

Abstract: Generalizable novel view synthesis aims to render unseen views from uncalibrated input images without requiring per-scene optimization. Recent feed-forward approaches based on 3D Gaussian Splatting have achieved promising efficiency and rendering quality. However, most of them assign a fixed number of Gaussians to each pixel or voxel, ignoring the spatially varying complexity of real-world scenes. Such uniform allocation often wastes Gaussian primitives in smooth regions while providing insufficient capacity for fine structures, complex geometry, and high-frequency details. This motivates us to predict region-dependent primitive cardinalities rather than impose a fixed primitive budget everywhere, enabling a more expressive yet compact 3D scene representation. Therefore, we propose SplatWeaver, a generalizable novel view synthesis framework that is able to dynamically allocate Gaussian primitives over different regions in a feed-forward manner. Specifically, SplatWeaver introduces cardinality Gaussian experts and a pixel-level routing scheme, wherein each expert specializes in producing a specific number of primitives from 0 to M, and the routing scheme coordinates these experts to adaptively determine how many Gaussian primitives should be allocated to each spatial location. Moreover, SplatWeaver incorporates a high-frequency prior with attendant guidance module and routing regularization to stabilize expert selection and promote complexity-aware allocation. By leveraging high-frequency structural cues, the routing process is encouraged to assign more Gaussian primitives to fine structures, complex geometry, and textured regions, while suppressing redundant primitives in smooth areas. Extensive experiments across diverse scenarios show that SplatWeaver consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, delivering more faithful novel-view renderings with fewer Gaussian primitives.

</details>


### [44] [Sword: Style-Robust World Models as Simulators via Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping for VLA Policy Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07288)
*Jiaxuan Gao,Yongjian Guo,Zhong Guan,Wen Huang,Wanlun Ma,Xi Xiao,Junwu Xiong,Sheng Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sword框架，通过结构引导风格增强和动态潜在引导策略，解决世界模型在LIBERO基准中的泛化性差和长程误差累积问题，显著提升VLA模型的后训练成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在作为模拟器部署时存在泛化性差和长程误差累积问题，对初始状态扰动敏感（颜色、光照等变化会引发级联幻觉），限制了其作为模拟器的可靠性。

Method: 1) 结构引导风格增强（Structure-Guided Style Augmentation）：解耦交互环境中的视觉纹理与任务相关动态，提升泛化性；2) 动态潜在引导（Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping）：保持训练与推理一致性，同时控制内存消耗。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，Sword在泛化性、生成质量、鲁棒性、保真度以及VLA模型强化学习后训练的成功率上均显著优于基线WoVR。

Conclusion: Sword框架有效缓解了世界模型在闭环部署中的级联幻觉和长程误差累积问题，提升了世界模型作为任务模拟器的可靠性。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with World Models has gained increasing attention. One representative approach treats learned World Models as generative simulators, enabling policy optimization entirely within "imagination." However, when deployed as simulators for specific environments such as the LIBERO benchmark, existing World Models often suffer from poor generalization and long-horizon error accumulation. During closed-loop rollouts, these models are highly sensitive to initial-state perturbations; minor changes in color, illumination, and other visual factors can trigger cascading hallucinations, leading to severe blurriness or overexposure. Moreover, long-horizon error accumulation further degrades the quality and fidelity of predicted future states. These issues limit the reliability of World Models as simulators. To mitigate these problems, we propose Sword, a robust World Model framework. Our method introduces Structure-Guided Style Augmentation to disentangle the visual textures of interactive environments from task-relevant dynamics, thereby improving generalization. We further propose Dynamic Latent Bootstrapping, which maintains consistency between training and inference while keeping memory consumption low. Extensive experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method significantly outperforms the baseline WoVR in terms of generalization, generation quality, robustness, fidelity, and the success rate of reinforcement-learning post-training for VLA models.

</details>


### [45] [EgoPro-Bench: Benchmarking Personalized Proactive Interaction in Egocentric Video Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07299)
*Dongchuan Ran,Linyu Ou,Xueheng Li,Wenwen Tong,Chenxu Guo,Hewei Guo,Kaibing Wang,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoPro-Bench是一个用于训练和评估多模态大模型在流式第一人称视频中主动交互能力的新型基准，包含2400个评估视频和12000+训练视频，覆盖12个领域，并提出了“短思考、更好交互”的原则以优化交互时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要是被动式的，无法持续感知环境或主动协助用户；现有主动交互基准局限于警报场景、忽视个性化上下文、且未能评估人机交互的精确时机。

Method: 构建EgoPro-Bench基准，利用模拟用户画像生成多样化用户意图，构建12个领域的高保真人机交互数据；提出专用评估协议和指标；引入“短思考、更好交互”原则，在意图识别前分配有限token预算以提升交互性能。

Result: EgoPro-Bench显著增强了MLLMs的意图理解能力，使其能够准确识别人机交互的适当时机。

Conclusion: EgoPro-Bench为下一代以用户为中心的主动交互智能体奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain primarily reactive, failing to continuously perceive environments or proactively assist users. While emerging benchmarks address proactivity, they are largely confined to alert scenarios, neglect personalized context, and fail to evaluate the precise timing of human-machine interactions (HMI).In this paper, we introduce EgoPro-Bench, a novel benchmark for training and evaluating proactive interaction capabilities based on streaming egocentric videos; it comprises 2,400 videos in the evaluation set and over 12,000 videos in the training set.Unlike previous works, EgoPro-Bench leverages simulated user profiles to generate diverse user intentions and to construct high-fidelity HMI data across 12 distinct domains.Subsequently, we propose a specialized evaluation protocol and metrics, train proactive interaction models designed for efficient reasoning and low-latency interaction on streaming video data, and conduct comprehensive evaluations.Furthermore, we introduce an interaction principle termed "short thinking, better interaction", which allocates a limited token budget prior to intent recognition, thereby enhancing interaction performance.The experiments demonstrate that EgoPro-Bench substantially enhances the intention understanding capabilities of MLLMs and enables accurate identification of appropriate timings for HMI, thereby laying a solid foundation for next-generation user-centric proactive interactive agents.

</details>


### [46] [Amortized-Precision Quantization for Early-Exit Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07317)
*Rui Fang,Hsi-Wen Chen,Ming-Syan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出APQ和MAQEE方法，通过联合优化退出阈值和量化位宽，在视觉任务中实现高效低精度早期退出推理，减少95%的BOPs同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化方法假设静态全深度执行，当早期退出决策被量化噪声扰动时不稳定，噪声沿动态推理路径放大误差，导致低精度早期退出部署脆弱。

Method: 提出APQ（分摊精度量化）公式化层间量化噪声随机暴露并揭示深度-精度权衡；在此基础上提出MAQEE（互适应量化与早期退出），一个双层框架联合优化退出阈值和位宽，并引入显式风险控制提升推理稳定性。

Result: 在分类、检测和分割任务中，MAQEE建立更优的精度-效率帕累托前沿，减少高达95%的BOPs同时保持精度，超越强基线最多20%。

Conclusion: APQ和MAQEE有效解决了低精度早期退出中量化噪声导致的推理不稳定问题，实现了精度与效率之间更优的权衡。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance across vision tasks, yet their deployment with low-precision early exiting remains fragile. Existing quantization methods assume static full-depth execution, making them unstable when exit decisions are perturbed by quantization noise, which can amplify errors along dynamic inference paths. In this paper, we introduce Amortized-Precision Quantization (APQ), a utilization-aware formulation that accounts for layer-wise stochastic exposure to quantization noise and reveals depth-precision trade-offs. Building on APQ, we propose Mutual Adaptive Quantization with Early Exiting (MAQEE), a bi-level framework that jointly optimizes exit thresholds and bit-widths under explicit risk control to improve inference stability. MAQEE establishes a superior Pareto frontier in the accuracy-efficiency trade-off, reducing BOPs by up to 95% while maintaining accuracy and outperforming strong baselines by up to 20\% across classification, detection, and segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [47] [GEM: Generating LiDAR World Model via Deformable Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07326)
*Yang Wu,Zhaojiang Liu,Qiang Meng,Youquan Liu,Renliang Weng,Jianjun Qian,Jian Yang,Jin Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GEM是一个基于可变形Mamba架构的生成式LiDAR世界模型，通过LiDAR扫描令牌化、动态-静态特征解耦和三路径可变形Mamba扫描，有效解决了点云无序性和动静态目标区分难题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR世界模型的发展滞后于基于摄像头或占据数据的世界模型，主要因两大核心挑战：LiDAR点云固有的无序性以及难以区分动态物体与静态结构。

Method: 1) 利用顺序激光扫描与Mamba处理机制的结构相似性，通过自定义LiDAR场景分词器将LiDAR扫描压缩为紧凑表示；2) 通过动态-静态分离器对分词化特征进行无监督解耦；3) 引入三路径可变形Mamba对解耦特征进行选择性扫描和自适应门控融合，增强时空理解；4) 可选集成规划器和BEV布局控制器以支持自主rollout和"what-if"场景生成。

Result: 在多种基准和评估设置下均达到当前最优性能，展示了方法的优越性和有效性。

Conclusion: GEM通过可变形Mamba架构有效解决了LiDAR世界模型的两大核心挑战，显著提升了保真度和想象力，并具备自主rollout和"what-if"场景生成的潜力。

Abstract: World models, which simulate environmental dynamics and generate sensor observations, are gaining increasing attention in autonomous driving. However, progress in LiDAR-based world models has lagged behind those built on camera videos or occupancy data, primarily due to two core challenges: the inherent disorder of LiDAR point clouds and the difficulty of distinguishing dynamic objects from static structures. To address these issues, we propose GEM: a Generative LiDAR world model that leverages deformable mamba architecture, significantly improving fidelity and imaginative capability. Specifically, leveraging the structural similarity between sequential laser scanning and Mamba's processing mechanism, we first tokenize LiDAR sweeps into compact representations via a custom LiDAR scene tokenizer. After unsupervised disentanglement of tokenized features via a dynamic-static separator, a tri-path deformable Mamba is introduced to perform selective scanning and adaptive gating fusion over the disentangled features, leading to enhanced spatial-temporal understanding of the world evolution. Optionally, a planner and a BEV layout controller can be integrated to explore the model's capability for autonomous rollout and its potential to generate ``what-if" scenarios. Extensive experiments show that GEM achieves state-of-the-art performances across diverse benchmarks and evaluation settings, demonstrating its superiority and effectiveness. Project page: https://github.com/wuyang98/GEM.

</details>


### [48] [GC-ART: Global Learnable Second-Order Rational Tone Curves for Illumination Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07329)
*Wei Huang,Joyce Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GC-ART是一个轻量级可微分的全局自适应有理色调映射预处理模块，通过643参数的MLP从每通道软直方图预测端点固定的有理色调曲线，在分类器前逐点应用。在CIFAR-10上匹配基线干净准确率，在乘性变暗和对比度损坏上优于基线，且计算量远低于卷积式增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像增强方法（如Zero-DCE++）在鲁棒分类任务中表现有限，且卷积式增强器计算开销大。作者希望设计一个轻量级、可微分、能学习全局色调校正的预处理模块，在不牺牲干净准确率的前提下提升模型在多种损坏下的鲁棒性。

Method: GC-ART首先从输入图像提取每通道软直方图，通过643参数的MLP预测端点固定的有理色调曲线参数，然后逐点应用该曲线进行色调映射。整个模块与分类器端到端联合训练，损失函数为交叉熵加软单调性惩罚项。

Result: 在CIFAR-10 + ResNet-18上：干净准确率与基线持平；乘性变暗场景下优于基线；对比度损坏场景下达到48.45%（基线46.27%，Zero-DCE++ 47.13%）。计算量远低于卷积式增强器。超参数未调优，仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 基于直方图条件的有理曲线能够学习有用的全局色调校正（包括对比度扩展行为），通过逐点映射天然保持边缘位置，是一种轻量有效的鲁棒分类预处理方案。

Abstract: We introduce GC-ART (Global Curve Adaptive Rational Tone-mapping), a lightweight differentiable pre-processing module for robust image classification. GC-ART predicts an endpoint-pinned rational tone curve from per-channel soft histograms using a 643-parameter MLP, then applies the curve pointwise before the classifier. The module is trained end-to-end with cross-entropy and a soft monotonicity penalty. On CIFAR-10 with a CIFAR-style ResNet-18, GC-ART matches clean accuracy with the unenhanced baseline and other learned enhancers, improves over the baseline on multiplicative darkening, and achieves the best learned-method result on contrast corruption (48.45% vs. 46.27% for the baseline and 47.13% for Zero-DCE++). These results suggest that histogram-conditioned rational curves can learn useful global tone corrections, including contrast-expanding behavior, while preserving edge locations by construction through pointwise mapping. GC-ART also uses substantially fewer FLOPs than convolutional learned enhancers at 32 x 32. The current hyperparameters are untuned, leaving room for systematic improvement.

</details>


### [49] [RCoT-Seg: Reinforced Chain-of-Thought for Video Reasoning and Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07334)
*Junwei Wen,Deshui Miao,Guangming Lu,Xin Li,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RCoT-Seg是一种视频推理分割框架，通过将任务分解为时序视频推理（TVR）和关键帧目标感知（KTP）两个阶段，利用智能体关键帧选择模块（基于CoT-start语料库初始化和GRPO优化）和SAM2传播机制，解决了现有方法中关键帧选择狭窄、时序理解薄弱的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的视频推理分割方法通过简单采样或辅助MLLM选择帧后使用[SEG]标记预测掩码，存在监督有限且帧-语言相似性规则导致关键帧选择范围狭窄的问题，这削弱了整体时序理解并在复杂多目标场景中产生脆弱的定位结果。

Method: RCoT-Seg将VRS分解为两个阶段：(1) TVR阶段：提出智能体关键帧选择模块，利用精心构建的CoT-start语料库初始化，并通过GRPO在任务对齐奖励下进行优化，通过自我评估生成和重新选择关键帧；(2) KTP阶段：在选定帧上执行高分辨率分割，并采用基于SAM2的方法将掩码传播到整个视频序列，替代启发式采样和外部选择器。

Result: 大量实验结果表明，所提出的RCoT-Seg在多个基准上取得了优于现有最先进方法的表现。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离时序推理与空间感知，RCoT-Seg有效提升了关键帧选择的准确性和时序推理能力，同时通过高分辨率分割和SAM2传播改善了空间精度和帧间一致性。

Abstract: Video Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to segment target objects in videos based on implicit instructions that convey human intent and temporal logic. Existing MLLM-based methods predict masks with a [SEG] token after selecting frames via simple sampling or an auxiliary MLLM, where limited supervision and frame-language similarity rules often yield narrow-scope keyframe choices that weaken holistic temporal understanding and lead to brittle localization in complex multi-object scenes. To address these issues, we introduce RCoT-Seg, a video-of-thought framework that factorizes VRS into temporal video reasoning (TVR) and keyframe target perception (KTP), explicitly separating temporal reasoning from spatial perception. Specifically, in the TVR stage, an agentic keyframe selection module, initialized with a curated CoT-start corpus and refined by GRPO under task-aligned rewards, is proposed to generate and reselect the keyframe through self-evaluation, strengthening moment localization and temporal reasoning. In the KTP stage, RCoT-Seg performs high-resolution segmentation on the selected frame and propagates masks with SAM2-based methods across the sequence, replacing heuristic sampling and external selectors while improving spatial precision and inter-frame consistency. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that the proposed RCoT-Seg achieves favorable performance against the state-of-the-art methods. The code and models will be publicly released at https://github.com/Victor-wjw/RCoT-Seg.

</details>


### [50] [Disambiguating 2D-3D Correspondences in Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields for Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07351)
*Miso Lee,Sangeek Hyun,Yerim Jeon,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplitGS-Loc 提出利用混合高斯分裂和权重筛选策略，将光度优化的高斯特征场转化为精确的一对一 2D-3D 对应关系，从而实现无需逐场景训练或迭代优化的高精度视觉定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于高斯泼溅的特征场（GSFFs）在光度优化后存在固有问题：每个高斯的体积极大使像素-点映射呈多对一关系，导致PnP姿态估计不稳定；光度优化会产生缺乏多视图一致性的冗余高斯。这些缺陷使得GSFFs难以适用于2D-3D匹配和视觉定位。

Method: 1) 基于混合高斯（Mixture-of-Gaussians）的分裂策略：将每个高斯分解为多个更小的高斯，将模糊的多对一对应关系替换为精确的一对一对应关系。2) 利用GS光栅化中的组合权重，筛选出在多视图下显著且一致贡献的高斯，通过强像素-高斯关联聚合判别性特征，强制执行多视图一致性。

Result: 在多个定位基准测试上达到最先进性能，实验验证了该方法能够通过利用高斯属性扩展光度GSFFs的实用性，实现准确且高效的定位。

Conclusion: SplitGS-Loc 通过高斯属性层面的设计（分裂与筛选），有效解决了GSFFs在视觉定位中的内在缺陷，无需逐场景训练或迭代姿态优化，即可实现稳定且高精度的PnP定位。

Abstract: While Gaussian Splatting-based Feature Fields (GSFFs) have shown promise for visual localization, this paper highlights that photometrically optimized GSFFs are inherently ill-suited for 2D-3D matching. The volumetric extent of each Gaussian induces many-to-one pixel-to-point mappings that destabilize PnP-based pose estimation, while photometric optimization gives rise to superfluous Gaussians devoid of multi-view consistency. To address these issues, we propose SplitGS-Loc, a localization-specialized GSFFs construction framework that disambiguates 2D-3D correspondences by exploiting Gaussian attributes. Our key design, Mixture-of-Gaussians-based splitting, decomposes each Gaussian into smaller Gaussians, replacing ambiguous many-to-one with precise one-to-one correspondences. In parallel, we exploit composition weights from GS rasterization to select Gaussians that significantly and consistently contribute across multiple views and aggregate discriminative features through strong pixel-Gaussian associations, enforcing multi-view consistency. The resulting compact yet discriminative feature fields enable stable PnP convergence, achieving state-of-the-art performance on localization benchmarks. Extensive experiments validate that SplitGS-Loc extends the utility of photometric GSFFs to accurate and efficient localization by exploiting Gaussian attributes, without per-scene training or iterative pose refinement.

</details>


### [51] [TTF: Temporal Token Fusion for Efficient Video-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07355)
*Simin Huo,Ning LI*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TTF是一种免训练、即插即用的视频语言模型令牌压缩框架，通过局部窗口相似性搜索融合冗余视觉令牌，在去除约67%视觉令牌的同时保留99.5%的基线准确率，仅引入0.16 GFLOPs的匹配开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频语言模型随着视频长度增加，视觉令牌数量急剧膨胀（如32帧448×448分辨率产生超8000个视觉令牌），导致LLM预填充阶段成为吞吐量瓶颈。现有方法依赖全局相似性或注意力引导压缩，存在额外开销。

Method: 提出Temporal Token Fusion (TTF)：自动选择锚帧，对后续每帧进行局部窗口（如3×3）相似性搜索，融合超过阈值的令牌；通过坐标对齐保持压缩后序列在预填充和解码阶段的位置一致性，实现与现有VLM管道的无缝集成。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B上，阈值t=0.70时，去除约67%的视觉令牌，保留99.5%的基线准确率，仅引入约0.16 GFLOPs的匹配开销。

Conclusion: TTF提供了一种实用、高效的视频理解解决方案，作为免训练即插即用的方法，有效缓解了视频LLM的推理效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) face rapid inference costs as visual token counts scale with video length. For example, 32 frames at $448{\times}448$ resolution already yield >8,000 visual tokens in Qwen3-VL, making LLM prefill the dominant throughput bottleneck. Existing methods often rely on global similarity or attention-guided compression, incurring offsets to their gains. We propose \textbf{Temporal Token Fusion (TTF)}, a training-free, plug-and-play pre-LLM token compression framework that exploits structured temporal redundancy in video. TTF automatically selects an anchor frame, then for each subsequent frame, performs a local window similarity search (e.g.,$3\times 3$), fusing tokens that exceed a threshold. The compressed sequence maintains positional consistency across both prefill and decoding through coordinate realignment, enabling seamless integration with existing VLM pipelines. On Qwen3-VL-8B with threshold t=0.70, TTF removes about 67\% of visual tokens while retaining 99.5\% of the baseline accuracy and introducing only ${\approx}0.16$\,GFLOPs of matching overhead. Overall, TTF offers a practical, efficient solution for video understanding. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}{https://github.com/Cominder/ttf}

</details>


### [52] [UniD-Shift: Towards Unified Semantic Segmentation via Interpretable Share-Private Multimodal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07356)
*Shuai Zhang,Zhecheng Shi,Zhuxiao Li,Jing Ou,Tengxi Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniD-Shift，一种面向大规模3D点云语义分割的联合2D-3D多模态融合框架。通过SAM视觉编码器和SPTNet几何编码器提取互补特征，将特征显式分解为共享子空间和私有子空间，并利用轻量级注意力融合模块对齐共享表征。在SemanticKITTI和nuScenes上超越多模态基线，且在域迁移场景下表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR点云的稀疏采样模式与图像观测中的视角依赖几何畸变导致跨模态对齐困难，现有方法难以实现稳定的多模态融合。

Method: 1) 使用SAM视觉编码器和SPTNet几何编码器分别提取语义和几何特征；2) 将双模态特征显式分解为共享子空间和私有子空间；3) 轻量级注意力融合模块聚合共享特征为一致的跨模态表征；4) 正则化训练目标保证语义对齐和子空间独立性。

Result: 在SemanticKITTI和nuScenes基准上，分割精度一致优于代表性多模态基线，且计算效率具有竞争力；在nuScenes USA-Singapore跨域评估中表现出稳定的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 显式分解共享/私有子空间是多模态点云语义分割的有效策略，能在保持计算效率的同时提升精度和跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation of large-scale 3D point clouds is crucial for applications such as autonomous driving and urban digital twins. However, the sparse sampling pattern of LiDAR and the view-dependent geometric distortion in image observations complicate cross-modal alignment and hinder stable fusion. Inspired by the fact that 2D images captured by cameras are representations of the 3D world, we recognize that the features learned from 2D and 3D segmentation share some common semantics, while other aspects remain modality-specific. This insight motivates a unified multimodal framework for joint 2D-3D semantic segmentation. We combine a SAM-based vision encoder with a SPTNet-based geometric encoder to extract complementary semantic and geometric representations. The resulting features from both modalities are explicitly decomposed into shared and private subspaces, where the shared components summarize semantic factors common to both domains, and the private components preserve properties that are unique to each modality. A lightweight attention-based fusion module aggregates the shared features into a consistent cross-modal representation, and a regularized training objective ensures both semantic alignment and subspace independence. Experiments on the SemanticKITTI and nuScenes benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in segmentation accuracy over representative multimodal baselines, accompanied by competitive computational efficiency. Cross-domain evaluation on nuScenes USA-Singapore shows stable performance under distribution shifts, demonstrating strong generalization. The implementation code is publicly available at: https://github.com/shuaizhang69/UniD-Shift.

</details>


### [53] [A Marine Debris Detection Framework for Ocean Robots via Self-Attention Enhancement and Feature Interaction Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07388)
*Yuyang Li,Jiashu Han,Yinyi Lai,Wenbin Kang,Zenghui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-MD，一种增强型YOLO检测框架，通过双分支卷积增强自注意力模块(DB-CASA)、轻量级移位操作和SFG-Loss动态样本重加权，解决海洋垃圾检测中图像模糊、复杂背景和小目标等挑战。在UODM数据集上达到0.875精度、0.822 F1-score和0.849 mAP50，超越现有最先进方法，并通过机器人边缘部署实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾检测对生态保护至关重要，但现有方法在低质量图像（模糊、复杂背景、小目标）下性能严重下降，亟需一种鲁棒且高效的检测框架以提升实际部署效果。

Method: 1) 提出双分支卷积增强自注意力模块(DB-CASA)，增强空间-通道交互以改善退化图像中的特征表示；2) 引入轻量级移位操作，在不显著增加参数量的前提下提升多尺度目标的细粒度特征提取能力；3) 提出SFG-Loss，通过动态样本重加权缓解类别不平衡和优化不稳定性问题。

Result: 在UODM数据集上，YOLO-MD达到0.875精度、0.822 F1-score和0.849 mAP50，优于最新最先进方法；真实机器人边缘部署实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: YOLO-MD通过结合双重注意力机制、轻量级特征提取和动态损失函数，有效解决了海洋垃圾检测中的多类挑战，在学术指标和实际部署中均表现优异。

Abstract: Marine debris detection for ocean robot is crucial for ecological protection, yet performance is often degraded by low-quality images with blur, complex backgrounds, and small targets. To address these challenges, we propose YOLO-MD, an enhanced YOLO-based detection framework. A Dual-Branch Convolutional Enhanced Self-Attention (DB-CASA) module is designed to strengthen spatial-channel interactions, improving feature representation in degraded images. Additionally, a lightweight shift-based operation is introduced to enhance fine-grained feature extraction for objects of varying scales while maintaining parameter efficiency. We further propose SFG-Loss to mitigate class imbalance and optimization instability via dynamic sample reweighting. Experiments on the UODM dataset demonstrate that YOLO-MD achieves 0.875 precision, 0.822 F1-score, and 0.849 mAP50, outperforming the latest state-of-the-art methods. The effectiveness of this method has also been verified through real-world robotic edge deployment experiments.

</details>


### [54] [ST-Gen4D: Embedding 4D Spatiotemporal Cognition into World Model for 4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07390)
*Haonan Wang,Hanyu Zhou,Tao Gu,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ST-Gen4D框架，通过时空认知（全局外观图与局部动态图融合）引导4D高斯生成，在3D/4D任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D生成模型仅保证全局外观一致性，无法揭示物理世界的局部动态拓扑，缺乏对4D时空规律的本质认知。

Method: 1) 多模态时空表征编码；2) 时空认知：构建全局外观图与局部动态图，通过语义桥接融合为4D认知图；3) 世界模型基于4D认知图推理未来状态；4) 以认知结果为条件引导潜在扩散模型生成4D高斯。

Result: 在3D和4D生成任务上均优于现有方法，验证了结构合理性与拓扑一致性。

Conclusion: 深度融合4D内在认知与生成先验，能够同时保证全局外观连贯与局部动态拓扑一致，是4D生成的有效范式。

Abstract: Generative models have achieved success in producing apparently coherent 2D videos, but remain challenging in the physical world due to lack of 4D spatiotemporal scale. Typically, existing 4D generative models directly embed macro scale constraints to enhance overall spatiotemporal consistency. However, these methods only ensure global appearance coherence and fail to reveal the local dynamics of the physical world. Our insight is that global appearance structure and local dynamic topology empower 4D spatiotemporal cognition, thereby enabling 4D generation with spatiotemporal regularities. In this work, we propose ST-Gen4D, a 4D generation framework with 4D spatiotemporal cognition-based world model. Our model is guided by four key designs: 1) Spatiotemporal representation. We encode various modalities into multiple representations as a feature basis. 2) Spatiotemporal cognition. We sculpture these representations into global appearance graph and local dynamic graph, and fuse them via semantic-bridged spatiotemporal fusion to obtain a 4D cognition graph. 3) Spatiotemporal reasoning. We utilize a world model to derive future state based on the 4D cognition. 4) Spatiotemporal generation. We leverage the derived cognition as condition to guide latent diffusion for 4D Gaussian generation. By deeply integrating 4D intrinsic cognition with generative priors, our model guarantees the structural rationality and topological consistency of 4D generation. Moreover, we propose ST-4D datasets by aggregating public 4D datasets and self-built subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our ST-Gen4D across 3D and 4D generation tasks.

</details>


### [55] [BalCapRL: A Balanced Framework for RL-Based MLLM Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07394)
*Shaokai Ye,Vasileios Saveris,Yihao Qian,Jiaming Hu,Elmira Amirloo,Peter Grasch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的平衡图像描述框架，通过GDPO风格奖励解耦归一化和长度条件奖励掩码，联合优化准确性、覆盖率和语言质量，在多个模型上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像描述的强化学习方法及评估指标过于强调单一维度的质量，导致不同核心维度（如实用性与流畅性）之间存在权衡，缺乏一个能同时兼顾多目标优化的平衡框架。

Method: 采用GDPO风格的奖励解耦归一化处理连续值奖励，并引入长度条件奖励掩码作为更合适的长度惩罚项，构建联合优化实用性准确性、参考覆盖率和语言质量的连续多目标奖励框架。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B、Qwen2.5-VL 3B和7B等多个基座模型上，DCScore提升+13.6，CaptionQA提升+9.0，CapArena提升+29.0。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效平衡图像描述的多维质量指标，优于原始GRPO，并在不同规模和架构的模型上展现出泛化能力。

Abstract: Image captioning is one of the most fundamental tasks in computer vision. Owing to its open-ended nature, it has received significant attention in the era of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In pursuit of ever more detailed and accurate captions, recent work has increasingly turned to reinforcement learning (RL). However, existing captioning-RL methods and evaluation metrics often emphasize a narrow notion of caption quality, inducing trade-offs across core dimensions of captioning. For example, utility-oriented objectives can encourage noisy, hallucinated, or overlong captions that improve downstream question answering while harming fluency, whereas arena-style objectives can favor fluent but generic descriptions with limited usefulness. To address this, we propose a more balanced RL framework that jointly optimizes utility-aware correctness, reference coverage, and linguistic quality. In order to effectively optimize the resulting continuous multi-objective reward formulation, we apply GDPO-style reward-decoupled normalization to continuous-valued captioning rewards and show that it improves performance over vanilla GRPO. Additionally, we introduce length-conditional reward masking, yielding a more suitable length penalty for captioning. Across LLaVA-1.5-7B and Qwen2.5-VL 3B and 7B base models, our method consistently improves caption quality, with peak gains of +13.6 DCScore, +9.0 CaptionQA, and +29.0 CapArena across different models.

</details>


### [56] [Exposing and Mitigating Temporal Attack in Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07398)
*Zheyuan Gu,Minghao Shao,Zhen Wang,Yusong Wang,Mingkun Xu,Shijie Zhang,Hao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对时空深度伪造检测器易受规避攻击且过度依赖脆弱时域频谱线索的问题，提出SpInShield——一种时域谱不变防御框架，通过学习频谱对抗器合成严重频谱变形并采用捷径抑制优化策略，在模拟幅度谱攻击下AUC比最强基线高21.30个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空深度伪造检测器虽AUC高，但易受规避攻击，因为它们倾向于过拟合脆弱的时域频谱线索，而非学习鲁棒的语义因果关系。

Method: 提出SpInShield框架，包括：(1) 可学习的频谱对抗器，动态合成严重的频谱变形以模拟极端攻击场景；(2) 捷径抑制优化策略，强制编码器提取可靠取证线索并从隐空间中清除不稳定频谱统计特征。

Result: 在广泛使用的数据集上取得有竞争力的性能，在模拟幅度谱攻击下AUC比最强基线高出21.30个百分点。

Conclusion: SpInShield通过解耦语义运动与可操纵的频谱伪影，有效提升了深度伪造检测器对规避攻击的鲁棒性。

Abstract: While spatiotemporal deepfake detectors achieve high AUC, our experiments reveal their susceptibility to evasion attacks. These models tend to overfit on fragile temporal spectrum cues, rather than learning robust semantic causality. To mitigate this vulnerability, we propose SpInShield, a temporal spectral-invariant defense framework explicitly designed to decouple semantic motion from manipulatable spectral artifacts. We propose a learnable spectral adversary that dynamically synthesizes severe spectral deformations, simulating extreme attack scenarios. By employing a shortcut suppression optimization strategy, SpInShield compels the encoder to extract reliable forensic cues while purging unstable spectral statistics from the latent space. Experiments show that SpInShield obtains competitive performance on widely used datasets and outperforms the strongest baseline by 21.30 percentage points in AUC under simulated amplitude spectral attacks.

</details>


### [57] [GPO-V: Jailbreak Diffusion Vision Language Model by Global Probability Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07399)
*Yu Pan,Andi Zhang,Yi Wang,Sibei Yang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了扩散视觉语言模型（dVLMs）看似对传统越狱攻击（固定前缀优化FPO）具有抵抗力，但实际上存在独特的拒绝模式（即时拒绝与渐进拒绝）。作者利用渐进去噪过程发现了潜在攻击面，并提出全局概率优化（GPO）这一通用越狱范式，以及首个针对dVLMs的视觉模态越狱框架GPO-V。实验证明GPO-V具有强跨模型迁移性，揭示了非序列生成架构中的关键安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dVLMs被认为对传统越狱攻击具有鲁棒性，但这种看似安全的表象具有欺骗性。作者希望通过深入分析dVLMs的安全机制，发现其独特的拒绝模式，并利用去噪轨迹中潜在的攻击面来突破其安全护栏。

Method: 1) 将传统越狱攻击归类为固定前缀优化（FPO）；2) 分析dVLMs的两种拒绝模式：即时拒绝和渐进拒绝；3) 提出全局概率优化（GPO），一种针对掩码扩散模型去噪轨迹的通用越狱范式；4) 基于GPO设计首个视觉模态越狱框架GPO-V，生成具有跨模型迁移性的隐蔽扰动。

Result: GPO-V能够产生具有优异跨模型迁移性的隐蔽扰动，成功突破dVLMs的安全护栏，揭示了非序列自回归生成架构中存在关键安全漏洞。

Conclusion: dVLMs面临独特的安全对齐挑战，当前防御范式需要立即进行根本性重新评估，以应对基于扩散的生成模型所特有的风险。

Abstract: Diffusion Vision-Language Models (dVLMs), built upon the non-causal foundations of Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs), have demonstrated remarkable efficacy in multimodal tasks by departing from the traditional autoregressive generation paradigm. While dVLMs appear inherently robust against conventional jailbreak tactics, which we categorize as Fixed Prefix Optimization (FPO) (e.g., anchoring responses with "Sure, here is"), this perceived resilience is deceptive. Our investigation into the safety landscape of dVLMs reveals a unique refusal pattern: Immediate Refusal and Progressive Refusal. We find that while FPO-based attacks often fail by triggering the latter, the progressive refinement process itself uncovers a novel, latent attack surface. To exploit this vulnerability, we propose Global Probability Optimization (GPO), a general jailbreak paradigm designed specifically for the denoising trajectory of masked diffusion models. Unlike prefix-based methods, GPO manipulates the global generative dynamics to bypass guardrails in diffusion language models. Building on this, we introduce GPO-V, the first visual-modality jailbreak framework tailored for dVLMs. Empirical results demonstrate that GPO-V produces stealthy perturbations with exceptional cross-model transferability, revealing a critical security gap in non-sequential generative architectures. Our findings underscore the critical urgency of addressing safety alignment in dVLMs. These results necessitate an immediate and fundamental re-evaluation of current defense paradigms to mitigate the unique risks of diffusion-based generation. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GPO-V-0250.

</details>


### [58] [InsHuman: Towards Natural and Identity-Preserving Human Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07402)
*Jie Li,Shulian Zhang,Yangyang Gao,Wenbo Li,Yulun Zhang,Yong Guo,Jian Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsHuman提出了一种面向身份保持的人体插入方法，通过人体-背景自适应融合（HBAF）、人脸身份保持（FFIP）和双向数据配对（BDP）策略，解决了现有模型在姿势不适配、人数不一致和身份改变等问题，并在构建的高质量数据集上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在进行人体插入时存在三个主要问题：人体姿态与背景不匹配、人数不一致以及面部身份改变；同时，公开的人体数据集缺乏全身肖像和真实的人-背景物理交互。

Method: 1）Human-Background Adaptive Fusion (HBAF)：检测前景人体获得二值掩码，采用区域感知加权对齐预测与真实潜空间中的人体区域，确保姿态、数量和外观与目标背景协调；2）Face-to-Face ID-Preserving (FFIP)：检测并匹配生成图像与源图像的人脸，利用人脸识别特征强制保持每个面部的身份一致性；3）Bidirectional Data Pairing (BDP)：构建包含真实人-背景交互的高质量数据集BDP-InsHuman。

Result: 实验表明InsHuman在生成合理图像的同时保持人体身份不变，在各项指标上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: InsHuman通过三项关键技术有效解决了人体插入中的姿态适配、人数控制和身份保持难题，为身份保持的人体图像生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Human insertion aims to naturally place specific individuals into a target background. Although existing image editing models may have such ability, they often produce failure cases, including inappropriate human pose in new background, inconsistent number of people, and modified facial identity. Moreover, publicly available human datasets often lack full-body portraits and realistic physical interaction between humans and their background. To address these challenges, we propose InsHuman for natural and identity-preserving human insertion. Specifically, we propose Human-Background Adaptive Fusion (HBAF), which detects foreground humans to obtain a binary mask and applies region-aware weighting to align the human regions between predicted and ground-truth latents, ensuring the person's pose, count, and overall appearance are coherently adapted to the target background.We further propose Face-to-Face ID-Preserving (FFIP), which detects and matches faces between the generated image and the source image in terms of face recognition features to enforce identity consistency for each face.In addition, we propose Bidirectional Data Pairing (BDP) strategy to construct BDP-InsHuman, a high-quality dataset with realistic human-background interactions. Experiments demonstrate that InsHuman achieves significant improvements in generating plausible images while keeping human identity unchanged.

</details>


### [59] [ChartREG++: Towards Benchmarking and Improving Chart Referring Expression Grounding under Diverse referring clues and Multi-Target Referring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07415)
*Tianhao Niu,Ziyu Han,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统性的图表指代表达定位基准，支持多种定位形式、多个引用目标、多样化的定位线索和图表类型，并利用代码驱动合成流水线生成像素级实例掩码训练实例分割模型，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表指代表达定位基准存在四个局限：(1)主要使用边界框，限制了对细粒度图表元素的定位精度；(2)大多假设单个或两个引用目标实例，无法处理多实例引用；(3)语言表达过度依赖文本线索或数据排序线索；(4)覆盖的图表类型范围狭窄。

Method: (1)构建系统性基准，支持多种定位形式、多个引用目标、多样化定位线索和图表类型；(2)提出代码驱动的合成流水线，利用绘图程序与渲染图表基元之间的对齐关系，生成像素级精确实例掩码；(3)用合成掩码训练实例分割模型，并将其集成到通用多模态定位框架中。

Result: 在多模态大模型上的评估显示存在显著性能差距；所提系统在基准上一致优于基线方法，并能很好地泛化到ChartQA导出的真实图表定位基准上。

Conclusion: 现有图表指代表达定位基准存在多方面不足，本文提出的系统性基准和代码驱动的合成流水线方法能有效提升定位性能，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Referring expression grounding is a core problem in visual grounding and is widely used as a diagnostic of spatial grounding and reasoning in vision and language models, yet most prior work focuses on natural images. In contrast, existing chart referring expression grounding-related benchmarks remain limited: (1) they largely adopt bounding boxes, constraining localization precision for fine chart elements (2) they mostly assume a single and two referred target instances, failing to handle multi-instance target references; (3) the language expressions over-rely on textual cues or data-rank clues (4) they cover only a narrow range of chart types. To address these issues, we introduce a chart referring expression grounding benchmark that systematically supports multiple localization forms, multiple referred targets, diverse grounding cues and diverse chart types. Results across representative multimodal large models reveal a significant performance gap. We further introduce a code-driven synthesis pipeline that exploits the inherent alignment between plotting programs and rendered chart primitives to derive pixel accurate instance masks across chart element types and granularities. We train an instance segmentation model with the synthesized masks and integrate it into a general-purpose multimodal grounding framework. The resulting system consistently outperforms baselines on our benchmark and generalizes well to a ChartQA-derived real-chart grounding benchmark.

</details>


### [60] [Learning Image-Adaptive Scale Fields for Metric Depth Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07418)
*Yuanyan Li,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种图像自适应尺度场建模方法，通过将度量深度恢复表示为低维线性组合，利用稀疏度量锚点高效恢复准确的度量深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计通常只能得到未知尺度或偏移的相对深度，在只有稀疏度量锚点的情况下恢复准确的度量深度具有挑战性，但这对实际应用至关重要。

Method: 将度量深度恢复建模为图像自适应尺度场，通过从MDE估计和中间表示中提取的语义和几何线索构建低维基映射，利用稀疏度量锚点通过最小二乘问题高效求解基映射权重。

Result: 在多个数据集和代表性MDE模型上的实验表明，该方法在度量深度精度、极端锚点稀疏性下的鲁棒性方面均有提升，且具有可解释的空间尺度变化分解能力。

Conclusion: 该方法有效且具有通用适用性，能够从稀疏度量锚点中准确恢复度量深度，并提供了可解释的尺度场分解。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation (MDE) typically produces depth estimations that are defined up to an unknown scale or shift. When only sparse metric anchors are available, recovering accurate metric depth becomes challenging yet necessary for practical applications. We address this problem by formulating metric depth recovery as image-adaptive scale field modeling. Instead of directly correcting the depth, we reformulate the correction as a low-dimensional linear combination of image-adaptive basis maps. These maps are derived from semantic and geometric cues encoded in the MDE estimations and intermediate representations. The weights of basis maps are efficiently determined from sparse metric anchors via a least-squares problem. This formulation yields improved metric depth accuracy, strong robustness under extreme anchor sparsity, and an interpretable decomposition of spatial scale variations. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and representative MDE models demonstrate the effectiveness and general applicability of our approach.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Photorealistic and Efficient Bokeh Rendering via Diffusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07429)
*Linxiao Shi,Siming Zheng,Zerong Wang,Hao Zhang,Jinwei Chen,Bo Li,Shifeng Chen,Peng-Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagicBokeh是一个基于扩散模型的统一框架，通过替代训练策略和焦点感知掩码注意力机制，联合优化散景渲染和超分辨率，同时引入退化感知深度模块，在低分辨率输入上高效生成高质量、可控的散景效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动设备因紧凑光学设计（小光圈）难以产生自然的散景效果；现有学习方法在处理高数码变焦（低分辨率、细节丢失）图像时表现不佳；直接将图像增强与散景渲染串联的两阶段方案效率低且误差累积。

Method: 1) 基于扩散模型的统一框架，联合优化散景渲染和超分辨率；2) 替代训练策略；3) 焦点感知掩码注意力机制；4) 退化感知深度模块，提升低质量输入下的深度估计精度。

Result: MagicBokeh能够在真实低分辨率图像上高效生成照片级真实的散景效果，显著提升视觉保真度和可控性。

Conclusion: 提出的统一扩散框架有效解决了低质量输入下的散景渲染难题，为散景渲染技术的未来进步铺平了道路。

Abstract: Existing mobile devices are constrained by compact optical designs, such as small apertures, which make it difficult to produce natural, optically realistic bokeh effects. Although recent learning-based methods have shown promising results, they still struggle with photos captured under high digital zoom levels, which often suffer from reduced resolution and loss of fine details. A naive solution is to enhance image quality before applying bokeh rendering, yet this two-stage pipeline reduces efficiency and introduces unnecessary error accumulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose MagicBokeh, a unified diffusion-based framework designed for high-quality and efficient bokeh rendering. Through an alternative training strategy and a focus-aware masked attention mechanism, our method jointly optimizes bokeh rendering and super-resolution, substantially improving both controllability and visual fidelity. Furthermore, we introduce degradation-aware depth module to enable more accurate depth estimation from low-quality inputs. Experimental results demonstrate that MagicBokeh efficiently produces photorealistic bokeh effects, particularly on real-world low-resolution images, paving the way for future advancements in bokeh rendering. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/vivoCameraResearch/MagicBokeh.

</details>


### [62] [Sparse Autoencoders as Plug-and-Play Firewalls for Adversarial Attack Detection in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07447)
*Hao Wang,Yiqun Sun,Pengfei Wei,Lawrence B. Hsieh,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEgis，一个基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的轻量级对抗攻击检测框架，通过插入预训练VLM并利用稀疏潜特征检测对抗扰动图像，无需对抗训练即可实现域内、跨域和跨攻击场景下的可靠检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）在现实应用（尤其是智能体系统）中部署日益广泛，但其安全性关注不足，现有VLM（包括商业和开源模型）仍易受对抗攻击，下游应用面临重大风险。

Method: 在预训练VLM中插入SAE模块，使用标准重建目标训练；利用学习到的稀疏潜特征中自然捕获的攻击相关信号，对输入图像是否被对抗扰动进行分类；支持从多个层聚合信号以提升鲁棒性。

Result: 在域内、跨域和跨攻击设置下均取得强性能，尤其在跨域泛化方面显著优于现有基线；多层级信号融合进一步提升了鲁棒性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 首次探索SAE作为VLM中即插即用的对抗攻击检测机制，无需额外对抗训练、引入极小开销，为提升现实VLM系统的安全性提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have advanced rapidly and are increasingly deployed in real-world applications, especially with the rise of agent-based systems. However, their safety has received relatively limited attention. Even the latest proprietary and open-weight VLMs remain highly vulnerable to adversarial attacks, leaving downstream applications exposed to significant risks. In this work, we propose a novel and lightweight adversarial attack detection framework based on sparse autoencoders (SAEs), termed SAEgis. By inserting an SAE module into a pretrained VLM and training it with standard reconstruction objectives, we find that the learned sparse latent features naturally capture attack-relevant signals. These features enable reliable classification of whether an input image has been adversarially perturbed, even for previously unseen samples. Extensive experiments show that SAEgis achieves strong performance across in-domain, cross-domain, and cross-attack settings, with particularly large improvements in cross-domain generalization compared to existing baselines. In addition, combining signals from multiple layers further improves robustness and stability. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to explore SAE as a plug-and-play mechanism for adversarial attack detection in VLMs. Our method requires no additional adversarial training, introduces minimal overhead, and provides a practical approach for improving the safety of real-world VLM systems.

</details>


### [63] [A Unified Framework for the Detection and Classification of Fatty Pancreas in Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07466)
*Ioan-Tudor-Alexandru Anghel,Ciprian-Mihai Ceausescu,Elena Dana Nedelcu,Elena Raluca Stirban,Camelia Croitoru,Despina Ungureanu,Ana Maria Palan,Gabriela Pop*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于TransUNet分割引导的超声图像脂肪胰自动分类框架，通过模拟临床医生对胰腺回声强度的判断逻辑，实现了无需标注训练数据的无监督分类（准确率87.8%）和有监督分类（SVM准确率89.7%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非酒精性脂肪胰疾病（NAFPD）是未充分诊断的疾病，与代谢综合征、胰岛素抵抗及胰腺癌风险增加相关。当前诊断依赖临床医生对超声图像的主观视觉评估，缺乏自动化的客观分类方法。

Method: 采用TransUNet架构（ResNet编码器+Transformer瓶颈）进行胰腺和脾静脉分割；基于解剖结构引导的补丁提取；通过对比胰周脂肪回声与胰腺实质回声进行成对纹理比较，模拟临床推理；分割模型通过肝脏分割任务的领域特异性迁移学习初始化。

Result: 在214例腹部超声图像数据集上（107例专家标注），5折交叉验证：SVM（RBF核）平均准确率89.7%±1.8%，F1值0.898±0.019；无监督K-Means基线准确率87.8%。

Conclusion: 首次提出端到端的超声图像脂肪胰自动分类框架，基于分割引导的纹理分析提取的临床相关特征即便在无标注训练数据下也能有效分类。

Abstract: Non-alcoholic fatty pancreas disease (NAFPD) is an underdiagnosed condition associated with metabolic syndrome, insulin resistance, and increased risk of pancreatic cancer. Diagnosis typically relies on subjective visual assessment of ultrasound images by clinicians. We propose an end-to-end framework for automatically classifying normal versus fatty pancreas from abdominal ultrasound images. Our method employs a TransUNet-based segmentation architecture with a ResNet encoder and transformer bottleneck to delineate the pancreas and the splenic vein, followed by anatomically-guided patch extraction and patient-level classification through pairwise texture comparison. The feature engineering mimics clinical reasoning by comparing the echogenicity of peri-venous fat to the pancreatic parenchyma, providing an interpretable signal for classification. The segmentation models are initialized via domain-specific transfer learning from a liver segmentation task. We validate the full pipeline on a clinical dataset of 214 abdominal ultrasound images with 107 expert-labeled cases using 5-fold cross-validation. SVM with RBF kernel achieves a mean cross-validated accuracy of 89.7\%\,$\pm$\,1.8\% and F1 of 0.898\,$\pm$\,0.019, while the unsupervised K-Means baseline reaches 87.8\% accuracy, demonstrating that the proposed features capture the relevant clinical signal even without labeled training data. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end automated framework for fatty pancreas classification from ultrasound using segmentation-guided texture analysis.

</details>


### [64] [Consensus Entropy: Harnessing Multi-VLM Agreement for Self-Verifying and Self-Improving OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.11101)
*Yulong Zhang,Tianyi Liang,Xinyue Huang,Erfei Cui,Guoqing Wang,Xu Guo,Chenhui Li,Gongshen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Consensus Entropy (CE)，一种无需训练、与模型无关的指标，通过测量多模型间输出一致性的熵来估计OCR输出的可靠性；基于CE构建CE-OCR框架，实现轻量级多模型验证、择优与自适应路由，显著提升OCR质量验证的F1分数（比VLM-as-Judge提升42.1%），且无需监督训练即可即插即用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在OCR任务中虽然平均准确率提升，但在样本级别的错误检测方面仍然薄弱，且缺乏有效的无监督质量控制方法。

Method: 提出Consensus Entropy (CE)，通过计算多个模型输出之间的一致性熵来评估输出可靠性，核心假设是正确预测在输出空间收敛而错误预测发散。基于CE构建CE-OCR框架，包含集成验证、最优输出选择和自适应路由机制。

Result: CE在质量验证上比VLM-as-Judge方法提升F1分数42.1%；CE-OCR在相同成本下一致优于self-consistency和单模型基线。

Conclusion: CE是一种无需训练、即插即用的OCR质量验证指标，能有效提升OCR输出的可靠性，且具有良好的模型无关性和通用性。

Abstract: Optical Character Recognition (OCR) is fundamental to Vision-Language Models (VLMs) and high-quality data generation for LLM training. Yet, despite progress in average OCR accuracy, state-of-the-art VLMs still struggle with detecting sample-level errors and lack effective unsupervised quality control. We introduce Consensus Entropy (CE), a training-free, model-agnostic metric that estimates output reliability by measuring inter-model agreement entropy. The core insight is that correct predictions converge in output space, while errors diverge. Based on CE, we develop CE-OCR, a lightweight multi-model framework that verifies outputs by ensemble agreement, selects the best outputs, and further improves efficiency through adaptive routing. Experiments demonstrate that CE is robust for quality verification, improving F1 scores by 42.1% over VLM-as-Judge. CE-OCR achieves consistent OCR gains, outperforming self-consistency and single-model baselines at the same cost. Notably, CE requires no training or supervision, enabling plug-and-play integration. Code: https://github.com/Aslan-yulong/consensus-entropy.

</details>


### [65] [ForgeVLA: Federated Vision-Language-Action Learning without Language Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07474)
*Yuhao Zhou,Yunpeng Zhu,Yang Zhou,Jindi Lyu,Jian Lan,Zhangyuan Wang,Dan Si,Thomas Seidl,Qing Ye,Jiancheng Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForgeVLA提出了一种联邦学习框架，用于从分布式视觉-动作对中训练VLA模型，无需集中原始数据或人工标注，通过指令分类器恢复缺失的语言模态，并解决视觉-语言特征坍塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型扩展受到标注训练数据获取成本高昂的严重瓶颈，而部署的机器人产生的视觉-动作对虽丰富但因约束无法集中聚合且存在严重异质性。

Method: ForgeVLA框架中，每个客户端配备具身指令分类器将视觉-动作对映射到预定义指令集以恢复语言模态；结合客户端对比规划损失与服务器端自适应聚合策略来学习任务判别性表示，缓解视觉-语言特征坍塌。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，ForgeVLA显著优于其他基线方法，消融研究验证了各组件的贡献。

Conclusion: ForgeVLA通过联邦学习范式有效利用分布式未标注的视觉-动作数据训练VLA模型，解决了数据异质性和特征坍塌问题，为机器人通用智能的大规模训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models hold great promise for general-purpose robotic intelligence, yet scaling up such models is severely bottlenecked by the high cost of acquiring annotated training data. Fortunately, vision-equipped robots deployed across various domains already produce abundant vision-action pairs that can be leveraged to scale up VLA training more efficiently. However, these raw data cannot be centrally aggregated due to various constraints and also exhibit severe heterogeneity. To address these challenges, in this paper, we propose ForgeVLA, a federated VLA training framework that learns VLA models from distributed vision-action pairs without centralizing raw data or requiring manual annotations. Specifically, each client in ForgeVLA is equipped with an embodied instruction classifier that maps vision-action pairs to a predefined instruction set, recovering the missing language modality and forming complete vision-language-action triplets. Beyond triplet construction, we also identify vision-language feature collapse as a critical challenge that has been largely overlooked in prior federated VLA research. To mitigate this issue, ForgeVLA combines a client-side contrastive planning loss with a server-side adaptive aggregation strategy to learn task-discriminative representations efficiently. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ForgeVLA significantly outperforms other baselines, and ablation studies further validate the contribution of each component.

</details>


### [66] [ReasonEdit: Towards Interpretable Image Editing Evaluation via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07477)
*Honghua Chen,Zitong Xu,Huiyu Duan,Xinyun Zhang,Xiongkuo Min,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReasonEdit-22K数据集（22K编辑图像、113K思维链样本、1.3M人工判断）和RE-Reward奖励模型，以及基于GRPO训练的ReasonEdit可解释评估模型，用于文本引导图像编辑的透明化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TIE评估方法依赖标量分数、缺乏可解释性，且缺乏高质量解释数据集和有效奖励模型来训练可解释评估器。

Method: 构建ReasonEdit-22K数据集，提出RE-Reward（基于MLLM的奖励模型），使用GRPO算法训练ReasonEdit可解释评估模型。

Result: ReasonEdit在人类偏好对齐和公开基准泛化性上表现优异，能生成高质量可解释评估文本。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了更透明可信的图像编辑评估，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent text-guided image editing (TIE) models have achieved remarkable progress, however, many edited results still suffer from artifacts, unintended modifications, and suboptimal aesthetics. Although several benchmarks and evaluation methods have been proposed, most existing approaches rely on scalar scores and lack interpretability. This limitation largely stems from the absence of high-quality interpretation datasets for TIE and effective reward models to train interpretable evaluators. To address these challenges, we introduce ReasonEdit-22K, the first dataset that combines 22K edited images with 113K Chain-of-Thought (CoT) samples, along with 1.3M human judgments assessing these interpretations in terms of logicality, accuracy, and usefulness. Building upon this dataset, we propose RE-Reward, a multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based reward model designed to provide human-aligned feedback for evaluating interpretable reasoning in image editing. Furthermore, we develop ReasonEdit, which is trained using reward signals derived from RE-Reward and the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) algorithm to learn an interpretable evaluation model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReasonEdit achieves superior alignment with human preferences and exhibits strong generalization across public benchmarks. In addition, it is capable of generating high-quality interpretable evaluation text, enabling more transparent and trustworthy assessment for image editing. The code is available at https://github.com/IntMeGroup/ReasonEdit.

</details>


### [67] [AudioFace: Language-Assisted Speech-Driven Facial Animation with Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07478)
*Kai Zheng,Zejian Kang,Rui Mao,Hongyuan Zou,Yuanchen Fei,Xuanyang Xu,Xiangru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AudioFace是一个语言辅助的语音驱动面部表情生成框架，利用多模态大语言模型和文本/音素级线索，将口部相关面部系数预测视为结构化生成问题，在多个评估指标上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音驱动面部动画方法直接映射音频到面部系数，忽视了语音产生的语言学和音韵学结构，导致口部动作的准确性不足。

Method: 提出AudioFace框架，引入多模态大语言模型的先验知识，融合转录文本和音素级线索作为桥梁，将口部面部系数预测建模为语言和发音信息引导的结构化生成问题。

Result: 在多个评估指标上均达到优越性能，验证了语言辅助和多模态先验引导方法在语音驱动面部动画中的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言辅助和多模态先验引导的策略能有效提升语音驱动面部动画的性能，尤其在与发音相关的口部动作生成方面。

Abstract: Speech-driven facial animation requires accurate correspondence between acoustic signals and facial motion, especially for articulation-related mouth movements. However, directly mapping speech audio to facial coefficients often overlooks the linguistic and phonetic structure underlying speech production. In this paper, we propose AudioFace, a language-assisted framework for speech-driven blendshape generation that treats mouth-related facial coefficient prediction as a structured generation problem guided by linguistic and articulatory information. Instead of relying solely on acoustic features, our method leverages the prior knowledge of multimodal large language models and introduces transcript- and phoneme-level cues to bridge speech signals with interpretable facial actions. Extensive experiments show that AudioFace achieves superior performance across multiple evaluation metrics, validating the effectiveness of language-assisted and multimodal-prior-guided speech-driven facial animation.

</details>


### [68] [How Far Is Document Parsing from Solved? PureDocBench: A Source-TraceableBenchmark across Clean, Degraded, and Real-World Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07492)
*Zhiheng Li,Zongyang Ma,Jiaxian Chen,Jianing Zhang,Zhaolong Su,Yutong Zhang,Zhiyin Yu,Ruiqi Liu,Xiaolei Lv,Bo Li,Jun Gao,Ziqi Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Bing Li,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对OmniDocBench基准测试存在注释质量问题和污染风险（12.08%错误率），本文提出PureDocBench——一个可编程生成、可追溯源的文档解析基准，包含来自10个领域66个子类别的1,475页文档，每个页面有干净、数字退化、真实退化三个版本（共4,425张图像）。评估40个模型发现：最佳模型得分仅约74/100，差距达44.6分；≤4B参数的专业解析器可媲美大5-100倍的通用VLM，但公式识别是共同瓶颈（均未超过67%）；通用VLM在退化条件下得分下降更少，导致排名反转，使纯干净评估具有误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档解析基准OmniDocBench存在两个关键问题：(1) 通过三阶段审计流水线发现其21,353个评分块中有2,580个错误（12.08%），注释质量堪忧；(2) 公开超过一年，存在模型对基准数据过拟合的污染风险。这些问题导致基于OmniDocBench的排名不可靠。

Method: 提出PureDocBench，一种程序化生成的、源可追溯的基准测试：从HTML/CSS渲染文档图像，并从中产生可验证的注释，覆盖10个领域66个子类别1,475页，每页有干净、数字退化、真实退化三个版本（共4,425张图像）。评估40个模型，涵盖流水线专家、端到端专家和通用VLM三类。

Result: (i) 最佳模型得分仅约74/100，最弱与最强模型间差距44.6分；(ii) ≤4B参数的专业解析器可媲美大5-100倍的通用VLM，但公式识别是共同瓶颈（平均不超过67%）；(iii) 通用VLM在数字/真实退化下仅损失0.99/8.52总得分，而流水线专家损失4.90/14.21，导致排名反转。

Conclusion: 文档解析远未解决；纯干净环境下的评估对实际部署具有误导性；所有数据、代码和工件均已公开发布。

Abstract: The past year has seen over 20 open-source document parsing models, yet thefield still benchmarks almost exclusively on OmniDocBench, a 1,355-pagemanually annotated dataset whose top scores have saturated above 90%. Athree-stage audit pipeline we run on OmniDocBench screens its 21,353evaluator-scored blocks and confirms 2,580 errors (12.08%); combined with overa year of public availability, both annotation quality and contamination riskcall its rankings into question. To address these issues, we presentPureDocBench, a programmatically generated, source-traceable benchmark thatrenders document images from HTML/CSS and produces verifiable annotations fromthe same source, covering 10 domains, 66 subcategories, and 1,475 pages, eachin three versions: clean, digitally degraded, and real-degraded (4,425 imagestotal). Evaluating 40 models spanning pipeline specialists, end-to-endspecialists, and general-purpose VLMs, we find: (i) document parsing is farfrom solved: the best model scores only ~74 out of 100, with a 44.6-point gapbetween the strongest and weakest models; (ii) specialist parsers with <=4Bparameters rival or surpass general VLMs that are 5-100x larger, yet formularecognition remains a shared bottleneck where no model exceeds 67% whenaveraging the formula metric across all three tracks; (iii) general VLMs loseonly 0.99/8.52 Overall points under digital/real degradation versus 4.90/14.21for pipeline specialists, producing ranking reversals that make clean-onlyevaluation misleading for deployment. All data, code, and artifacts arepublicly released.

</details>


### [69] [DIMoE-Adapters: Dynamic Expert Evolution for Continual Learning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07494)
*Mengxin Qin,Xiang Zhang,Xi Wang,Kun Wei,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DIMoE-Adapters框架，通过动态增量混合专家适配器解决视觉语言模型在多领域任务增量学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境，利用自校准专家演化(SCEE)和原型引导专家选择(PGES)实现动态专家演化，在多个设置下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多领域任务增量学习中，大的领域偏移加剧了稳定性-可塑性困境，现有方法依赖固定架构和静态参数分配，限制了新领域适应并加剧了灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出DIMoE-Adapters框架，包含两个协作组件：SCEE通过专家优化动态构建和演化稀疏专家池提升可塑性并减少冗余；PGES基于SCEE形成的专家池控制专家利用，提升已见和未见任务的稳定性。

Result: 在多种设置下，DIMoE-Adapters均优于之前的SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 动态专家演化范式能有效平衡稳定性与可塑性，解决多领域任务增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Continual learning enables vision-language models to accumulate knowledge and adapt to evolving tasks without retraining from scratch. However, in multi-domain task-incremental learning, large domain shifts intensify the stability-plasticity dilemma. Most existing methods rely on fixed architectures with statically allocated parameters, which limits adaptation to new domains and aggravates catastrophic forgetting. To address these challenges, we propose DIMoE-Adapters, a Dynamic Incremental Mixture-of-Experts Adapters framework that introduces a dynamic expert evolution paradigm to balance stability and plasticity. This paradigm is implemented through two collaborative components: Self-Calibrated Expert Evolution (SCEE) and Prototype-Guided Expert Selection (PGES). SCEE constructs and evolves a sparse expert pool through expert optimization dynamics, improving plasticity while reducing redundant capacity. PGES controls expert utilization based on the pool shaped by SCEE, improving stability across both previously encountered and unseen tasks. Extensive experiments show that DIMoE-Adapters outperforms previous state-of-the-art methods across various settings.

</details>


### [70] [Cloud-top infrared observations reveal the four-dimensional precipitation structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07499)
*Tianchi Xu,Ziqiang Ma,Andrea Marinoni,Yuanpeng He,Xiaoqing Li,Chuanfeng Zhao,Kang He,Jintao Xu,Bohan Zhou,Wenbo Zhao,Haoshuang Chen,Tun Wang,Dongdong Wang,Yang Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出4DPrecipNet深度学习框架，证明静止轨道红外云顶观测能够通过物理约束（水汽优先约束）恢复降水的四维（垂直+时间）结构，突破了传统认为红外只能感知云顶属性的局限，为全球连续降水监测提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球尺度的四维降水信息对理解地球能量和水循环至关重要，但目前缺乏观测手段。传统理论认为静止轨道红外观测主要感知云顶属性，对云下降水敏感性有限，需要突破这一认知局限。

Method: 提出物理约束的深度学习框架4DPrecipNet：1）引入"水汽优先"约束，要求潜表征恢复可降水水汽量，确保热力学一致性；2）集成多通道红外辐射数据与雷达反演的降水廓线；3）从静止轨道数据重建降水系统的垂直和时间演化。

Result: 该框架能够捕捉深对流结构及其演化过程，在大样本和独立雷达对比中均表现出稳健性能，证明云下降水信息物理上编码在云顶红外观测中。

Conclusion: 证实了红外云顶观测中蕴含着此前未被利用的云下降水可观测性，建立了全球连续降水结构监测的新途径。

Abstract: Accurate four-dimensional (4D) precipitation information is essential for understanding the Earth's energy and water cycles, yet remains observationally unresolved at global scales. Conventional theory holds that geostationary infrared observations primarily sense cloud-top properties, with limited sensitivity to sub-cloud precipitation. Here we show that cloud-top infrared measurements nevertheless encode sufficient information to recover the four-dimensional structure of precipitation, revealing a previously unexploited observability of sub-cloud processes. We introduce a physically constrained deep learning framework, 4DPrecipNet, in which a moisture-first constraint requires the latent representation to recover precipitable water vapour, anchoring the model in thermodynamic consistency. By integrating multi-channel infrared radiances with these constraints and radar-derived precipitation profiles, we reconstruct the vertical and temporal evolution of precipitation systems from geostationary orbit. The framework captures deep convective structures and their evolution, with robust performance across large samples and independent radar comparisons. These results demonstrate that sub-cloud precipitation is physically encoded in cloud-top infrared observations, establishing a new pathway for continuous global monitoring of precipitation structure.

</details>


### [71] [Diffusion-APO: Trajectory-Aware Direct Preference Alignment for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07503)
*Jingyuan Zhu,Biaolong Chen,Le Zhang,Aixi Zhang,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Diffusion-APO算法，通过同步训练噪声与推理去噪路径来解决视频扩散模型与人类意图的对齐问题，并基于此构建了无需标量奖励的统一模块化RLHF框架，在视觉质量和指令遵循上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型对齐方法（如DPO、GRPO）受限于易有偏的奖励模型或次优的时间步采样，训练噪声分布与实际推理轨迹之间存在固有偏差，缺乏可扩展且轨迹感知的对齐路径。

Method: 提出Diffusion-APO（对齐偏好优化）算法，通过同步训练噪声与推理去噪路径最大化梯度信号效能；并构建统一的模块化RLHF框架，集成了在线排序、半在线锚定、离线精炼和蒸馏感知漂移校正四个模块，无需基于标量奖励的策略梯度。

Result: 在视觉质量和指令遵循上一致优于DPO、GRPO等标准基线，且在模型加速过程中有效保持生成保真度。

Conclusion: Diffusion-APO提供了一条鲁棒、端到端的可扩展视频扩散对齐路径，解决了训练-推理分布不匹配问题，并实现了灵活的多阶段偏好对齐。

Abstract: Efficiently aligning large-scale video diffusion models with human intent requires a scalable and trajectory-aware pathway that bridges the inherent discrepancy between training noise distributions and practical inference trajectories. While existing paradigms such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) attempt to address this, they are often hindered by either reliance on bias-prone, complex reward models or suboptimal timestep sampling. In this paper, we propose Diffusion-APO (Aligned Preference Optimization), a trajectory-aware algorithm that resolves this misalignment by synchronizing training noise with inference-time denoising paths to maximize gradient signal efficacy. To translate this algorithmic innovation into a practical solution, we introduce a unified and modular RLHF framework that integrates online ranking, half-online anchoring, offline refinement, and distillation-aware drift correction. This framework enables flexible, multi-stage preference alignment across diverse data and computational constraints without relying on scalar-reward-based policy gradients. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that Diffusion-APO consistently outperforms standard baselines in visual quality and instruction following, while effectively preserving generative fidelity during model acceleration, providing a robust, end-to-end pathway for scalable video diffusion alignment.

</details>


### [72] [InterLV-Search: Benchmarking Interleaved Multimodal Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07510)
*Bohan Hou,Jiuning Gu,Jiayan Guo,Ronghao Dang,Sicong Leng,Xin Li,Xuemeng Song,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InterLV-Search基准，用于评估交错式语言-视觉智能体搜索，包含三个难度级别和2061个样本，实验表明当前最佳模型总体准确率低于50%，揭示了视觉证据寻求、搜索控制和多模态证据整合方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准中视觉证据要么仅限于输入，要么被视为答案终点，而非交错搜索轨迹的一部分，缺乏对文本与视觉证据反复交织以指导后续搜索能力的评估。

Method: 构建InterLV-Search基准，包含2061个样本，分三个级别：主动视觉证据寻求、受控离线交错多模态搜索、开放网页交错多模态搜索；包含多模态多分支样本（涉及多实体比较）；Level 1/2使用自动化流水线，Level 3使用机器主导人工监督的开放网页流水线；提供InterLV-Agent实现标准化工具使用、轨迹记录和评估。

Result: 在专有和开源多模态智能体上的实验表明，当前系统远未解决交错多模态搜索问题，最佳模型总体准确率低于50%。

Conclusion: 当前多模态智能体在视觉证据寻求、搜索控制和多模态证据整合方面仍面临重大挑战，需要进一步发展以实现有效的交错多模态搜索。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for multimodal agentic search evaluate multimodal search and visual browsing, but visual evidence is either confined to the input or treated as an answer endpoint rather than part of an interleaved search trajectory. We introduce \textbf{InterLV-Search}, a benchmark for Interleaved Language-Vision Agentic Search, in which textual and visual evidence is repeatedly used to condition later search. It contains 2,061 examples across three levels: active visual evidence seeking, controlled offline interleaved multimodal search, and open-web interleaved multimodal search. Beyond existing benchmarks, it also includes multimodal multi-branch samples that involve comparison between multiple entities during the evidence search. We construct Level 1 and Level 2 with automated pipelines and Level 3 with a machine-led, human-supervised open-web pipeline. We further provide InterLV-Agent for standardized tool use, trajectory logging, and evaluation. Experiments on proprietary and open-source multimodal agents show that current systems remain far from solving interleaved multimodal search, with the best model below 50% overall accuracy, highlighting challenges in visual evidence seeking, search control, and multimodal evidence integration. We release the benchmark data and evaluation code at https://github.com/hbhalpha/InterLV-Search-Bench

</details>


### [73] [Hierarchical Dual-Subspace Decoupling for Continual Learning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07512)
*Mengxin Qin,Xiang Zhang,Kun Wei,Xu Yang,Cheng Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HDSD框架，通过层次化双子空间解耦方法（FMM模块分解参数空间为通用和任务特定子空间，GFM模块评估参数变化捕获稳定知识，HLM模块通过SVD和缩放机制约束子空间更新），有效缓解视觉-语言模型在类增量学习中的子空间干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类增量学习方法主要限制参数更新，但忽略了参数在高维空间中的结构特性。不同任务诱导的更新位于多个重叠的低秩子空间中，导致跨任务子空间干扰和严重遗忘。

Method: 提出HDSD框架，包含三个核心模块：(1) FMM将参数空间分解为通用和任务特定子空间；(2) GFM评估跨任务参数相对变化，用自适应阈值捕获稳定可迁移知识；(3) HLM通过SVD进行结构化参数分解，利用缩放机制约束不同子空间尺度的更新。

Result: 在传统基准上的大量实验表明，HDSD达到了最先进的结果。

Conclusion: HDSD通过层次化双子空间解耦降低了子空间干扰和参数漂移，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，在类增量学习任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning aims to continuously acquire new knowledge while preserving previously learned information, thereby mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Existing methods primarily restrict parameter updates but often overlook their structural properties in high-dimensional spaces. From a subspace perspective, updates induced by different tasks tend to lie in multiple overlapping low-rank subspaces, leading to cross-task subspace interference and severe forgetting. To address this issue, we propose HDSD, a Hierarchical Dual-Subspace Decoupling framework for continual learning in vision-language models. Specifically, we introduce a lightweight Feature Modulation Module (FMM) that explicitly decomposes the parameter space into general and task-specific subspaces. Building on this design, we develop two complementary components. First, a General Fusion Module (GFM) evaluates relative parameter changes across tasks and uses an adaptive threshold to capture stable and transferable knowledge.
