Based on this finding, we propose SphereVAD, a fully training-free, zero-shot VAD framework that recasts anomaly discrimination as von Mises-Fisher (vMF) likelihood-ratio geodesic inference on the unit hypersphere, unleashing latent discriminability through principled geometric reasoning rather than learning new representations. Specifically, SphereVAD first applies Frechet mean centering to unfold feature distributions and eliminate domain biases, then employs Holistic Scene Attention (HSA) to reinforce feature consistency using cross-video priors, and finally performs vMF-guided Spherical Geodesic Pulling (SGP) to align ambiguous segments with directional prototypes on the spherical manifold. This training-free pipeline requires only minimal synthetic images for calibration. SphereVAD establishes new state-of-the-art results among training-free approaches on three major benchmarks and remains competitive with fully supervised baselines. Code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [109] [TRAS: An Interactive Software for Tracing Tree Ring Cross Sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08025)
*Henry Marichal,Diego Passarella,Gregory Randall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRAS是一个开源图形软件，集成了三种树轮检测算法（传统图像处理+两种深度学习方法），支持自动检测与手动校正，在18张火炬松横截面图像上DeepCS-TRD方法F-score达81.0%，与CooRecorder的相关性r>0.99，减少了约80%的手动标注工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统树轮标注依赖人工，耗时、主观且难以规模化，亟需自动化工具提高效率与可重复性。

Method: 开发了开源图形化树轮分析套件TRAS，集成三种互补检测算法（经典图像处理方法CS-TRD和两种深度学习方法DeepCS-TRD、INBD），提供自动检测、手动校正、假阳性去除、缺失环添加功能，并计算早材/晚材面积、周长、等效宽度及自定义路径宽度等树木年代学指标。

Result: 在18张专家标注的火炬松横截面图像上，DeepCS-TRD的F-score为81.0%，精确率86.4%；自动检测将手动校正工作量减少至约20%的环边界；环宽测量与CooRecorder的相关系数r>0.99。

Conclusion: TRAS提供了一个灵活、可重复的跨平台树轮分析解决方案，代码开源，能有效减少人工标注工作并保持高精度。

Abstract: Tree ring marking remains a key step in dendrometry and dendrochronology, but it is often performed manually, making the process time-consuming, subjective, and difficult to scale to large image datasets.
  We present the Tree Ring Analyzer Suite (TRAS), an open-source graphical software for automatic delineation, manual correction, and measurement of tree rings in wood cross-sectional images. TRAS integrates three complementary detection algorithms: the classical image-processing method CS-TRD and two deep-learning approaches, DeepCS-TRD and INBD. The interface allows users to refine automatic detections, remove false positives, and manually add missing rings. It also computes dendrochronological metrics such as earlywood and latewood areas, ring perimeter, equivalent ring width, and custom path-based ring-width measurements.
  TRAS was evaluated on 18 expertly annotated Pinus taeda L. cross-section images. DeepCS-TRD achieved the best automatic detection performance, with an F-score of 81.0% and precision of 86.4%. Automatic detection reduced the required manual correction effort to approximately 20% of ring boundaries. For one-dimensional ring-width measurements, TRAS showed excellent agreement with CooRecorder ($r > 0.99$). Common detection errors, such as jump propagation or false positives near knots, were easily corrected through the postprocessing interface.
  TRAS provides a flexible and reproducible solution for tree-ring analysis on Windows, macOS, and Linux. Code is available at the https://hmarichal93.github.io/tras.

</details>


### [110] [STARFlow2: Bridging Language Models and Normalizing Flows for Unified Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08029)
*Ying Shen,Tianrong Chen,Yuan Gao,Yizhe Zhang,Yuyang Wang,Miguel Ángel Bautista,Shuangfei Zhai,Joshua M. Susskind,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: STARFlow2 提出基于自回归归一化流的统一多模态生成框架，通过在预训练 VLM 流与 TarFlow 流之间使用残差跳跃连接，结合深度-浅层流设计和统一 FAE 潜在空间，实现缓存友好的交错文本-图像生成，在图像生成和多模态理解基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态系统将自回归语言建模与扩散图像生成器组合，导致因果文本生成与迭代视觉去噪之间存在结构性不匹配。作者观察到自回归归一化流与 LLM 共享相同的因果掩码、KV-cache 机制和从左到右结构，因此是最自然的统一多模态生成范式。

Method: 基于 Pretzel 架构，将预训练 VLM 流与 TarFlow 流通过残差跳跃连接垂直交错，两者在相同因果掩码下运行。结合深度-浅层流设计和统一 FAE 潜在空间，使得文本和视觉输出可直接进入 KV-cache 而无需重新编码。

Result: 在图像生成和多模态理解基准测试上展现出强劲性能，验证了自回归流作为统一多模态建模可行基础的有效性。

Conclusion: 自回归归一化流是构建真正统一多模态生成模型的有力范式，解决了因果文本生成与视觉生成之间的结构不匹配问题。

Abstract: Deep generative models have advanced rapidly across text and vision, motivating unified multimodal systems that can understand, reason over, and generate interleaved text-image sequences. Most existing approaches combine autoregressive language modeling with diffusion-based image generators, inheriting a structural mismatch between causal text generation and iterative visual denoising. We observe that autoregressive normalizing flows are autoregressive Transformers--sharing the same causal mask, KV-cache mechanism, and left-to-right structure as LLMs--making them the most natural paradigm for true unified multimodal generation. We present STARFlow2, built on the Pretzel architecture that vertically interleaves a pretrained VLM stream with a TarFlow stream via residual skip connections, both operating under the same causal mask. Combined with a deep-shallow flow design and a unified FAE latent space, STARFlow2 enables cache-friendly interleaved generation where both text and visual outputs directly enter the KV-cache without re-encoding. Experiments demonstrate strong performance across image generation and multimodal understanding benchmarks, validating autoregressive flows as a viable foundation for unified multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [111] [PET-Adapter: Test-Time Domain Adaptation for Full and Limited-Angle PET Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08030)
*Rüveyda Yilmaz,Yuli Wu,Johannes Stegmaier,Volkmar Schulz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PET-Adapter，一种测试时域自适应框架，使仅在体模数据上预训练的生成式PET重建模型能适应不同临床数据集，在不需要配对真实数据的情况下，仅用2步扩散步数即可实现高质量3D重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PET图像重建受泊松噪声和物理退化因素影响，有限角度采集进一步加剧问题。深度学习方法泛化到未见临床数据分布时需大量重新训练，限制了临床实用性。

Method: 1) 层间低秩解剖条件化（layer-wise low-rank anatomical conditioning）；2) 基于有序子集期望最大化（OSEM）的热启动初始化，从物理信息重建出发，将扩散步数从50降至2；3) 测试时域自适应，无需配对真实数据。

Result: 在多个临床数据集上的全角度和有限角度设置下，3D重建性能均优于对比方法，显著降低计算开销同时保持高质量。

Conclusion: PET-Adapter展示了临床可行性和计算效率，解决了预训练模型泛化到临床分布的关键挑战，为领域自适应PET重建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Positron Emission Tomography (PET) image reconstruction is inherently challenged by Poisson noise and physical degradation factors, which are further exacerbated in limited-angle acquisitions. While deep learning methods demonstrate promising performance, their generalization to unseen clinical data distributions remains limited without extensive retraining. We propose PET-Adapter, a test-time domain adaptation framework for generative PET reconstruction models pretrained solely on phantom data. Our method enables adaptation to clinical datasets with varying anatomies, tracers, and scanner configurations without requiring paired ground truth. PET-Adapter introduces layer-wise low-rank anatomical conditioning during adaptation and Ordered Subset Expectation Maximization-based warm-starting that initializes the generation from physics-informed reconstructions, reducing diffusion steps from 50 to 2 without compromising quality. Experiments across multiple clinical datasets demonstrate superior 3D reconstruction performance in both full-angle and limited-angle settings, highlighting the clinical feasibility and computational efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [112] [Object Hallucination-Free Reinforcement Unlearning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08031)
*Kaidi Jia,Yujie Lin,Chengyi Yang,Jiayao Ma,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HFRU框架，通过强化学习在视觉编码器层面进行深度语义移除，在遗忘率和保留率上均超过98%，且几乎不引入对象幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM机器遗忘方法主要微调语言解码器，导致遗忘表面化，无法擦除底层视觉表征，且易引入对象幻觉。

Method: 两阶段框架：对齐破坏 + GRPO优化（含抽象奖励），在视觉编码器上操作实现深度语义移除。

Result: 在物体识别和人脸身份任务上，遗忘率和保留率均超98%，对象幻觉极低，显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: HFRU通过视觉编码器层面的强化遗忘框架，实现了更彻底的语义级知识移除，同时有效缓解了对象幻觉问题。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) raise growing concerns about privacy, copyright, and bias, motivating machine unlearning to remove sensitive knowledge. However, existing methods primarily fine-tune the language decoder, leading to superficial forgetting that fails to erase underlying visual representations and often introduces object hallucination. We propose HFRU, a reinforcement unlearning framework that operates on the vision encoder for deep semantic removal. Our two-stage approach combines alignment disruption with GRPO-based optimization using a composite reward, including an abstraction reward that encourages semantically valid substitutions and mitigates hallucinations. Experiments on object recognition and face identity tasks show that HFRU achieves over 98% forgetting and retention performance, while introducing negligible object hallucination, significantly outperforming prior methods.Our code and implementation details are available at https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/HFRU.

</details>


### [113] [SCOPE: Structured Decomposition and Conditional Skill Orchestration for Complex Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08043)
*Tianfei Ren,Zhipeng Yan,Yiming Zhao,Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Guohui Zhang,Hang Xu,Xiaoxiao Ma,Shiting Huang,Ke Xu,Wenxuan Huang,Lionel Z. Wang,Lin Chen,Zehui Chen,Jie Huang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过维护结构化规范来追踪语义承诺（semantic commitments），并条件式调用检索、推理和修复技能，解决文本到图像生成中复杂视觉意图的完整实现问题。同时提出Gen-Arena基准和EGIP评估指标，实验表明SCOPE在承诺级意图实现上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型在视觉保真度上进展显著，但在实现复杂视觉意图时，由于需求（语义承诺）在定位、生成和验证的整个生命周期中难以保持一致的可识别性（即概念断裂），导致忠实实现面临挑战。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架：维护演化的结构化规范来追踪语义承诺，并条件式调用检索、推理和修复技能来处理未解决或违反的承诺。同时构建Gen-Arena基准（含实体级和约束级规范的人工标注数据集）和EGIP评估指标（严格的实体优先通过准则）。

Result: SCOPE在Gen-Arena上达到0.60 EGIP，显著优于所有基线；在WISE-V上达0.907，在MindBench上达0.61，均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 持久性承诺追踪对于复杂图像生成中的意图实现是有效的，SCOPE框架通过维护承诺的完整生命周期一致性显著提升了复杂视觉意图的忠实实现能力。

Abstract: While text-to-image models have made strong progress in visual fidelity, faithfully realizing complex visual intents remains challenging because many requirements must be tracked across grounding, generation, and verification. We refer to these requirements as semantic commitments and formalize their lifecycle discontinuity as the Conceptual Rift, where commitments may be locally resolved or checked but fail to remain identifiable as the same operational units throughout the generation lifecycle. To address this, we propose SCOPE, a specification-guided skill orchestration framework that maintains semantic commitments in an evolving structured specification and conditionally invokes retrieval, reasoning, and repair skills around unresolved or violated commitments. To evaluate commitment-level intent realization, we introduce Gen-Arena, a human-annotated benchmark with entity- and constraint-level specifications, together with Entity-Gated Intent Pass Rate (EGIP), a strict entity-first pass criterion. SCOPE substantially outperforms all evaluated baselines on Gen-Arena, achieving 0.60 EGIP, and further achieves strong results on WISE-V (0.907) and MindBench (0.61), demonstrating the effectiveness of persistent commitment tracking for complex image generation.

</details>


### [114] [MoCoTalk: Multi-Conditional Diffusion with Adaptive Router for Controllable Talking Head Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08050)
*Xinyan Ye,Jiankang Deng,Abbas Edalat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoCoTalk是一个多条件视频扩散框架，通过自适应多条件路由器和口部增强着色网格实现可控说话头生成，在多个指标上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有说话头生成方法通常只处理身份、姿态、表情和口部动态中的一个子集，且多条件融合时依赖固定权重或启发式策略，导致异构条件间的干扰问题未被充分解决。

Method: 1) 提出自适应多条件路由器(AMCR)，实现通道级、时间步感知的条件门控融合；2) 设计口部增强着色网格(Mouth-Augmented Shading Mesh)，解耦头部运动、口部运动、表情和光照；3) 引入唇部一致性损失增强音视频对齐。

Result: 在结构、运动和感知指标的大多数评估上达到SOTA性能，同时提供单条件方法不具备的属性级可控性。

Conclusion: MoCoTalk通过自适应条件融合机制和几何先验解耦，有效解决了多条件说话头生成中的干扰问题，兼顾了生成质量与可控性。

Abstract: Talking-head generation requires joint modeling of identity, head pose, facial expression, and mouth dynamics. Existing methods typically address only a subset of these factors, and rely on fixed-weight or heuristic fusion when multiple conditions are involved. We present MoCoTalk, a multi-conditional video diffusion framework that unifies four complementary control signals: a reference image, facial keypoints, 3DMM-rendered shading meshes, and the corresponding speech audio. To resolve destructive interference among heterogeneous conditions, we introduce an Adaptive Multi-Condition Router that computes channel-wise, timestep-aware gating over the four condition streams, allowing the fusion strategy to vary with both feature subspace and noise level. To better capture speech-related facial dynamics, we design a Mouth-Augmented Shading Mesh, a 3DMM-based representation that decouples head motion, mouth motion, expression, and lighting. This design provides a temporally consistent geometric prior and allows flexible recombination of these attributes at inference. We further introduce a lip consistency loss to tighten audio-visual alignment. Extensive experiments show that MoCoTalk achieves state-of-the-art performance on the majority of structural, motion, and perceptual metrics, while offering attribute-level controllability that single-condition methods do not provide.

</details>


### [115] [Towards Highly-Constrained Human Motion Generation with Retrieval-Guided Diffusion Noise Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08054)
*Hanchao Liu,Fang-Lue Zhang,Shining Zhang,Tai-Jiang Mu,Shi-Min Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种检索引导的扩散噪声优化方法，通过关系任务解析识别困难约束并检索参考数据，结合奖励引导的掩码初始化噪声，在无需训练的条件下解决高度约束的人体运动生成任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理具有严峻时空约束（如严重空间障碍、指定步数）的高级定制任务时表现不佳，亟需一种能应对此类高度约束运动生成任务的方法。

Method: 基于免训练扩散噪声优化框架，引入检索引导机制。首先通过关系任务解析（利用LLM）对目标约束分组并识别困难约束；然后从大规模运动数据集中检索可满足困难约束的参考运动；通过奖励引导的掩码将随机噪声与检索噪声结合，获得更优的扩散噪声初始化；最后从该初始化出发进行噪声优化以生成满足高度约束的运动。

Result: 成功解决具有高度约束的生成任务，相比现有方法在严重空间障碍、指定步数等挑战性场景下表现更优。

Conclusion: 通过检索引导和关系任务解析，显著提升了运动生成模型在高度约束场景下的能力，无需额外训练，且LLM的引入增强了智能体自动推理能力。

Abstract: Generating human motion that satisfies customized zero-shot goal functions, enabling applications such as controllable character animation and behavior synthesis for virtual agents, is a critical capability. While current approaches handle many unseen constraints, they fail on tasks with very challenging spatiotemporal restrictions, such as severe spatial obstacles or specified numbers of walking steps. To equip motion generators for these highly constrained tasks, we present a retrieval-guided method built on the training-free diffusion noise optimization framework. The key idea is to search within large motion datasets for guidance that can potentially satisfy difficult constraints. We introduce relational task parsing to group target constraints and identify the difficult ones to be handled by retrieved reference. A better initialization for diffusion noise is then obtained via a reward-guided mask that combines random noise with retrieved noise. By optimizing diffusion noise from this improved initialization, we successfully solve highly constrained generation tasks. By leveraging LLM for relational task parsing, the whole framework is further enabled to automatically reason for what to retrieve, improving the intelligence of moving agents under a training-free optimization scheme.

</details>


### [116] [Flow-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08063)
*Zhen Fang,Wenxuan Huang,Yu Zeng,Yiming Zhao,Shuang Chen,Kaituo Feng,Yunlong Lin,Lin Chen,Zehui Chen,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flow-OPD 提出首个面向 Flow Matching 模型的两阶段后训练对齐框架，通过单奖励 GRPO 微调领域专家教师、基于流式冷启动的在线蒸馏以及流形锚点正则化，在 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 上将 GenEval 从 63 提升至 92、OCR 精度从 59 提升至 94，有效解决了多任务对齐中的奖励稀疏和梯度干扰问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Flow Matching 文生图模型在多任务对齐下存在两个关键瓶颈：标量奖励导致的奖励稀疏性，以及联合优化异构目标时产生的梯度干扰，这引发了竞争指标间的"跷跷板效应"和普遍的奖励黑客行为。

Method: 采用两阶段对齐策略：第一阶段通过单奖励 GRPO 微调培育领域专精教师模型；第二阶段通过基于流的冷启动方案建立稳健初始策略，再通过在线采样、任务路由标注和密集轨迹级监督的三步编排将异构专长整合到单一学生模型中。同时引入流形锚点正则化，利用任务无关教师提供全数据监督，将生成锚定在高保真流形上。

Result: 在 Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 上，GenEval 从 63 提升至 92，OCR 精度从 59 提升至 94，整体比原始 GRPO 提升约 10 个点，同时保持图像保真度和人类偏好对齐，并展现出"超越教师"的涌现效应。

Conclusion: Flow-OPD 构建了一种可扩展的对齐范式，用于打造通用型文生图模型，有效解决了多任务对齐中的核心难题。

Abstract: Existing Flow Matching (FM) text-to-image models suffer from two critical bottlenecks under multi-task alignment: the reward sparsity induced by scalar-valued rewards, and the gradient interference arising from jointly optimizing heterogeneous objectives, which together give rise to a 'seesaw effect' of competing metrics and pervasive reward hacking. Inspired by the success of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) in the large language model community, we propose Flow-OPD, the first unified post-training framework that integrates on-policy distillation into Flow Matching models. Flow-OPD adopts a two-stage alignment strategy: it first cultivates domain-specialized teacher models via single-reward GRPO fine-tuning, allowing each expert to reach its performance ceiling in isolation; it then establishes a robust initial policy through a Flow-based Cold-Start scheme and seamlessly consolidates heterogeneous expertise into a single student via a three-step orchestration of on-policy sampling, task-routing labeling, and dense trajectory-level supervision. We further introduce Manifold Anchor Regularization (MAR), which leverages a task-agnostic teacher to provide full-data supervision that anchors generation to a high-quality manifold, effectively mitigating the aesthetic degradation commonly observed in purely RL-driven alignment. Built upon Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, Flow-OPD raises the GenEval score from 63 to 92 and the OCR accuracy from 59 to 94, yielding an overall improvement of roughly 10 points over vanilla GRPO, while preserving image fidelity and human-preference alignment and exhibiting an emergent 'teacher-surpassing' effect. These results establish Flow-OPD as a scalable alignment paradigm for building generalist text-to-image models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Domain-level metacognitive monitoring in frontier LLMs: A 33-model atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06673)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 聚合元认知质量评分掩盖了MMLU基准测试中不同领域间的模型内差异。通过对33个前沿大语言模型进行47,151次观测，发现每个模型在领域层面的监控能力均存在显著差异：应用/专业知识最容易监控，形式推理和自然科学最难。领域分组仅是实用基准分类而非有效的潜在构念。部分模型家族内部轮廓聚类显著，而另一些则不然。Gemma 4相比Gemma 3有显著提升。研究结果支持在特定应用领域部署前进行基准阶段的领域筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示聚合元认知质量指标（如整体AUROC）如何掩盖模型在MMLU不同知识领域间监控能力的真实差异，从而验证基准领域层面筛选的必要性。

Method: 对33个前沿LLM（来自8个模型家族）施测1,500道MMLU题目（每个领域250题，共6个领域），采用口头化置信度（0-100）计算每个模型-领域单元格的Type-2 AUROC，总计47,151次观测。同时进行了受试者级一致性分析、轮廓聚类、Bootstrap置信区间和分半信度分析。

Result: 所有模型在领域层面均存在非平凡差异；应用/专业知识AUROC均值最高（.742），形式推理和自然科学最差（27/33模型排名垫底）；六领域分组的一致性比率为0.95，属实用分类而非潜在构念；Anthropic、Google-Gemini和Qwen家族内轮廓聚类显著，DeepSeek、Google-Gemma和OpenAI则不显著；Gemma 4 31B比Gemma 3 27B提升+.202；Bootstrap 95% CI中位宽度.199；聚合分半信度r=.893，轮廓级分半信度中位数r=.184。

Conclusion: 聚合指标掩盖了稳定的基准领域间变异，支持在特定应用领域部署前进行基准阶段的领域筛选。

Abstract: Aggregate metacognitive quality scores mask within-model variation across MMLU benchmark domains. We administered 1,500 MMLU items (250 per domain, under an a priori six-domain grouping) to 33 frontier LLMs from eight model families and computed Type-2 AUROC per model-domain cell using verbalized confidence (0-100). Total observations: 47,151. Every model with above-chance aggregate monitoring showed non-trivial domain-level variation. Applied/Professional knowledge was reliably the easiest benchmark domain to monitor (mean AUROC = .742, ranked top-2 in 21 of 33 models); Formal Reasoning and Natural Science were reliably the hardest (one of the two ranked bottom-2 in 27 of 33 models). The three middle domains were statistically indistinguishable (Kendall's W = .164). A subject-level coherence analysis (within-domain similarity ratio = 0.95) confirms the six-domain grouping is a pragmatic benchmark taxonomy, not a validated latent construct. Within-family profile-shape clustering is significant for Anthropic, Google-Gemini, and Qwen (permutation p < .0001) but not DeepSeek, Google-Gemma, or OpenAI. Gemma 4 31B showed a +.202 AUROC improvement over Gemma 3 27B. Three models classified Invalid on binary KEEP/WITHDRAW probes produced normal profiles under verbalized confidence, confirming probe-format specificity. Bootstrap 95% CIs on 198 cells have median width .199. Split-half aggregate stability r = .893; profile-level split-half is weaker (grand median r = .184). These results show stable benchmark-domain variation obscured by aggregate metrics, and support benchmark-stage domain screening as a step before deployment in specific application areas.

</details>


### [118] [VITA-QinYu: Expressive Spoken Language Model for Role-Playing and Singing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06765)
*Jiacheng Xu,Heting Gao,Liufei Xie,Zhenchuan Yang,Lijiang Li,Yiting Chen,Bin Zhang,Meng Chen,Chaoyu Fu,Weifeng Zhao,Wenjiang Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VITA-QinYu是首个支持角色扮演和歌唱生成的表现型端到端口语语言模型，采用混合语音-文本范式与多码本音频令牌，在保持自然对话能力的同时显著提升了表现力，在多项基准测试上达到最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类语音除了语言内容外还传递丰富的表现力信息（如个性、情绪、表演元素），现有口语模型主要聚焦自然对话，缺乏对角色扮演和歌唱等表现型语音生成的支持。

Method: 采用混合语音-文本范式，通过交错文本-音频建模与多码本音频令牌实现更丰富的副语言表征，同时保持模态间清晰分离以避免干扰；构建了包含15.8K小时自然对话、角色扮演和歌唱数据的综合数据生成流水线。

Result: 在客观角色扮演基准上领先同行SLM 7个百分点，歌唱MOS评分（5分制）超越0.13分；在C3和URO基准上对话准确率和流畅度分别领先1.38和4.98个百分点。

Conclusion: VITA-QinYu是首个将自然对话、角色扮演和歌唱能力统一于单一E2E SLM的模型，代码和模型已开源并提供支持流式全双工交互的易用演示。

Abstract: Human speech conveys expressiveness beyond linguistic content, including personality, mood, or performance elements, such as a comforting tone or humming a song, which we formalize as role-playing and singing. We present VITA-QinYu, the first expressive end-to-end (E2E) spoken language model (SLM) that goes beyond natural conversation to support both role-playing and singing generation. VITA-QinYu adopts a hybrid speech-text paradigm that extends interleaved text-audio modeling with multi-codebook audio tokens, a design enabling richer paralinguistic representation while preserving a clear separation between modalities to avoid interference. We further develop a comprehensive data generation pipeline to synthesize a total of 15.8K hours of natural conversation, role-playing, and singing data for training. VITA-QinYu demonstrates superior expressiveness, outperforming peer SLMs by 7 percentage points on objective role-playing benchmarks, and surpassing peer models by 0.13 points on a 5-point MOS scale for singing. Simultaneously, it achieves state-of-the-art conversational accuracy and fluency, exceeding prior SLMs by 1.38 and 4.98 percentage points on the C3 and URO benchmarks, respectively. We open-source our code and models and provide an easy-to-use demo with full-stack support for streaming and full-duplex interaction.

</details>


### [119] [IntentGrasp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Intent Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06832)
*Yuwei Yin,Chuyuan Li,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IntentGrasp基准测试集，用于评估大语言模型的意图理解能力，涵盖262,759个训练样本和12,909个测试样本（另有一个更具挑战性的470样本Gem集）。在20个LLM上的评估显示模型表现不佳（All Set低于60%，Gem Set低于25%），而人类表现约81.1%。作者提出Intentional Fine-Tuning (IFT)方法，在训练集上微调后取得了All Set上30+ F1点和Gem Set上20+ F1点的显著提升，并且跨领域泛化实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确理解语音、对话和文字背后的意图对于开发有用的大语言模型助手至关重要，而现有LLM在意图理解方面能力不足，缺乏专门的评估基准。

Method: 构建了IntentGrasp基准（从49个高质量开放许可语料库提取，覆盖12个领域，经源数据集整理、意图标签上下文化和任务格式统一），并在20个LLM上评估；提出Intentional Fine-Tuning (IFT)微调方法增强意图理解能力。

Result: 20个LLM在All Set上得分低于60%，在Gem Set上低于25%，其中17个模型低于随机基线（15.2%）；IFT微调后All Set提升30+ F1点，Gem Set提升20+ F1点；人类表现约81.1%。

Conclusion: LLM的意图理解能力仍有大幅提升空间，IFT是一种有效的增强方法，具有强跨领域泛化能力，为构建更有意图感、更强大且更安全的AI助手提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Accurately understanding the intent behind speech, conversation, and writing is crucial to the development of helpful Large Language Model (LLM) assistants. This paper introduces IntentGrasp, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the intent understanding capability of LLMs. Derived from 49 high-quality, open-licensed corpora spanning 12 diverse domains, IntentGrasp is constructed through source datasets curation, intent label contextualization, and task format unification. IntentGrasp contains a large-scale training set of 262,759 instances and two evaluation sets: an All Set of 12,909 test cases and a more balanced and challenging Gem Set of 470 cases. Extensive evaluations on 20 LLMs across 7 families (including frontier models such as GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-Pro, and Claude-Opus-4.7) demonstrate unsatisfactory performance, with scores below 60% on All Set and below 25% on Gem set. Notably, 17 out of 20 tested models perform worse than a random-guess baseline (15.2%) on Gem Set, while the estimated human performance is ~81.1%, showing substantial room for improvement. To enhance such ability, this paper proposes Intentional Fine-Tuning (IFT), which fine-tunes the models on the training set in IntentGrasp, yielding significant gains of 30+ F1 points on All Set and 20+ points on Gem Set. Tellingly, the leave-one-domain-out (Lodo) experiments further demonstrate the strong cross-domain generalizability of IFT, verifying that it is a promising approach to substantially enhancing the intent understanding of LLMs. Overall, by benchmarking and boosting intent understanding ability, this study sheds light on a promising path towards more intentional, capable, and safe AI assistants for human benefits and social good.

</details>


### [120] [TajPersLexon: A Tajik-Persian Lexical Resource and Hybrid Model for Cross-Script Low-Resource NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06886)
*Mullosharaf K. Arabov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TajPersLexon，一个包含40,112条塔吉克-波斯语词对的双语词汇资源，用于低资源场景下的跨文字词汇检索、音译和对齐。通过CPU基准测试比较了三种方法，神经模型和检索方法达到98-99%的top-1准确率，混合模型在准确率-效率权衡中表现优异，在OCR后校正任务中达96.4%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塔吉克语和波斯语虽亲缘关系近但使用不同文字系统，低资源场景下缺乏可用的词汇对齐资源，现有方法（如大型多语言句子变换器）在精确词汇匹配上表现不佳，需要构建专门的词汇资源并开发高效实用的对齐方法。

Method: 构建了40,112对塔吉克-波斯语并行词汇资源TajPersLexon；在纯CPU环境下对比三类方法：(i)轻量级混合流水线（可解释的混合模型）、(ii)神经序列到序列模型、(iii)检索方法；所有实验使用固定随机种子以确保可重复性。

Result: 神经模型和检索方法达到98-99%的top-1准确率；大型多语言句子变换器在该精确词汇匹配任务上失败；所提出的可解释混合模型在OCR后校正任务中达到96.4%准确率，提供了良好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 该词汇匹配任务本质上是可解的（solvable）；可解释混合模型在低资源实际应用中比大型神经模型更具实用价值，兼顾了准确率和效率；数据集、代码和模型将公开释放以促进后续研究。

Abstract: This work introduces TajPersLexon, a curated Tajik--Persian parallel lexical resource of 40,112 word and short-phrase pairs for cross-script lexical retrieval, transliteration, and alignment in low-resource settings. We conduct a comprehensive CPU-only benchmark comparing three methodological families: (i) a lightweight hybrid pipeline, (ii) neural sequence-to-sequence models, and (iii) retrieval methods. Our evaluation establishes that the task is essentially solvable, with neural and retrieval baselines achieving 98-99% top-1 accuracy. Crucially, we demonstrate that while large multilingual sentence transformers fail on this exact lexical matching, our interpretable hybrid model offers a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for practical applications, achieving 96.4% accuracy in an OCR post-correction task. All experiments use fixed random seeds for full reproducibility. The dataset, code, and models will be publicly released.

</details>


### [121] [MIST: Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Conversational Assistants for Smart Homes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06897)
*Maximillian Chen,Xuanming Zhang,Michael Peng,Zhou Yu,Alexandros Papangelis,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MIST是一个面向IoT设备的合成多轮语音驱动代码生成数据集，用于评估多模态大语言模型在物理世界约束下的混合主动语音交互能力。研究发现开源与闭源模型在该任务上存在显著差距，即使最先进的闭源模型也有较大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网设备在物理世界中的普及需要能够处理复杂用户体验的语音接口。现有LLM虽具备工具调用能力，但建模真实IoT设备涉及时空约束、语音输入、动态状态追踪和混合主动交互模式，这是一个困难且研究不足的挑战。

Method: 构建了MIST（多模态交互式语音工具调用数据集），这是一个合成生成的多轮、语音驱动的代码生成任务，运行在IoT设备上。同时提供了一个可扩展的数据生成框架，用于构建相关数据集。

Result: 开源与闭源多模态LLM在MIST上存在显著性能差距，即使是前沿的闭源模型也有相当大的改进空间。

Conclusion: MIST揭示了现有模型在物理世界约束下的混合主动语音助手任务中的不足，数据集和生成框架将促进相关研究方向的发展。

Abstract: The rise of Internet of Things (IoT) devices in the physical world necessitates voice-based interfaces capable of handling complex user experiences. While modern Large Language Models (LLMs) already demonstrate strong tool-usage capabilities, modeling real-world IoT devices presents a difficult, understudied challenge which combines modeling spatiotemporal constraints with speech inputs, dynamic state tracking, and mixed-initiative interaction patterns. We introduce MIST (the Multimodal Interactive Speech-based Tool-calling Dataset), a synthetic multi-turn, voice-driven code generation task that operates over IoT devices. We find that there is a significant gap between open- and closed-weight multimodal LLMs on MIST, and that even frontier closed-weight LLMs have substantial headroom. We release MIST and an extensible data generation framework to build related datasets in order to facilitate research on mixed-initiative voice assistants which reason about physical world constraints.

</details>


### [122] [Reflections and New Directions for Human-Centered Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06901)
*Caleb Ziems,Dora Zhao,Rose E. Wang,Matthew Jörke,Ahmad Rushdi,Advit Deepak,Sunny Yu,Anshika Agarwal,Harshvardhan Agarwal,Gabriela Aranguiz-Dias,Aditri Bhagirath,Justine Breuch,Huanxing Chen,Ruishi Chen,Sarah Chen,Haocheng Fan,William Fang,Cat Gonzales Fergesen,Daniel Frees,Tian Gao,Ziqing Huang,Vishal Jain,Yucheng Jiang,Kirill Kalinin,Su Doga Karaca,Arpandeep Khatua,Teland La,Isabelle Levent,Miranda Li,Xinling Li,Yongce Li,Angela Liu,Minsik Oh,Nathan J. Paek,Anthony Qin,Emily Redmond,Michael J. Ryan,Aadesh Salecha,Xiaoxian Shen,Pranava Singhal,Shashanka Subrahmanya,Mei Tan,Irawadee Thawornbut,Michelle Vinocour,Xiaoyue Wang,Zheng Wang,Henry Jin Weng,Pawan Wirawarn,Shirley Wu,Sophie Wu,Yichen Xie,Patrick Ye,Sean Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Cathy Zhou,Yiling Zhao,James Landay,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个以人为中心的大型语言模型（HCLLM）开发框架，整合了NLP、HCI和负责任AI的视角，主张在模型开发的每个阶段（从系统设计、数据来源、模型训练、评估到负责任部署）都需严格关注人类的关切、偏好、价值观和目标，并通过案例研究探讨了未来工作场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在多个领域产生广泛影响，现有模型开发过于侧重技术能力而忽视人类优先事项。作者旨在建立一个兼顾伦理、经济和技术目标的以人为本的开发框架，确保人类关切贯穿模型开发全流程而非仅在后期粗浅处理。

Method: 采用跨学科框架整合方法，融合NLP、HCI和负责任AI三个领域的视角，系统性地分析LLM开发生命周期的各个阶段（设计、数据、训练、评估、部署），并为每个阶段提出具体的人本化建议和最佳实践。

Result: 提出了HCLLM框架，涵盖从系统设计到负责任部署的全流程人本化指导原则；并通过未来工作场景的案例研究展示了该框架的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 强调模型开发者必须在每个开发阶段都严谨地考虑人类关切，而非仅在事后训练阶段草草处理。HCLLM框架为构建更具社会责任感、更符合人类价值观的语言模型提供了系统性指导。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping the private and professional lives of users, with numerous applications in business, education, finance, healthcare, law, and science. With this rise in global influence comes greater urgency to build, evaluate, and deploy these systems in a manner that prioritizes not only technical capabilities but also human priorities. This work presents a framework for developing Human-Centered Large Language Models (HCLLMs), which integrates perspectives from Natural Language Processing (NLP), Human-Computer Interaction (HCI), and responsible AI. Considering the ethics, economics, and technical objectives of language modeling, we argue that model developers need to address human concerns, preferences, values, and goals, not only during a cursory post-training stage, but rather with rigor and care at every stage of the pipeline. This paper offers human-centered insights and recommendations for developers at each stage, from system design to data sourcing, model training, evaluation, and responsible deployment. Then we conclude with a case study, applying these insights to understand the future of work with HCLLMs.

</details>


### [123] [Can LLMs Take Retrieved Information with a Grain of Salt?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06919)
*Behzad Shayegh,Mohamed Osama Ahmed,Fred Tung,Leo Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在检索增强场景中难以根据检索信息的确定性程度适当调整回答。本文评估了8个LLM在上下文确定性服从性上的表现，发现系统性问题，并提出结合提示策略的交互方法，平均减少25%的服从错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究缺乏对LLM能否根据检索信息的确定性程度适当调整回答的评估，这在医疗、金融等高风险领域具有重要现实影响。

Method: 评估8个LLM在上下文确定性服从性上的表现；提出结合先验提醒、确定性重新校准和上下文简化的交互策略。

Result: LLM存在系统性局限：在观察到不确定上下文后难以回忆先验知识、误解表达出的确定性、过度信任复杂上下文。所提策略在不修改模型权重的情况下平均减少25%的服从错误。

Conclusion: 交互设计可以有效提升LLM的可靠性，所提出的评估指标和策略具有通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Large language models have demonstrated impressive retrieval-augmented capabilities. However, a crucial area remains underexplored: their ability to appropriately adapt responses to the certainty of the retrieved information. It is a limitation with real consequences in high-stakes domains like medicine and finance. We evaluate eight LLMs on their context-certainty obedience, measuring how well they adjust responses to match expressed context certainty. Our analysis reveals systematic limitations: LLMs struggle to recall prior knowledge after observing an uncertain context, misinterpret expressed certainties, and overtrust complex contexts. To address these, we propose an interaction strategy combining prior reminders, certainty recalibration, and context simplification. This approach reduces obedience errors by 25% on average, without modifying model weights, demonstrating the efficacy of interaction design in enhancing LLM reliability. Our contributions include a principled evaluation metric, empirical insights into LLMs' uncertainty handling, and a portable strategy to improve context-certainty obedience across diverse LLMs.

</details>


### [124] [MultiSoc-4D: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Instruction-Induced Label Collapse in Closed-Set LLM Annotation of Bengali Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06940)
*Souvik Pramanik,S. M. Riaz Rahman Antu,Shak Mohammad Abyad,Md. Ibrahim Khalil,Md. Shahriar Hussain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiSoc-4D基准数据集（58K+孟加拉语社交媒体评论，涵盖情感、仇恨言论、讽刺等四维度标注），通过ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Grok等LLM系统性标注实验，发现"指令诱导标签坍缩"现象——LLM偏向回退标签（Other/Neutral/No），导致高一致率但少数类漏检（如79%仇恨和75%讽刺内容未被检测），且呈现"标签一致幻觉"（讽刺检测Fleiss' Kappa≈-0.001）。该偏见在40+模型中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在低资源语言的封闭式指令下进行自动化标注的行为尚未被充分研究，现有工作缺乏针对孟加拉语社交媒体多维度标注的系统性偏见诊断。

Method: 构建MultiSoc-4D（58K+条评论，6个来源，4维度标注），设计结构化流水线：ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude、Grok分别标注独立分区，共享20%验证集，与人工校准参考对比分析。

Result: 发现"指令诱导标签坍缩"现象：LLM系统性偏好回退标签（Other/Neutral/No），导致79%仇恨内容和75%讽刺内容漏检；讽刺检测Fleiss' Kappa≈-0.001，证实"标签一致幻觉"；该偏见在40+模型中普遍存在且不受架构差异影响。

Conclusion: MultiSoc-4D可作为孟加拉语NLP中标注偏见的诊断基准，揭示LLM在低资源语言标注中存在的系统性偏见问题。

Abstract: Annotation automation via Large Language Models (LLMs) is the core approach for scaling NLP datasets; however, LLM behavior with respect to closed-set instructions in low-resource languages has not been well studied. We present MultiSoc-4D, a Bengali social media dataset benchmark, which contains 58K+ social media comments from six sources annotated along four dimensions: category, sentiment, hate speech, and sarcasm. By employing a structured pipeline where ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, and Grok individually annotate separate partitions, while sharing a common validation set of 20%, we diagnose LLM behavior systematically. We discover a prevalent phenomenon called "instruction-induced label collapse", wherein LLMs show a systematic preference towards fallback labels (Other, Neutral, No), leading to high agreement rates but under-detection of minority categories. For example, we find that LLMs failed to detect 79% and 75% of instances with hateful and sarcastic content compared to a human-calibrated reference. Furthermore, we prove that it represents a "label agreement illusion", statistically validated via almost null Fleiss' Kappa ($κ\approx -0.001$) on sarcasm detection. Across 40+ LLMs, we benchmark this annotation bias propagation within the training pipeline, regardless of architectural differences. We release MultiSoc-4D as a diagnostic benchmark for annotation biases in Bengali NLP.

</details>


### [125] [Group of Skills: Group-Structured Skill Retrieval for Agent Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06978)
*Kun Zeng,Yu Huo,Siyu Zhang,Zi Ye,Yuecheng Zhuo,Haoyue Liu,Yuquan Lu,Junhao Wen,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GoSkills是一种推理时基于组结构检索的方法，它将技能检索从扁平列表转变为带有角色标签的紧凑执行上下文（包含Start、Support、Check、Avoid字段），在不改变下游智能体或技能库的前提下，显著提升了技能覆盖率和任务奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能增强智能体依赖大规模可复用技能库，但检索到的技能缺乏角色标注和上下文结构，智能体无法明确执行入口、支撑技能、可见前提条件和故障规避指引，导致执行效率低下。

Method: GoSkills方法包括四个步骤：1) 从类型化技能图中构建以锚点为中心的技能组；2) 通过组图扩展支持组；3) 将选中的组计划瓶颈压缩为有限原子技能负载集合；4) 渲染固定的执行契约，包含Start（执行入口）、Support（支撑技能）、Check（可见前提条件）、Avoid（故障规避指引）四个字段。

Result: 在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上的实验表明，GoSkills在小技能预算下保持了可见前提条件的覆盖率，优于扁平技能访问基线，并在奖励和智能体实际运行时间方面优于结构化检索参照方法。

Conclusion: GoSkills通过组结构检索和角色标记的执行契约，有效解决了技能检索中上下文缺失的问题，在不改变下游系统和技能库的前提下提升了智能体性能，验证了结构化技能组织对推理和执行效率的重要性。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents increasingly rely on large reusable skill libraries, but retrieving relevant skills is not the same as presenting usable context. Existing methods typically return atomic skills or dependency-aware bundles whose internal roles remain implicit, leaving the agent to infer the execution entry point, support skills, visible requirements, and failure-avoidance guidance. We introduce Group of Skills (GoSkills), an inference-time group-structured retrieval method that changes the agent-facing retrieval object from a flat skill list to a compact, role-labeled execution context. GoSkills builds anchor-centered skill groups from a typed skill graph, expands support groups through a group graph, bottlenecks the selected group plan into a bounded set of atomic skill payloads, and renders a fixed execution contract with Start, Support, Check, and Avoid fields, without changing the downstream agent, skill payloads, or execution environment. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld show that GoSkills preserves visible-requirement coverage under a small skill budget, improves over flat skill-access baselines, and often improves reward and agent-only runtime relative to structural retrieval references.

</details>


### [126] [Towards Closing the Autoregressive Gap in Language Modeling via Entropy-Gated Continuous Bitstream Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07013)
*Georgios Batzolis,Mark Girolami,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将文本建模为固定宽度二进制比特流上的连续扩散过程，利用匹配滤波残差参数化和基于熵率门控的Langevin型随机采样器，在LM1B和OpenWebText基准上显著缩小了扩散语言模型与自回归模型之间的质量差距，同时消除了标准DLM中O(V)的词汇表缩放瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在标准基准上长期落后于自回归模型，尽管最近的连续流和扩散方法缩小了这一差距，但仍需进一步改进以匹配自回归模型的样本质量和多样性。

Method: 将语义token表示为模拟比特序列，采用匹配滤波残差参数化以隔离上下文学习与独立比特后验；使用基于熵率门控的Langevin型校正随机采样器，自动在高信息区域集中随机性，其他区域保持近乎确定性；通过语义位补丁预测O(log V)逐位logits，消除O(V)词汇表缩放瓶颈。

Result: 在LM1B上，130M参数模型在匹配真实数据熵(4.31)时达到GenPPL=59.76（256 NFE），优于先前DLM基线并达到自回归参考水平；在OWT上，随机采样器建立了新的连续DLM帕累托前沿，GenPPL=27.06（熵5.26），使用比先前1024-NFE基线少4倍的步数。

Conclusion: 比特流扩散方法有效缩小了DLM与自回归模型之间的差距，消除了词汇表缩放瓶颈，为词汇表规模增长时的语言生成提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) promise parallel, order-agnostic generation, but on standard benchmarks they have historically lagged behind autoregressive models in sample quality and diversity. Recent continuous flow and diffusion approaches over token embeddings have narrowed this gap, suggesting continuous state spaces are highly effective for language. In this work, we further close the autoregressive gap by modeling text as a continuous diffusion process over fixed-width binary bitstreams. Our approach represents semantic tokens as analog bit sequences and utilizes a matched-filter residual parameterization to isolate contextual learning from analytic independent-bit posteriors. Crucially, we adopt a stochastic sampler that applies Langevin-type corrections gated by the entropy-rate profile, automatically concentrating stochasticity in high-information regions while remaining nearly deterministic elsewhere. On the One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B), our 130M-parameter bitstream model reaches a generative perplexity ($\GenPPL$) of $59.76$ at matched real-data entropy ($4.31$) using 256 neural function evaluations (NFEs), decisively outperforming prior DLM baselines and reaching the autoregressive reference. On OpenWebText (OWT), our stochastic sampler establishes a new continuous-DLM Pareto frontier, achieving $\GenPPL=27.06$ at an entropy of $5.26$ using $4\times$ fewer steps than previous 1024-NFE baselines. As an additional architectural benefit, bitstream diffusion removes the $\mathcal{O}(V)$ vocabulary scaling bottleneck shared by standard DLMs. By predicting $\mathcal{O}(\log V)$ bitwise logits via semantic bit-patching, our model yields a reduced memory footprint and higher throughput, demonstrating a scalable paradigm for language generation as vocabulary sizes grow.

</details>


### [127] [Cognitive Agent Compilation for Explicit Problem Solver Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07040)
*Hyeongdon Moon,Carolyn Rosé,John Stamper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出认知智能体编译框架(CAC)，利用大语言模型将问题解决知识编译为显式目标智能体，提升教育场景中AI系统的可检查性和可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在教育和辅导场景中应用广泛，但其预训练产生的知识边界模糊、难以约束，无法满足教育系统对可检查、可编辑的知识状态的明确需求。

Method: 受认知架构启发，CAC将知识表示、问题解决策略、验证与更新规则三个组件分离，利用强教师LLM将问题解决知识编译为显式的目标智能体。

Result: 基于小语言模型实现了早期概念验证，揭示了显式控制与可扩展泛化之间的关键设计权衡。

Conclusion: CAC是迈向教育应用中边界知识AI的初步步骤，为可检查、可编辑的智能辅导系统提供了新框架方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for tutoring, feedback generation, and content creation, but their broad pretraining makes them hard to constrain and poor substitutes for controllable learners. Educational systems often require inspectable and editable knowledge states: educators want to know what a system assumes the learner knows, and learners benefit when the system can justify actions in terms of explicit skills, misconceptions, and strategies. Inspired by cognitive architectures, we propose Cognitive Agent Compilation (CAC), a framework that uses a strong teacher LLM to compile problem-solving knowledge into an explicit target agent. CAC separates (i) knowledge representation, (ii) problem-solving policy, and (iii) verification and update rules, with the goal of making bounded problem solving more inspectable and editable in educational settings. We present an early proof of concept implemented with Small Language Models that surfaces key design trade-offs, particularly between explicit control and scalable generalization, and positions CAC as an initial step toward bounded-knowledge AI for educational applications.

</details>


### [128] [WiCER: Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine Iterative Knowledge Compilation for LLM Wiki Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07068)
*Juan M. Huerta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出WiCER算法，通过迭代式编译-评估-精炼流程解决LLM Wiki模式中的知识编译缺口问题，在保持亚秒级延迟的同时显著降低灾难性事实丢失率（减少55%），经17个领域6800个问题验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Wiki模式通过KV缓存推理实现亚秒级上下文访问，但存在编译缺口：原始文档编译为wiki时关键事实被灾难性丢弃，导致盲编译的失败率高达53-60%。

Method: WiCER算法受CEGAR启发，迭代执行三个步骤：编译wiki、用诊断探针评估知识保留情况、识别丢弃事实并强制在后续编译中保留，直至编译质量达标。

Result: 1-2次迭代恢复80%丢失质量（平均3.24 vs 原始全上下文3.47），灾难性故障减少55%；消融实验证实定向诊断贡献+0.95分，远超通用固定策略的+0.16分。

Conclusion: WiCER有效解决了LLM Wiki的编译缺口问题，为大规模知识注入提供可行方案；全上下文KV缓存在策展知识上优于RAG（4.38 vs 4.08，TTFT快7.3倍），但注意力稀释导致大规模时性能下降。

Abstract: The LLM Wiki pattern, to compile and provide domain knowledge into a persistent artifact and serve it to LLMs via KV cache inference, promises context access at sub-second latency with zero retrieval failure. Realizing this requires solving the compilation gap: LLM compilation distilling raw documents into a wiki without catastrophically discarding critical facts. We characterize this gap across 17 RepLiQA domains (6,800 questions): we observe that full context KV cache inference outperforms RAG on curated knowledge (4.38 vs. 4.08 out of 5, 7.3 faster TTFT) but degrades below RAG at scale due to attention dilution, and blind compilation fails entirely (2.14 to 2.32 vs. 3.46, 53 to 60% catastrophic failure rate). To address the compilation gap, we propose WiCER (Wiki-memory Compile, Evaluate, Refine), an iterative algorithm inspired by counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) that closes this gap. WiCER evaluates compiled wikis against diagnostic probes, identifies dropped facts, and forces their preservation in subsequent compilations. One to two iterations recover 80% of lost quality (mean 3.24 vs. 3.47 for raw full-context across the 15 topics with baselines), reducing catastrophic failures by 55% relative. An ablation across all 17 topics confirms that targeted diagnosis (+0.95), not generic pinning (+0.16), drives the gains. All code and benchmarks are released for reproducible research.

</details>


### [129] [Self-Consolidating Language Models: Continual Knowledge Incorporation from Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07076)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Zihan Dong,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自巩固语言模型(SCoL)，一种后训练框架，让LLM在持续上下文流中自行生成文本更新指令，选择特定Transformer层进行参数更新，通过元强化学习训练，实现知识获取与保留的平衡，并在多个基准上优于提示、摘要、批量测试时训练和顺序微调等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长上下文流（如段落流、对话、长上下文工作流）时，虽然上下文窗口变长能暴露更多信息，但并不能保证有用信息被有效保存和复用。现有方法难以解决持续学习中的知识整合与灾难性干扰之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出Self-Consolidating Language Models (SCoL)后训练框架：给定当前上下文，LLM学习生成文本更新指令，指定要更新自身的哪些Transformer层；由于提交的更新会改变后续生成选择的模型，采用元强化学习在演化的模型状态上进行训练；在SQuAD知识融入任务上使用监督QA奖励，在LongBench v2长上下文巩固任务上使用基于似然的内在奖励。

Result: 在知识获取和保留两方面均优于提示、摘要、批量测试时训练和顺序微调等基线方法；分析表明SCoL鼓励生成稀疏更新位置，且这些位置与高Fisher信息层对齐，模型学会了将可塑性导向损失敏感区域同时限制干扰；SCoL能够从较短的元训练流迁移到评估时更长的LongBench v2流，支持可扩展的流式巩固。

Conclusion: SCoL框架通过元强化学习训练LLM自主选择要更新的层，实现了有效的持续上下文巩固，并且具备良好的可扩展性和迁移能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly receive information as streams of passages, conversations, and long-context workflows. While longer context windows expose more evidence, they do not ensure that useful information is preserved and reused. We study continual context consolidation: writing current context into model weights while limiting interference with previously consolidated information. We propose \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{Co}nsolidating \textbf{L}anguage Models (SCoL), a post-training framework in which, given current context, an LLM learns to generate textual update instructions specifying which of its own Transformer layers should be updated. Because committed updates change the model that later generates future selections, we train SCoL with meta-reinforcement learning over an evolving model state. We instantiate SCoL with supervised QA rewards on SQuAD knowledge incorporation and intrinsic likelihood-based rewards for LongBench v2 long-context consolidation. Across both settings, SCoL improves acquisition and retention over prompting, summarization, batch test-time training, and sequential finetuning baselines. Analysis of learned selection patterns shows that SCoL encourages the LLM to generate sparse update locations that align with layers of high Fisher information, suggesting that the model learns to route plasticity toward loss-sensitive regions while limiting interference. Moreover, SCoL transfers from shorter meta-training streams to longer LongBench v2 streams at evaluation, suggesting that our framework supports scalable streaming consolidation.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Single Ground Truth: Reference Monism as Epistemic Injustice in ASR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07084)
*Anna Seo Gyeong Choi,Maria Teleki,James Caverlee,Miguel del Rio,Corey Miller,Hoon Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文批判了语音识别评估中"参考转录单一主义"的弊端，指出采用单一转录规范作为ground truth会对失语症患者造成认知不公，并提出WER-Range作为替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR评估默认将某一转录规范（如准确转录、非准确转录、法律转录）视为唯一的ground truth，但这种"参考单一主义"忽视了不同规范编码的不同规范性假设。失语症患者的言语特征（如临床意义的言语不流畅）在"干净"的参考转录中被视为错误，导致系统性不公正。

Method: 1. 提出哲学框架"诠释学鸿沟"（hermeneutical gap）来解释参考单一主义如何造成认知不公；2. 形式化定义"认知不公正距离"（Epistemic Injustice Distance, EID）来量化参考单一主义的代价；3. 使用AphasiaBank数据集进行实证，展示不同转录规范下WER的显著差异；4. 提出WER-Range方法，报告在多种合法规范下的性能范围而非单一值。

Result: 实证表明，同一ASR系统在失语症语音上的WER随转录规范的不同而大幅变化，基于"干净"参考的评估会系统性惩罚临床上有意义的言语特征。EID量化了这种不公正的程度。

Conclusion: ASR评估不应固守单一转录规范，而应采纳WER-Range等多规范评估方法，以包容不同说话群体的语言特征，避免认知不公正。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) evaluation compares system output to ground truth transcripts, with Word Error Rate (WER) quantifying the distance between them. But ground truth transcripts are not discovered - they are produced by human annotators following conventions that encode normative assumptions about which speech features matter. Different conventions (verbatim, non-verbatim, legal) produce different transcripts of identical speech and judge the same ASR output differently. This paper argues that reference monism - enforcing a single transcription convention as ground truth - commits epistemic injustice. Speakers with aphasia, whose speech includes clinically meaningful disfluencies, are systematically disadvantaged when evaluated against "clean" references that treat those disfluencies as errors. The harm is not merely differential performance, but that evaluative infrastructure lacks interpretive resources to recognize their contributions as legitimate. We develop a philosophical framework introducing the hermeneutical gap, formalize Epistemic Injustice Distance (EID) to measure reference monism's cost, and demonstrate empirically using AphasiaBank that WER varies depending on which convention defines ground truth. We propose WER-Range: reporting performance across legitimate conventions rather than assuming a single correct answer.

</details>


### [131] [SAGE: Hierarchical LLM-Based Literary Evaluation through Ontology-Grounded Interpretive Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07102)
*Tianyu Wang,Nianjun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAGE层次化评估框架，通过大语言模型多轮迭代反思与独立验证，在文化、情感心理、存在哲学三个层面评估文学质量，在600次评估中实现98.8%得分收敛和94%以上评分者一致性，并揭示出经典文学>通俗文学>大模型生成文本的稳定层级差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学质量的评估需要考察文化表征、情感深度和哲学思辨等难以直接计算的解释性维度，现有计算方法难以处理这些开放的、主观的审美判断，因此需要一种理论驱动的、可扩展的自动化评估框架。

Method: 提出SAGE框架，将文学质量分解为本体论锚定的解释性维度，通过结构化的大语言模型评估（含多轮迭代反思和独立验证）进行评测。在100篇短篇小说（50篇经典、30篇通俗、20篇大模型生成）上，采用双模式评估（基于内容和基于元数据）在三个分析层面进行验证。

Result: 600次评估中达到98.8%得分收敛，评分者间一致性>94%，内容/元数据模式近乎完美无差异。统计显示稳定的体裁层级（经典>通俗>大模型，p<0.001），文化批判与哲学深度效应量极大（Cohen's d>2.4），情感表征差距较小（d=1.68）。跨层相关性r=0.649-0.683，证实三个维度捕获了可区分的质量方面。

Conclusion: 理论驱动的大语言模型评估可以达到测量级可靠性，支持系统识别当前生成模型在文学创作中落后于人类的具体维度，为开放式文本生成的可扩展自动化评估提供了直接路径。

Abstract: Evaluating literary quality requires assessing interpretive dimensions such as cultural representation, emotional depth, and philosophical sophistication that resist straightforward computational measurement. We introduce SAGE, a hierarchical evaluation framework that decomposes literary quality into ontology-grounded interpretive dimensions assessed through structured large language model evaluation with multi-round iterative reflection and independent validation. We validate the framework on 100 short stories (50 canonical works, 30 pulp fiction, 20 LLM-generated narratives) across three analytical layers (cultural, emotional-psychological, existential-philosophical) using dual-mode assessment. Across 600 evaluations, the framework achieves 98.8% score convergence and greater than 94% inter-rater agreement, with near-perfect mode invariance between content-based and metadata-based evaluation. Statistical analysis reveals a consistent genre hierarchy (Canonical > Pulp > LLM, all p<0.001) with layer-specific discrimination: cultural critique and philosophical depth exhibit very large effect sizes (Cohen's d>2.4), while emotional representation shows smaller gaps (d=1.68), suggesting that affective patterns are more learnable from training data than critical stance or philosophical depth. Cross-layer correlations (r=0.649-0.683) confirm the three dimensions capture empirically distinguishable quality facets. These findings demonstrate that theory-driven LLM evaluation can achieve measurement-grade reliability and support systematic identification of where current generative models fall short of human literary production, with direct implications for scalable automated evaluation of open-ended text generation.

</details>


### [132] [Retrieve, Integrate, and Synthesize: Spatial-Semantic Grounded Latent Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07106)
*Jin Cui,Xinyue Long,Xunyong Zhang,Yadong Zhang,Chuanchang Su,Jingye Gan,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RIS框架，通过空间-语义锚定的潜在推理，解决多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉推理中潜在轨迹与预训练计算回路不兼容的问题，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM将视觉证据压缩为离散文本思维，造成细粒度感知的信息瓶颈；而潜在视觉推理方法存在流形不兼容问题：潜在轨迹偏离预训练推理回路、退化为实例无关模式、且在答案生成阶段常被绕过。

Method: 1) 构建带边界框和区域语义描述的分步接地推理数据集；2) RIS框架将潜在token锚定到空间和语义证据，通过渐进式注意力瓶颈强制执行其因果角色，并引入短语言过渡token将合成潜在状态桥接到词汇对齐的解码。

Result: 在V*, HRBench4K, HRBench8K, MMVP, BLINK基准上，RIS一致优于开源/闭源模型及潜在推理基线；学习到的潜在轨迹具有多样性、可解释性和渐进集成特性。

Conclusion: RIS提供了一种在MLLM中实现忠实内部视觉推理的实用路径，通过空间-语义锚定解决了潜在推理的流形兼容问题。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made remarkable progress on vision-language reasoning, yet most methods still compress visual evidence into discrete textual thoughts, creating an information bottleneck for fine-grained perception. Recent latent visual reasoning methods attempt to reason in continuous hidden states, but we find that they suffer from insufficient manifold compatibility: latent trajectories drift away from pretrained reasoning circuits, collapse into instance-agnostic patterns, and are often bypassed during answer generation. To address these issues, we propose RIS (Retrieve, Integrate, and Synthesize), a spatial-semantic grounded framework that develops latent reasoning as a compatible extension of pretrained MLLM computation. We first construct a step-wise grounded reasoning dataset with bounding boxes and region-specific semantic descriptions. Built on this supervision, RIS anchors latent tokens to both spatial and semantic evidence, enforces their causal role through a progressive attention bottleneck, and introduces short language transition tokens to bridge synthesized latent states back to vocabulary-aligned decoding. Experiments on V*, HRBench4K, HRBench8K, MMVP, and BLINK show consistent improvements over closed/open-source and latent reasoning baselines. Further analyses demonstrate that RIS learns diverse, interpretable, and progressively integrated latent trajectories, offering a practical path toward faithful internal visual reasoning in MLLMs.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond LoRA vs. Full Fine-Tuning: Gradient-Guided Optimizer Routing for LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07111)
*Haozhan Tang,Xiuqi Zhu,Xinyin Zhang,Boxun Li,Virginia Smith,Kevin Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合LoRA与全参数微调（MoLF）的统一框架，通过优化器级别的动态路由在FFT和LoRA之间切换，并针对内存受限环境提出了MoLF-Efficient版本，实验表明MoLF在所有设置下优于或接近FFT和LoRA中的最佳结果，MoLF-Efficient相比之前自适应LoRA方法提升最高20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示全参数微调在注入高熵知识方面具有表达力优势，而LoRA在低秩空间更新中因正则化效果可匹配甚至超越FFT，但依赖单一静态架构存在结构性局限，需要在两者之间进行动态权衡。

Method: 提出MoLF（Mixture of LoRA and Full Fine-Tuning）框架，在优化器级别动态路由更新到FFT和LoRA两个专家，确保两者在整个训练过程中都能获得精确梯度信号；同时提出MoLF-Efficient版本，冻结基础权重，仅在多个不同秩的LoRA专家之间路由更新。

Result: 在SQL、Medical QA和Counterfactual Knowledge三类任务以及多个小模型（Gemma-3-1B、Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-3B）上，MoLF在所有设置下优于或距离FFT/LoRA中最佳结果1.5%以内；MoLF-Efficient在Fact任务上比之前自适应LoRA方法提升20%，在Med和SQL上提升9%。

Conclusion: MoLF框架有效解决了FFT和LoRA之间的结构性权衡问题，通过动态路由机制在不同训练阶段自适应地选择最优更新方式，在保持性能的同时提供了更灵活的训练策略。

Abstract: Recent literature on fine-tuning Large Language Models highlights a fundamental debate. While Full Fine-Tuning (FFT) provides the representational plasticity required for high-entropy knowledge injection, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) can match or surpass FFT performance because many tasks only require updates in a low-rank space and benefit from LoRA's additional regularization. Through empirical evaluation across diverse tasks (SQL, Medical QA, and Counterfactual Knowledge) and varying language models (Gemma-3-1B, Qwen2.5-1.5B, and Qwen2.5-3B), we verify both trends and demonstrate that relying solely on either static architecture is structurally limited. To address this challenge, we propose a Mixture of LoRA and Full (MoLF) Fine-Tuning, a unified framework that enables continuous navigation between both training regimes. MoLF dynamically routes updates between FFT and LoRA at the optimizer level to ensure that exact gradient signals are available to both experts throughout training, yielding stable training dynamics. For memory-constrained environments, we also introduce MoLF-Efficient, which freezes base weights and only routes updates among a pair of LoRA experts of potentially varying rank. Our evaluations show that MoLF either improves on or stays within $1.5\%$ of the better of FFT and LoRA across all settings, while MoLF-Efficient outperforms prior adaptive LoRA approaches by up to $20\%$ on Fact and $9\%$ on Med and SQL.

</details>


### [134] [Region4Web: Rethinking Observation Space Granularity for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07134)
*Donguk Kwon,Dongha Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Region4Web框架，通过层次化分解和语义抽象将AXTree重组为功能区域，并设计PageDigest推理管线为智能体提供紧凑的页面摘要。实验表明，在WebArena基准上，该方法显著降低观测长度并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Web智能体以元素级粒度进行观测，忽略了页面的功能组织结构，迫使智能体每一步都从元素级信号中推断页面功能，效率低下且信息冗余。

Method: 提出Region4Web框架，通过层次化分解和语义抽象将AXTree重组为功能区域；设计PageDigest，一个面向Web的推理管线，为智能体提供跨步骤持久化的、紧凑的页面级摘要。

Result: 在WebArena基准上，PageDigest显著减少了观测长度，并在多种大语言模型和智能体方法上提升了整体任务成功率，不受骨干模型容量限制。

Conclusion: 以功能区域粒度进行观测比元素级处理能为智能体提供更紧凑且信息更丰富的决策基础。

Abstract: Web agents perceive web pages through an observation space, yet its granularity has remained an underexamined design choice. Existing work treats observation at the same element-level granularity as the action space, leaving the page's functional organization implicit and forcing the agent to infer it from element-level signals at every step. We argue observation should instead operate at the granularity of functional regions, parts of the page that each serve a distinct purpose. We propose Region4Web, a framework that reorganizes the AXTree into functional regions through hierarchical decomposition and semantic abstraction, exposing the page's functional organization as the basis for page state understanding. Moreover, we propose PageDigest, a web-specific inference pipeline that delivers this region-level observation to the actor agent as a compact per-page digest that persists across steps. On the WebArena benchmark, PageDigest substantially reduces observation length while improving overall task success rate across diverse backbone large language models (LLMs) and established agent methods, regardless of backbone capacity. These results show that operating at the granularity of functional regions delivers a more compact and informative basis for the actor agent than element-level processing alone.

</details>


### [135] [Structural Rationale Distillation via Reasoning Space Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07139)
*Jialin Yang,Jiankun Wang,Jiajun Wu,Henry Leung,Jiayu Zhou,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: D-RPC通过压缩教师模型的推理路径为可复用的高层模板，在保持多样性同时减少监督噪声，从而提升小模型在数学和常识推理任务上的蒸馏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在蒸馏推理能力到小模型时，对相似问题的推理链在结构和策略上差异极大（"像厨师做同一道菜每次手法不同"），这种不一致性给学生模型带来难以内化的噪声监督信号。

Method: D-RPC维护一个动态更新的可复用高层推理路径库；对每个训练问题检索最相关的路径，并以此约束教师模型生成一致的推理链；通过PAC-Bayes分析形式化了路径库大小与覆盖率的权衡。

Result: 在5个数学和常识推理基准上，使用两种学生模型，D-RPC一致优于思维链蒸馏、自由形式推理生成、直接蒸馏和结构化监督基线，且比模板繁重的方法使用更少token。

Conclusion: 约束教师模型使用一致的推理路径能有效降低监督熵、提升蒸馏效率，且存在一个最优的路径库大小，被理论分析和消融实验共同验证。

Abstract: When distilling reasoning from large language models (LLMs) into smaller ones, teacher rationales for similar problems often vary wildly in structure and strategy. Like a chef who makes the same dish differently each time, this inconsistency burdens the student with noisy supervision that is hard to internalize. We propose Distillation through Reasoning Path Compression (D-RPC), which constrains the teacher to follow a compact, dynamically maintained bank of reusable high-level reasoning paths. For each training question, D-RPC retrieves the most relevant path and conditions the teacher to follow it, producing rationales that are consistent across similar problems yet diverse enough to cover different problem types. A PAC-Bayes analysis formalizes the resulting trade-off between bank size and coverage: smaller banks reduce supervision entropy but risk coverage gaps, and the generalization bound identifies an optimal intermediate size confirmed by our ablations. Across five math and commonsense reasoning benchmarks with two student models, D-RPC consistently outperforms chain-of-thought distillation, freeform rationale generation, direct distillation, and structured-supervision baselines, while using fewer tokens than template-heavy alternatives.

</details>


### [136] [Beyond Reasoning: Reinforcement Learning Unlocks Parametric Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07153)
*Wanli Yang,Hongyu Zang,Junwei Zhang,Wenjie Shi,Du Su,Jingang Wang,Xueqi Cheng,Fei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习（RL）能否提升LLM对参数化知识的直接回忆能力。在零样本、一跳、闭卷QA设定下（无思维链，仅使用二元正确性奖励），RL在三个模型家族和多个事实QA基准上取得了约27%的平均相对增益。机制上，RL主要重新分配已有知识的概率质量，将正确答案从低概率尾部移动到可靠的贪心生成中。最难样例（答案在128次RL前采样中从未出现）仅占训练数据~18%，却驱动了~83%的收益。这表明RL的作用超越了推理，成为解锁而非获取潜在参数化知识的工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究强化学习除了提升LLM的推理能力外，是否也能改善其对参数化知识的直接回忆能力——这是一个尚未被充分研究的开放问题。

Method: 在零样本、一跳、闭卷QA设定下（无思维链），仅使用二元正确性奖励进行RL训练，并应用事实级别的训练-测试去重确保收益反映回忆提升而非推理或记忆。在多个模型家族和事实QA基准上评估，并进行机制分析和数据归因研究。

Result: RL在三个模型家族和多个事实QA基准上取得约27%平均相对增益，超越训练和推理时基线。机制上，RL主要重新分配已有知识的概率质量而非获取新事实。最难样例（答案从未出现）仅占~18%训练数据却驱动~83%的收益。

Conclusion: RL的作用超越了推理范畴，可作为一种解锁而非获取潜在参数化知识的工具。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved remarkable success in LLM reasoning, but whether it can also improve direct recall of parametric knowledge remains an open question. We study this question in a controlled zero-shot, one-hop, closed-book QA setting with no chain-of-thought, training only on binary correctness rewards and applying fact-level train-test deduplication to ensure gains reflect improved recall rather than reasoning or memorization. Across three model families and multiple factual QA benchmarks, RL yields ~27% average relative gains, surpassing both training- and inference-time baselines alike. Mechanistically, RL primarily redistributes probability mass over existing knowledge rather than acquiring new facts, moving correct answers from the low-probability tail into reliable greedy generations. Our data-attribution study reveals that the hardest examples are the most informative: those whose answers never appear in 128 pre-RL samples (only ~18% of training data) drive ~83% of the gain, since rare correct rollouts still emerge during training and get reinforced. Together, these findings broaden the role of RL beyond reasoning, repositioning it as a tool for unlocking rather than acquiring latent parametric knowledge.

</details>


### [137] [CLIPer: Tailoring Diverse User Preference via Classifier-Guided Inference-Time Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07162)
*Jinyan Su,Jinpeng Zhou,Claire Cardie,Wen Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLIPer是一种轻量级的推理时个性化方法，通过分类器引导LLM动态适应不同用户偏好，无需微调即可实现可控个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖对用户偏好组合进行微调，计算成本高昂且不实用，亟需一种轻量级、可动态调整的个性化方案。

Method: 提出CLIPer框架，在推理阶段使用分类器模型引导LLM生成，根据用户偏好（如帮助性、简洁性、幽默感）动态调整输出，无需微调基础模型。

Result: 实验表明CLIPer在单维度和多维度偏好上均能有效实现可扩展的个性化生成，额外计算开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: CLIPer为LLM个性化提供了一种高效、可控的推理时解决方案，避免了微调的高昂成本，同时保持了生成质量。

Abstract: Personalized LLMs can significantly enhance user experiences by tailoring responses to preferences such as helpfulness, conciseness, and humor. However, fine-tuning models to address all possible combinations of user preferences is computationally expensive and impractical. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{CLIPer}(\textbf{Cl}assifier-guided \textbf{I}nference-time \textbf{Per}sonalization), a lightweight personalization approach that leverages a classifier model to steer LLM generation dynamically to different user preferences at inference time. Our method eliminates the need for extensive fine-tuning, inducing negligible additional computational overhead while enabling more controllable and nuanced personalization across single and multi-dimensional preferences. Comprehensive empirical analyses demonstrate the scalability and effectiveness of our approach in delivering personalized language generation.

</details>


### [138] [Rethinking Experience Utilization in Self-Evolving Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07164)
*Weixiang Zhao,Yingshuo Wang,Yichen Zhang,Yanyan Zhao,Yu Zhang,Yang Wu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文研究自我进化智能体在运行时如何有效利用经验，提出ExpWeaver方法——将经验作为可选资源融入推理过程，仅在需要额外指导时调用。实验表明该方法优于现有刚性使用策略，并揭示经验应在高推理不确定性时选择性调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我进化智能体研究主要关注经验的构建、表示和更新，却忽视了经验在运行时决策中应如何使用的问题。大多数智能体采用刚性使用策略（初始化时一次性注入或每步都注入），不考虑当前决策是否需要经验辅助。

Method: 提出ExpWeaver，一种轻量级实现：保持经验构建不变，仅修改运行时利用方式——将经验作为推理过程中的可选资源，只有在需要额外指导时才被调用。在四种代表性框架、七种LLM基座模型和三类环境中进行验证。

Result: ExpWeaver在不同利用策略中持续取得最佳性能；强化学习实验表明该行为可通过训练被放大；使用模式分析、因果消融和基于熵的分析揭示ExpWeaver使智能体能够选择性调用经验，在有利的决策点和高推理不确定性下调用。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁从仅关注"存储什么经验"转向理解"经验应如何及何时进入决策过程"。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents improve by accumulating and reusing experience from past interactions. Existing work has largely focused on how experience is constructed, represented, and updated, while paying less attention to how experience should be used during runtime decision-making. As a result, most agents rely on rigid usage strategies, either injecting experience once at initialization or at every step, without considering whether it is needed for the current decision. This paper studies experience utilization as a critical design dimension of self-evolving agents. We ask whether agents benefit from interweaving experience use with decision-making, so that experience is invoked only when additional guidance is needed. To examine this question, we introduce {ExpWeaver}, a lightweight instantiation that leaves experience construction unchanged and modifies only runtime utilization by exposing experience as an optional resource during reasoning. Across four representative frameworks, seven LLM backbones, and three types of environments, ExpWeaver consistently achieves the best performance among different utilization strategies. Reinforcement learning experiments further show that this behavior can be amplified through training. Usage-pattern, causal ablation, and entropy-based analyses reveal that ExpWeaver enables agents to invoke experience selectively, at beneficial decision points, and under higher reasoning uncertainty. Overall, our findings call for a shift from merely studying \emph{what} experience to store toward understanding \emph{how} and \emph{when} experience should enter decision-making.

</details>


### [139] [A Reproducible Multi-Architecture Baseline for Token-Level Chinese Metaphor Identification under the MIPVU Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07170)
*Yufeng Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在PSU中文隐喻语料库上系统比较了三种模型家族（RoBERTa微调、MelBERT、Qwen3.5-9B QLoRA）在MIPVU框架下的词级隐喻识别效果，发现MelBERT MIP-only取得最佳测试正类F1（0.7281），而Qwen生成式配置落后编码器基线约11个F1点。研究揭示了SPV通道在中文中无效、生成模型召回率不足、格式设计导致任务失败三个关键发现，并公开了完整资源作为未来研究的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文词级隐喻识别在MIPVU框架下研究不足，缺乏可重复的多架构基准，现有工作多聚焦英文或句子级别，且不同模型家族在中文数据集上的系统对比尚属空白。

Method: 在PSU CMC数据集上，使用固定五种随机种子，系统比较三类模型：(i)中文RoBERTa-wwm-ext-large编码器微调；(ii)基于MCD7构建基本义资源并适配中文的MelBERT（含MIP-only和Full变体）；(iii)Qwen3.5-9B经QLoRA微调作为指令微调生成式基线。

Result: MelBERT MIP-only以0.7281 F1最优，MelBERT Full为0.7270，RoBERTa为0.7142，Qwen QLoRA仅为0.6157。生成模型落后主要体现在召回率不足，SPV通道在中文中未贡献可靠信号。

Conclusion: 编码器模型显著优于生成式模型；SPV通道在中文隐喻识别中无效；生成模型因离散输出约束导致召回率瓶颈；任务格式设计对模型表现有决定性影响。

Abstract: Metaphor is pervasive in everyday language, yet token-level computational identification of metaphor-related words in Chinese under the MIPVU framework remains under-explored relative to English. This paper presents a reproducible multi-architecture baseline for token-level metaphor identification on the PSU Chinese Metaphor Corpus (PSU CMC), the only widely available MIPVU-annotated Chinese corpus. We systematically compare three model families: (i) encoder fine-tuning with Chinese RoBERTa-wwm-ext-large; (ii) MelBERT adapted to Chinese using a newly constructed basic-meaning resource derived from the Modern Chinese Dictionary, 7th edition (MCD7), comprising 74,823 entries with 71.51% PSU CMC vocabulary coverage; and (iii) Qwen3.5-9B fine-tuned with QLoRA as an instruction-tuned generative baseline. Across five fixed seeds, MelBERT MIP-only achieves the strongest performance at 0.7281 +/- 0.0050 test positive F1, marginally above MelBERT Full (0.7270 +/- 0.0069) and clearly above plain RoBERTa (0.7142 +/- 0.0121). The Qwen QLoRA generative configuration trails encoder baselines by approximately 11 F1 points (0.6157 +/- 0.0113). Three findings merit attention: (1) the SPV channel of MelBERT does not contribute reliable positive signal in Chinese, consistent with the dominance of conventional metaphor; (2) the Qwen-encoder gap is concentrated in recall, reflecting the discrete-commitment limitation of generative output; (3) several Qwen task formulations fail due to format design rather than model capacity. We release all split manifests, per-seed outputs, the MCD7 basic-meaning embedding pipeline, and training scripts to serve as a common reference for future Chinese metaphor identification research.

</details>


### [140] [Topology-Enhanced Alignment for Large Language Models: Trajectory Topology Loss and Topological Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07172)
*Yurui Pan,Ke Xu,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于持续同调（0维持久同调）的拓扑增强对齐框架，通过轨迹拓扑损失（TTL）优化SFT、拓扑偏好优化（TPO）优化DPO，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上超越多种强基线，验证了轨迹几何与持久同调在可控对齐中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法（SFT、RLHF/DPO）仅依赖局部token似然或标量分数，忽略了表示空间的全局几何结构，导致对齐过程中缺乏对语义轨迹整体拓扑的约束。

Method: 1) SFT阶段：将prompt与gold-answer嵌入作为混合点云，通过0维持久同调提取"prompt-answer桥梁"，提出轨迹拓扑损失（TTL）使模型更新方向对齐这些拓扑桥梁；2) DPO阶段：构造主题特定语义偏好向量，将rejected与chosen响应之间的改进方向与中间隐藏层的这些向量对齐，提出拓扑偏好优化（TPO），并引入动态加权机制平衡DPO与TPO损失。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上使用UltraChat和Anthropic HH-RLHF数据集，拓扑增强目标在自动偏好指标和LLM-judge评估中一致优于per-example、nearest-neighbor、random regularizer等强非拓扑基线，同时保持或改善了毒性指标。

Conclusion: 持久同调与轨迹几何为可控对齐提供了有前景的方向，拓扑约束能够有效引导LLM生成更符合语义拓扑结构的输出。

Abstract: Alignment of large language models (LLMs) via SFT and RLHF/DPO typically ignores the global geometry of the representation space, relying instead on local token likelihoods or scalar scores. We view generation as tracing a semantic trajectory in hidden space and propose a topology-enhanced alignment framework that regularizes these trajectories using 0-dimensional persistent homology. First, for SFT, we introduce Trajectory Topology Loss (TTL). Treating prompt and gold-answer embeddings as a mixed point cloud, we use a 0D persistent homology algorithm to extract "prompt-answer bridges." TTL aligns the model's actual update direction with these topological bridges rather than arbitrary directions. Second, for DPO, we propose Topological Preference Optimization (TPO). TPO constructs topic-specific semantic preference vectors and aligns the improvement direction between rejected and chosen responses with these vectors in an intermediate hidden layer. We also introduce a dynamic weighting scheme to balance DPO and TPO losses. Evaluating on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct using UltraChat and Anthropic HH-RLHF, our topology-enhanced objectives consistently outperform strong non-topological baselines (e.g., per-example, nearest-neighbor, random regularizers) on automatic preference metrics and LLM-judge evaluations, while maintaining or improving toxicity. Results show persistent homology and trajectory geometry offer a promising direction for controllable alignment.

</details>


### [141] [Learning Agent Routing From Early Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07180)
*Yimin Wang,Jiahao Qiu,Xuan Qi,Xinzhe Juan,Jingzhe Shi,Zelin Zhao,Hongru Wang,Shilong Liu,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BoundaryRouter，一种无需训练的路由框架，通过在轻量级LLM推理和完整Agent执行之间进行智能路由，在不牺牲性能的情况下显著降低延迟和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent在复杂推理任务上表现优异但延迟高、计算成本大；而许多查询实际上在轻量级LLM能力范围内，无需完整Agent执行。如何在冷启动场景下有效路由查询以平衡效率与性能是核心挑战。

Method: 提出BoundaryRouter框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 基于早期行为经验构建紧凑的经验记忆库；(2) 基于语料引导的推理（rubric-guided reasoning）辅助路由决策；(3) 在推理时检索相似案例指导路由。同时构建RouteBench基准测试，涵盖域内、释义和域外三种路由场景。

Result: 相比完整Agent，推理时间降低60.6%；相比直接LLM推理，性能提升28.6%；相比基于提示的路由和仅检索路由，平均性能分别提升37.9%和8.2%。

Conclusion: BoundaryRouter在冷启动场景下实现了高效且有效的查询路由，显著降低了计算成本同时保持了较高的任务性能，验证了无需训练的路由框架在实际应用中的可行性。

Abstract: LLM agents achieve strong performance on complex reasoning tasks but incur high latency and compute cost. In practice, many queries fall within the capability boundary of cutting-edge LLMs and do not require full agent execution, making effective routing between LLMs and agents a key challenge. We study the problem of routing queries between lightweight LLM inference and full agent execution under realistic cold-start settings. To address this, we propose BoundaryRouter, a training-free routing framework that uses early behavioral experience and rubric-guided reasoning to decide whether to answer a query with direct LLM inference or escalate to an agent. BoundaryRouter builds a compact experience memory by executing both systems on a shared seed set and retrieves similar cases at inference time to guide routing decisions. To evaluate this method, we introduce RouteBench, a benchmark covering in-domain, paraphrased, and out-of-domain route settings. Experiments show that BoundaryRouter reduces inference time by 60.6% compared to the agent while improving performance by 28.6% over direct LLM inference, outperforming prompt-based and retrieval-only routing by an average of 37.9% and 8.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [142] [The Text Uncanny Valley: Non-Monotonic Performance Degradation in LLM Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07186)
*Zekai Tong,Ruiyao Xu,Aryan Shrivastava,Chenhao Tan,Ari Holtzman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现，在单词中插入空格破坏单词边界时，LLM检测目标信息的准确率呈U型曲线（即“文本恐怖谷”现象），并提出了模式转换假说来解释这一现象：LLM在接近正常文本时使用词级模式，在高度碎片化文本时使用字符级模式，而U型谷底正是两种模式均失效的混乱过渡区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准测试主要关注语法正确的输入，对不完美文本（如含噪声、拼写错误）的评估存在显著空白。本文旨在研究单词边界破坏（在单词内插入空格）如何影响LLM检测目标信息的能力。

Method: 通过系统性地在单词内部插入空白字符来破坏单词边界，测量LLM检测准确率随插入率变化的情况；提出“模式转换假说”（词级模式→字符级模式的过渡）；设计四项实验和一项分析进行验证：上下文学习、正则化扰动、数学推理任务（对比不同模型）、以及分词熵分析。

Result: 检测准确率随插入率增加呈U型曲线；上下文学习无法挽救谷底性能；正则化扰动显著减弱U型效应；数学推理任务在较弱模型（Gemini 3.0 Flash）上复现U型，但更强模型上不显著；分词熵在F1最小值之前达到峰值，支持模式冲突解释。

Conclusion: 揭示了LLM在面对含噪声/未经整理的文本输入时存在一种在清洁文本基准测试中不可见的失效模式，这对实际部署场景具有直接意义。

Abstract: Existing Large Language Model (LLM) benchmarks primarily focus on syntactically correct inputs, leaving a significant gap in evaluation on imperfect text. In this work, we study how word-boundary corruption affects how LLMs detect targeted information. By inserting whitespace characters within words to break them into fragments, LLMs' detection accuracy follows a U-shaped curve with the increase in insertion rate. We refer to this curve as the Text Uncanny Valley. To explain such observation, we propose a mode transition hypothesis: LLMs operate in a word-level mode for near-normal text and a character-level mode for heavily fragmented text, with the valley marking the disordered transition where neither mode is effective. Four experiments and one analysis are consistent with this account: in-context learning fails to rescue valley-bottom performance; regularizing the perturbation substantially reduces the U-shape; a math reasoning task replicates the U-shape for Gemini 3.0 Flash but not for stronger models, suggesting the effect is attenuated when tasks rely less on exact lexical alignment; and tokenization entropy peaks before the F1 minimum, consistent with a regime-conflict interpretation. These findings reveal a failure mode invisible to clean-text benchmarks yet directly relevant to any deployment scenario involving noisy or uncurated text inputs.

</details>


### [143] [PSK@EEUCA 2026: Fine-Tuning Large Language Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation for Multi-Class Toxicity Detection in Gaming Chat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07201)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了结合Llama 3.1 8B与5%合成数据增强的分类系统，在EEUCA 2026《坦克世界》聊天消息毒性分类任务中取得0.6234 F1-macro，排名第4/35。研究发现“验证陷阱”现象：高验证性能反而导致测试泛化能力下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏社区中存在毒性行为，需要自动分类系统来识别和缓解。EEUCA 2026共享任务旨在推动该领域研究，具体目标是对《坦克世界》聊天消息进行六类毒性分类。

Method: 探索多种方法：基于编码器的模型、指令微调LLM（LoRA微调）、层次分类、一对多策略、集成方法。最优系统为Llama 3.1 8B + 5%合成数据增强。

Result: 测试集F1-macro达0.6234，在35支队伍中排名第4。发现“验证陷阱”：高验证性能与低测试泛化能力负相关。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强策略有效提升模型性能；数据集标注模式影响模型泛化，需警惕验证集过拟合导致的测试集表现下降。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for the EEUCA 2026 Shared Task on Understanding Toxic Behavior in Gaming Communities. The task involves classifying World of Tanks chat messages into six toxicity categories: Non-toxic, Insults/Flaming, Other Offensive, Hate/Harassment, Threats, and Extremism. We explore multiple approaches including encoder-based models, instruction-tuned LLMs with LoRA fine-tuning, hierarchical classification, one-vs-rest strategies, and various ensemble methods. Our best system combines Llama 3.1 8B with carefully calibrated 5\% synthetic data augmentation, achieving an F1-macro score of 0.6234 on the test set, placing 4th out of 35 participating teams. We provide extensive analysis of the dataset's annotation patterns and their impact on model generalization, revealing a critical ''validation trap'' phenomenon where high validation performance correlates with poor test transfer.

</details>


### [144] [Reformulating KV Cache Eviction Problem for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07234)
*Tho Mai,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LaProx方法，将KV缓存淘汰从逐头权重平均方法重新表述为输出感知的层间矩阵乘法近似问题，通过显式建模注意力图与投影值状态之间的乘法交互来准确量化token贡献，并考虑跨头依赖。实验表明仅用5% KV缓存即可保持模型性能，在极端压缩下相比现有最优方法减少高达2倍准确率损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法主要依赖局部注意力权重，忽略了值表示、输出投影和跨头交互的影响，导致在长上下文推理中内存和运行时开销大。

Method: 将KV缓存淘汰重新表述为输出感知的层间矩阵乘法近似问题，提出LaProx方法，显式建模注意力图与投影值状态之间的乘法交互以量化token贡献，并设计统一淘汰策略分配全局可比较的重要性分数。

Result: 在LongBench和Needle-In-A-Haystack的19个数据集上，仅用5% KV缓存即可保持模型性能，在所有配置下持续优于现有方法，极端压缩下准确率损失降低高达2倍。

Conclusion: LaProx通过输出感知的矩阵乘法近似视角和全局统一淘汰策略，有效解决了KV缓存压缩问题，在性能和效率之间取得了更优平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) support long-context inference but suffer from substantial memory and runtime overhead due to Key-Value (KV) Cache growth. Existing KV Cache eviction methods primarily rely on local attention weights, neglecting the influence of value representations, output projection, and inter-head interactions. In this work, we reformulate KV Cache eviction from a conventional head-wise, weight-averaging approach into an output-aware, layer-wise matrix multiplication approximation problem. We introduce LaProx, a novel eviction strategy that explicitly models the multiplicative interaction between attention maps and projected value states to accurately quantify token contributions while accounting for inter-head dependencies. Building on this metric, we propose the first unified eviction strategy that assigns globally comparable importance scores to tokens, enabling model-wide selection instead of local, head-wise decisions. Experimental results across 19 datasets on long-context benchmarks LongBench and Needle-In-A-Haystack demonstrate that our approach maintains model performance with only 5\% of the KV cache and consistently outperforms prior works across all configurations.
