The paper concludes that although deep learning can capture sequential dependencies, classical machine learning remains a strong baseline when combined with effective feature engineering such as TF-IDF, particularly under limited data and computational resources.

</details>


### [181] [SCENE: Recognizing Social Norms and Sanctioning in Group Chats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07823)
*Mateusz Jacniacki,Maksymilian Bilski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCENE基准，用于评估LLM智能体在多人群聊中识别和适应隐性社交规范与制裁的能力。测试6个模型发现，Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro的规范适应能力显著优于开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在在线群聊中识别和适应隐性行为规范的能力尚未被充分探索，缺乏相关的评估基准和方法。

Method: 构建SCENE基准，生成非角色扮演的多人聊天场景，包含脚本化人格、隐藏规范、违规触发和制裁机制；提出两种功能适应能力的评估指标：对负面制裁的响应性，以及从同伴行为中学习规范的能力。

Result: Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro在隐性规范适应方面显著优于所有评估的开源模型。

Conclusion: SCENE为动态、交互式的LLM社交能力评估提供了新的基准方向，填补了隐性规范适应评估的空白。

Abstract: Online group chats are social spaces with implicit behavior patterns that, when broken, are often met with social sanctioning from the group. The ability and willingness of LLM-based agents to recognize and adapt to these norms remains mostly unexplored. We introduce SCENE, a social-interaction benchmark focused on implicit norms and social sanctioning in multi-party chat. SCENE generates plausible non-roleplay scenarios with scripted personas that follow a hidden norm, create opportunities for the subject agent to violate it, and sanction breaches when they occur. We further propose behavioral evaluation metrics for two functional adaptation abilities: responsiveness to negative sanctioning, and adapting norm from peers behavior. We evaluate six frontier and open-weight models on SCENE. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro adapt to implicit norms significantly more than the evaluated open-weight models. SCENE contributes one benchmark in the direction of recent calls for dynamic, interactional evaluation of LLM social capabilities.

</details>


### [182] [Measuring and Mitigating the Distributional Gap Between Real and Simulated User Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07847)
*Shuhaib Mehri,Philippe Laban,Sumuk Shashidhar,Marwa Abdulhai,Sergey Levine,Michel Galley,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化评估AI用户模拟器与真实用户行为分布差异的方法，通过对24种基于LLM的用户模拟器在编程和写作任务上的系统评估，发现模拟器与真实用户之间存在显著分布差距，且不同模型家族、规模和行为维度差异各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器广泛用于AI助手的交互训练与评估，但现有研究仅关注模拟器生成类人回复的能力，尚未验证其是否能够捕捉真实用户行为的广泛异质分布。需要一种量化方法来衡量这一分布差距。

Method: 从真实和模拟对话中提取用户行为表征，通过聚类将其量化为离散分布，然后计算散度指标来衡量分布差异。结合人类研究和消融实验验证方法的有效性。

Result: 对24种基于LLM的用户模拟器在编程和写作任务上的系统评估表明，模拟器与真实用户之间存在较大分布差距，该差距因模型家族、规模和行为维度而异。多数模拟器行为相似，少数与众不同。结合行为互补的模拟器可使分布更接近真实用户。TF-IDF分析揭示了模拟器能捕捉、遗漏和幻想的可解释行为模式。

Conclusion: 现有用户模拟器在行为分布上与真实用户存在显著差距，但通过组合行为互补的模拟器可以缩小这一差距。所提方法为评估和改进用户模拟器提供了有效工具。

Abstract: As user simulators are increasingly used for interactive training and evaluation of AI assistants, it is essential that they represent the diverse behaviors of real users. While existing works train user simulators to generate human-like responses, whether they capture the broad and heterogeneous distribution of real user behaviors remains an open question. In this work, we introduce a method to measure the distributional gap between real and simulated user behaviors, validated through a human study and ablations. Given a dataset of real and simulated conversations, our method extracts representations of user behavior from each conversation, quantizes them into discrete distributions via clustering, then computes divergence metrics. We provide the first systematic evaluation of 24 LLM-based user simulators on coding and writing tasks, and reveal a large distributional gap from real users that varies across model families, scales, and behavioral facets. Pairwise comparisons show that most simulators behave similarly, while a few stand apart. Combining behaviorally complementary simulators brings the resulting distribution closer to real users compared to either simulator on its own. Finally, a TF-IDF analysis of the clusters surfaces interpretable patterns of behaviors that simulators capture, miss, and hallucinate.

</details>


### [183] [MatryoshkaLoRA: Learning Accurate Hierarchical Low-Rank Representations for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07850)
*Ionut-Vlad Modoranu,Mher Safaryan,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MatryoshkaLoRA，通过在LoRA适配器之间插入一个精心设计的对角矩阵P，学习层次化低秩表示，支持动态秩选择，并在精度与效率间取得更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA需要静态预定义秩r，需穷举网格搜索；而DyLoRA等秩自适应方法在高秩时因缺乏一致的梯度信号导致次优结果，数据效率低下。

Method: 在LoRA适配器之间插入一个固定的对角矩阵P，通过缩放子秩（sub-ranks）来学习层次化低秩表示；仅改变P即可恢复LoRA和DyLoRA，确保所有子秩都能高效嵌入梯度信息。同时提出AURAC指标用于评估层次化低秩适配器的性能。

Result: MatryoshkaLoRA比先前的秩自适应方法学习到更准确的层次化低秩表示，在多个数据集上实现了更优的精度-性能权衡。

Conclusion: MatryoshkaLoRA是一种通用的秩自适应训练框架，通过简单的架构修改实现了动态秩选择，在保持精度的同时显著提升了参数效率。

Abstract: With the rise in scale for deep learning models to billions of parameters, the computational cost of fine-tuning remains a significant barrier to deployment. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become the standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning, the need to set a predefined, static rank $r$ requires exhaustive grid searches to balance efficiency and performance. Existing rank-adaptive solutions such as DyLoRA mitigate this by sampling ranks during the training from a predefined distribution. However, they often yield sub-optimal results at higher ranks due to lack of consistent gradient signals across the full hierarchy of ranks, thus making these methods data-inefficient. In this paper, we propose MatryoshkaLoRA, a general, Matryoshka-inspired training framework for LoRA that learns accurate hierarchical low-rank representations by inserting a fixed, carefully crafted diagonal matrix $P$ between the existing LoRA adapters to scale their sub-ranks accordingly. By introducing this simple modification, our general framework recovers LoRA and DyLoRA only by changing $P$ and ensures all sub-ranks embed the available gradient information efficiently. Our MatryoshkaLoRA supports dynamic rank selection with minimal degradation in accuracy. We further propose Area Under the Rank Accuracy Curve (AURAC), a metric that consistently evaluates the performance of hierarchical low-rank adapters. Our results demonstrate that MatryoshkaLoRA learns more accurate hierarchical low-rank representations than prior rank-adaptive approaches and achieves superior accuracy-performance trade-offs across ranks on the evaluated datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/MatryoshkaLoRA.

</details>


### [184] [Beyond "I cannot fulfill this request": Alleviating Rigid Rejection in LLMs via Label Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07883)
*Ying Zhang,Congyu Qiao,Xin Geng,Ning Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LANCE通过标签增强和变分推断预测细粒度拒绝分布，为精炼模型提供多路文本梯度以中和提示中的有害元素，使LLM既能生成安全响应又能避免僵化拒绝，在保持高安全标准的同时显著提升交互的自然性和有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM安全对齐中的拒绝机制存在"僵化拒绝"问题，即使用通用模板（如"我无法满足这个请求"）不加区分地触发拒绝，严重损害了人机交互的自然性。

Method: 提出LANCE框架，利用变分推断进行标签增强，预测跨多个拒绝类别的连续分布；这些细粒度拒绝分布为精炼模型提供多路文本梯度，用于中和提示中的有害元素，使LLM生成安全且避免僵化拒绝的响应。

Result: 实验表明LANCE显著缓解了僵化拒绝问题，同时维持了高安全标准，在响应的有用性和自然性方面显著优于现有基线模型。

Conclusion: LANCE证明了通过细粒度标签增强和多路文本梯度引导的方法，可以在保证安全的前提下有效提升LLM交互的自然性和灵活性，为解决安全对齐中的僵化拒绝问题提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on safety alignment to obey safe requests while refusing harmful ones. However, traditional refusal mechanisms often lead to "rigid rejection," where a general template (e.g., "I cannot fulfill this request") indiscriminately triggers refusals and severely undermines the naturalness of interactions between humans and LLMs. To address this issue, LANCE is proposed in this paper to ensure safe yet flexible and natural responses via label enhancement. Specifically, LANCE employs variational inference to perform label enhancement, predicting a continuous distribution across multiple rejection categories. These fine-grained rejection distributions provide multi-way textual gradients for a refinement model to neutralize the hazardous elements in the prompt, so that the LLMs could generate safe responses that avoid rigid rejections while preserving the naturalness of interactions. Experiments demonstrate that LANCE significantly alleviates the rigid rejection problem while maintaining high security standards, significantly outperforming existing baseline models in terms of helpfulness and naturalness of responses.

</details>


### [185] [CoCoReviewBench: A Completeness- and Correctness-Oriented Benchmark for AI Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07905)
*Hexuan Deng,Xiaopeng Ke,Yichen Li,Ruina Hu,Dehao Huang,Derek F. Wong,Yue Wang,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对AI审稿系统评估中人工标注不可靠的问题，构建了类别特定的基准子集，利用审稿人-作者-元审稿讨论过滤不可靠评论，提出CoCoReviewBench基准（包含3900篇ICLR/NeurIPS论文），实现更可靠细粒度的AI审稿评估，发现现有AI审稿存在幻觉问题，推理模型表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI审稿系统评估依赖人工审稿作为黄金参考，但人工审稿通常仅覆盖部分关键问题且包含错误，不可靠，亟需更可靠的评估方法。

Method: 1) 构建类别特定基准子集，在缺失对应人工审稿时跳过评估以增强完备性；2) 利用审稿人-作者-元审稿讨论作为专家标注，过滤不可靠审稿以增强正确性；3) 构建CoCoReviewBench基准，涵盖ICLR和NeurIPS共3900篇论文。

Result: AI审稿在正确性方面仍然有限，容易产生幻觉；推理模型作为审稿人表现更有效。

Conclusion: CoCoReviewBench提供了更可靠的AI审稿评估基准，揭示了现有AI审稿的局限性，并为改进方向（特别是推理模型的应用）提供了指引。

Abstract: Despite the rapid development of AI reviewers, evaluating such systems remains challenging: metrics favor overlap with human reviews over correctness. However, since human reviews often cover only a subset of salient issues and sometimes contain mistakes, they are unreliable as gold references. To address this, we build category-specific benchmark subsets and skip evaluation when the corresponding human reviews are missing to strengthen Completeness. We also leverage reviewer--author--meta-review discussions as expert annotations and filter unreliable reviews accordingly to strengthen Correctness. Finally, we introduce CoCoReviewBench, which curates 3,900 papers from ICLR and NeurIPS to enable reliable and fine-grained evaluation of AI reviewers. Analysis shows that AI reviewers remain limited in correctness and are prone to hallucinations, and highlights reasoning models as more effective reviewers, motivating further directions for improving AI reviewers. Benchmarks and models are available at https://github.com/hexuandeng/CoCoReviewBench.

</details>


### [186] [How Value Induction Reshapes LLM Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07925)
*Arnav Arora,Natalie Schluter,Katherine Metcalf,Maartje ter Hoeve*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在对话式大语言模型中植入特定价值观（如好奇心、开放性、共情、有用性、无害性、诚实性）的意外效应，包括对其他价值观表达的影响、模型安全性、拟人化语言使用以及QA基准表现。发现：植入价值观会引发其他相关甚至对立价值观的表达；植入正向价值观可提升安全性；所有价值观都会增加拟人化语言，使模型更具验证性和谄媚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究价值观植入在LLM中的意外效应，特别是价值观之间的相互影响、对模型安全性的潜在改变，以及可能引发的成瘾性、谄媚性等负面行为，从而更全面地理解价值观对齐的副作用。

Method: 利用现有偏好数据集的价值观子集对模型进行微调，分别测量价值观植入对其他价值观表达、模型安全性、拟人化语言以及各类QA基准性能的影响。

Result: (i) 植入某种价值观会导致其他相关甚至对立价值观的表达发生变化；(ii) 植入正向价值观能提升模型安全性；(iii) 所有被测试的价值观都会增加拟人化语言使用，使模型更具验证性和谄媚性。

Conclusion: 价值观植入存在复杂的连锁效应，正向价值观虽能提升安全性，但同时会通过拟人化语言增加谄媚行为，需在设计对齐策略时权衡这些意外后果。

Abstract: Conversational Large Language Models are post-trained on language that expresses specific behavioural traits, such as curiosity, open-mindedness, and empathy, and values, such as helpfulness, harmlessness, and honesty. This is done to increase utility, ensure safety, and improve the experience of the people interacting with the model. However, values are complex and inter-related -- inducing one could modify behaviour on another. Further, inducing certain values can make models more addictive or sycophantic through language used in the generations, with a potential detrimental effect on the user. We investigate these and other unintended effects of value induction into models. We fine-tune models using curated value subsets of existing preference datasets, measuring the impact of value induction on expression of other values, model safety, anthropomorphic language, and various QA benchmarks. We find that (i) inducing values leads to expression of other related, and sometimes contrastive values, (ii) inducing positive values increases safety, and (iii) all values increase anthropomorphic language use, making models more validating and sycophantic.

</details>


### [187] [How to Train Your Latent Diffusion Language Model Jointly With the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07933)
*Viacheslav Meshchaninov,Alexander Shabalin,Egor Chimbulatov,Nikita Gushchin,Ilya Koziev,Alexander Korotin,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LDLM（潜在扩散语言模型），通过联合训练潜在编码器、扩散模型和解码器来构建适合文本生成的潜在空间，并设计了包括MSE解码器损失、扩散到编码器预热、自适应时间步采样和解码器输入噪声在内的训练策略。在OpenWebText和LM1B上，LDLM在生成性能上超越现有离散和连续扩散语言模型，同时快2-13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在扩散模型的主要挑战在于构建一个合适的潜在空间。现有方法未能有效解决潜在空间的构建问题，导致扩散模型质量较低。

Method: LDLM方法包括：（1）联合训练潜在编码器、扩散模型和解码器；（2）利用预训练语言模型的表示并通过可训练编码器重塑潜在空间；（3）提出简单训练策略：MSE解码器损失、扩散到编码器预热、自适应时间步采样以及解码器输入噪声。

Result: 在OpenWebText和LM1B数据集上，LDLM在生成性能上优于现有的离散和连续扩散语言模型，同时推理速度提升2-13倍。消融实验表明各训练组件对生成性能均有显著影响。

Conclusion: 联合学习潜在空间是使潜在扩散模型在文本生成中具备竞争力的关键步骤。LDLM通过联合训练和精心设计的训练策略，在效率和性能上均取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models offer an attractive alternative to discrete diffusion for non-autoregressive text generation by operating on continuous text representations and denoising entire sequences in parallel. The major challenge in latent diffusion modeling is constructing a suitable latent space. In this work, we present the Latent Diffusion Language Model (LDLM), in which the latent encoder, diffusion model, and decoder are trained jointly. LDLM builds its latent space by reshaping the representations of a pre-trained language model with a trainable encoder, yielding latents that are easy to both denoise and decode into tokens. We show that naive joint training produces a low-quality diffusion model, and propose a simple training recipe consisting of an MSE decoder loss, diffusion-to-encoder warmup, adaptive timestep sampling, and decoder-input noise. Ablations show that each component substantially impacts generation performance. On OpenWebText and LM1B, LDLM achieves better generation performance than existing discrete and continuous diffusion language models while being $2{\text -}13\times$ faster, indicating that jointly learning the latent space is a key step toward making latent diffusion competitive for text generation.

</details>


### [188] [Ask Early, Ask Late, Ask Right: When Does Clarification Timing Matter for Long-Horizon Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07937)
*Anmol Gulati,Hariom Gupta,Elias Lumer,Sahil Sen,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了长程AI智能体在执行复杂任务时，不同信息维度（目标、输入、约束、上下文）的澄清时机对任务成功率的影响。通过强制注入框架在受控时间点提供真实澄清，发现澄清价值高度依赖于缺失信息的类型：目标澄清在10%执行进度后几乎失效，而输入澄清可持续至50%。跨模型相关性分析证实这些时序特征是任务内在属性。进一步研究发现，当前所有前沿模型都未能在经验最优窗口内主动提问，策略要么过度提问（52%会话）要么从不提问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对长程AI智能体在执行过程中不同时间点进行澄清的价值变化的量化测量。当指令不完整时，智能体不仅需要决定是否提问，还需要决定何时提问，而此前没有工作衡量澄清价值随执行进程的变化规律。

Method: 提出强制注入框架，在智能体轨迹的受控时间点提供真实澄清，覆盖四个信息维度（目标、输入、约束、上下文）、三个智能体基准、四个前沿模型（共计84个任务变体、6000+次运行）。同时进行了300次无脚本会话的补充研究。

Result: 目标澄清在执行10%后几乎丧失所有价值（pass@3从0.78降至基线水平），输入澄清可持续到约50%执行进度。推迟任何澄清类型到轨迹中期之后都会导致性能低于从不提问。跨模型Kendall tau相关性（相同任务覆盖的模型间0.78-0.87，全模型面板0.34-0.67）表明时序特征是任务内在属性。当前无任何前沿模型能在经验最优窗口内提问。

Conclusion: 首次量化了AI智能体在不同执行阶段进行澄清的边际价值，为时序感知的澄清策略提供了定量基础和经验设计目标，弥补了现有理论框架缺乏实证数据的不足。

Abstract: Long-horizon AI agents execute complex workflows spanning hundreds of sequential actions, yet a single wrong assumption early on can cascade into irreversible errors. When instructions are incomplete, the agent must decide not only whether to ask for clarification but when, and no prior work measures how clarification value changes over the course of execution. We introduce a forced-injection framework that provides ground-truth clarifications at controlled points in the agent's trajectory across four information dimensions (goal, input, constraint, context), three agent benchmarks, and four frontier models (three per benchmark; one on a single benchmark only; 84 task variants; 6,000+ runs). Counter to the common intuition that "earlier is always better," we find that the value of clarification depends sharply on what information is missing: goal clarification loses nearly all value after 10% of execution (pass@3 drops from 0.78 to baseline), while input clarification retains value through roughly 50%. Deferring any clarification type past mid-trajectory degrades performance below never asking at all. Cross-model Kendall tau correlations (0.78-0.87 among models sharing identical task coverage; 0.34-0.67 across the full 4-model panel) confirm these timing profiles are substantially task-intrinsic. A complementary study of 300 unscripted sessions reveals that no current frontier model asks within the empirically optimal window, with strategies ranging from over-asking (52% of sessions) to never asking at all. These empirical demand curves provide the quantitative foundation that existing theoretical frameworks require but have lacked, and establish concrete design targets for timing-aware clarification policies. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [189] [GLiGuard: Schema-Conditioned Classification for LLM Safeguard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07982)
*Urchade Zaratiana,Mary Newhauser,George Hurn-Maloney,Ash Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiGuard，一个仅0.3B参数的基于双向编码器的内容审核模型，通过模式条件化输入设计，单次前向传播即可同时评估提示安全、响应安全、拒绝检测、14种细粒度危害类别和11种越狱策略，在9个安全基准上达到与7B-27B解码器模型相当的F1分数，同时吞吐量提升16倍、延迟降低17倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型内容审核依赖7B-27B参数的自回归解码器，将分类问题重构为序列生成任务，导致高延迟且难以扩展至多维度评估，亟需更高效轻量的解决方案。

Method: 基于GLiNER2改造的0.3B参数双向编码器，通过将任务定义和标签语义编码为结构化token模式注入输入序列，实现非自回归单次前向传播同时完成提示安全、响应安全、拒绝检测、14种危害类别和11种越狱策略的评估。

Result: 在9个安全基准上，GLiGuard以比7B-27B解码器模型小23-90倍的参数量取得相当的F1分数，吞吐量提升16倍，延迟降低17倍。

Conclusion: 紧凑型双向编码器能够在大幅降低推理成本的同时，接近大型护栏模型的准确率，为高效内容审核提供了新范式。

Abstract: Ensuring safe, policy-compliant outputs from large language models requires real-time content moderation that can scale across multiple safety dimensions. However, state-of-the-art guardrail models rely on autoregressive decoders with 7B--27B parameters, reformulating what is fundamentally a classification problem as sequential text generation, a design choice that incurs high latency and scales poorly to multi-aspect evaluation. In this work, we introduce \textbf{GLiGuard}, a 0.3B-parameter schema-conditioned bidirectional encoder adapted from GLiNER2 for LLM content moderation. The key idea is to encode task definitions and label semantics directly into the input sequence as structured token schemas, enabling simultaneous evaluation of prompt safety, response safety, refusal detection, 14 fine-grained harm categories, and 11 jailbreak strategies in a single non-autoregressive forward pass. This schema-conditioned design lets supported task and label blocks be composed directly in the input schema at inference time. Across nine established safety benchmarks, GLiGuard achieves F1 scores competitive with 7B--27B decoder-based guards despite being 23--90$\times$ smaller, while delivering up to 16$\times$ higher throughput and 17$\times$ lower latency. These results suggest that compact bidirectional encoders can approach the accuracy of much larger guard models while drastically reducing inference cost. Code and models are available at https://github.com/fastino-ai/GLiGuard.

</details>


### [190] [Fast Byte Latent Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08044)
*Julie Kallini,Artidoro Pagnoni,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Gargi Ghosh,Luke Zettlemoyer,Christopher Potts,Xiaochuang Han,Srinivasan Iyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Byte Latent Transformer (BLT)的三种加速推理方法：BLT-D（块级扩散并行生成）、BLT-S（自推测解码）、BLT-DV（扩散+自回归验证），在保持字节级语言模型质量的同时显著降低生成延迟，内存带宽成本降低超50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 字节级语言模型虽无需子词词表且性能与词元级模型相当，但其逐字节自回归生成方式极为缓慢，严重限制了实际应用。现有字节级模型因推理速度瓶颈而难以实用化。

Method: 1) BLT-D：在标准下一字节预测损失基础上添加辅助的块级扩散目标，训练后可在单次解码步骤中并行生成多个字节；2) BLT-S：利用BLT局部解码器超越常规patch边界继续生成草稿字节，再通过单次完整模型前向传播验证；3) BLT-DV：在基于扩散的生成后增加自回归验证步骤。

Result: 所有方法在生成任务上的估计内存带宽成本较原始BLT降低超50%。不同方法在速度与生成质量之间提供不同的权衡方案。

Conclusion: 三种方法各自具有独特优势，共同消除了字节级语言模型实际应用的关键障碍，为字节级模型的高效部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent byte-level language models (LMs) match the performance of token-level models without relying on subword vocabularies, yet their utility is limited by slow, byte-by-byte autoregressive generation. We address this bottleneck in the Byte Latent Transformer (BLT) through new training and generation techniques. First, we introduce BLT Diffusion (BLT-D), a new model and our fastest BLT variant, trained with an auxiliary block-wise diffusion objective alongside the standard next-byte prediction loss. This enables an inference procedure that generates multiple bytes in parallel per decoding step, substantially reducing the number of forward passes required to generate a sequence. Second, we propose two extensions inspired by speculative decoding that trade some of this speed for higher generation quality: BLT Self-speculation (BLT-S), in which BLT's local decoder continues generating past its normal patch boundaries to draft bytes, which are then verified with a single full-model forward pass; and BLT Diffusion+Verification (BLT-DV), which augments BLT-D with an autoregressive verification step after diffusion-based generation. All methods may achieve an estimated memory-bandwidth cost over 50% lower than BLT on generation tasks. Each approach offers its own unique advantages, together removing key barriers to the practical use of byte-level LMs.

</details>


### [191] [Uncertainty-Aware Structured Data Extraction from Full CMR Reports via Distilled LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08045)
*Yi Yu,Parker Martin,Zhenyu Bu,Yixuan Liu,Yi-Yu Zheng,Orlando Simonetti,Yuchi Han,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CMR-EXTR是一个轻量级框架，能将心脏磁共振自由文本报告转换为结构化数据，并利用教师-学生蒸馏管线和三重不确定性评分（分布合理性、采样稳定性、跨字段一致性）为每个字段分配置信度，实现离线推理并减少人工标注，达到99.65%的变量级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自由文本的心脏磁共振（CMR）报告转换为可审计的结构化数据仍是队列构建、纵向管理和临床决策支持的主要瓶颈。

Method: 采用教师-学生蒸馏管线实现完全离线推理并限制人工标注量；不确定性评分融合三种互补原则：分布合理性、采样稳定性和跨字段一致性，用于分诊人工审核。

Result: CMR-EXTR在变量级别达到99.65%的准确率，展示了可靠的提取能力和信息丰富的置信度分数。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对CMR报告且集成置信度估计的提取系统，代码已开源。

Abstract: Converting free-text cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) reports into auditable structured data remains a bottleneck for cohort assembly, longitudinal curation, and clinical decision support. We present CMR-EXTR, a lightweight framework that converts free-text CMR reports into structured data and assigns per-field confidence for quality control. A teacher-student distillation pipeline enables fully offline inference while limiting manual annotation. Uncertainty integrates three complementary principles -- distribution plausibility, sampling stability, and cross-field consistency -- to triage human review. Experiments show that CMR-EXTR achieves 99.65% variable-level accuracy, demonstrating both reliable extraction and informative confidence scores. To our knowledge, this is the first CMR-specific extraction system with integrated confidence estimation. The code is available at https://github.com/yuyi1005/CMR-EXTR.

</details>


### [192] [Accurate and Efficient Statistical Testing for Word Semantic Breadth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08048)
*Yo Ehara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Householder对齐置换检验方法，用于区分词义的上下文多样性差异与语义方向差异，在校正统计检验的I类错误率的同时保持了敏感性，并实现了GPU加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于离散度的词义广度假设检验容易将语义方向差异误判为广度差异，导致I类错误率膨胀。显著性检验应区分真实效应与微小差异下的偶然波动，因此需要一种能够分离方向差异与离散度差异的统计方法。

Method: 提出Householder对齐置换检验：首先通过Householder反射将两个词类型的均值方向对齐，然后在对齐后的词元向量云上进行置换检验，得到校准的非参数p值。同时设计了GPU批处理实现以加速线性代数和置换操作。

Result: 该方法将I类错误率降低了32.5%，同时保持了对真实广度差异的敏感性；GPU实现相比CPU基线实现了23倍加速。

Conclusion: Householder对齐置换检验能有效校正语义方向差异对词义广度统计检验的干扰，提供更可靠的p值，且具有实际应用的计算效率优势。

Abstract: Measuring the breadth of a word's meaning, or its spread across contexts, has become feasible with contextualized token embeddings. A word type can be represented as a cloud of token vectors, with dispersion-based statistics serving as proxies for contextual diversity (Nagata and Tanaka-Ishii, ACL2025). These measurements are useful for deciding appropriate sense distinctions when constructing thesauri and domain-specific dictionaries. However, when comparing the breadth of two word types, naive hypothesis testing on dispersion can be misleading: differences in semantic direction can masquerade as dispersion differences, inflating Type-I error and yielding "statistically significant" outcomes even when there is no true breadth difference. This is problematic because significance testing should distinguish genuine effects from incidental fluctuations in small-difference regimes. We propose a Householder-aligned permutation test to isolate dispersion differences from directional differences. Our method applies a single Householder reflection to align the mean directions of the two word types and then performs a permutation test on the aligned token clouds, yielding calibrated, non-parametric p-values. For practicality, we introduce a GPU-oriented implementation that batches permutations and linear algebra operations. Empirically, our alignment reduced Type-I error by 32.5% while preserving sensitivity to genuine breadth differences, and achieved a 23x speedup over the CPU baseline.

</details>


### [193] [CA-SQL: Complexity-Aware Inference Time Reasoning for Text-to-SQL via Exploration and Compute Budget Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08057)
*James Petullo,Nianwen Xue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CA-SQL，一种基于任务难度动态扩展搜索空间的Text-to-SQL方法，通过进化搜索启发式提示播种和新型投票机制，在BIRD基准的困难层级上使用GPT-4o-mini达到51.72%的SOTA性能，整体执行准确率61.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL方法在BIRD基准中最困难任务上表现不佳，根本原因在于解空间探索不充分，难以发现有望进一步精炼为正确答案的候选查询。

Method: 1) 基于任务难度估计动态调整候选解探索广度；2) 受进化搜索启发的自定义提示播种方法，增强基础LLM的探索行为；3) 新型投票机制从搜索生成的候选解中选出最佳方案。

Result: 在BIRD开发集困难层级上达到51.72%（SOTA），整体执行准确率61.06%，Soft F1得分68.77%，超越了包括使用更大模型在内的其他上下文学习方法。

Conclusion: CA-SQL证明了通过动态扩展搜索空间和进化启发式探索策略，即使使用较小模型也能在Text-to-SQL最困难任务上取得领先性能，为推理时计算资源的自适应分配提供了有效范式。

Abstract: While recent advancements in inference-time learning have improved LLM reasoning on Text-to-SQL tasks, current solutions still struggle to perform well on the most challenging tasks in the Bird-Bench (BIRD) benchmark. This is due to inadequate solution space exploration, which is necessary to uncover promising candidate queries that can be further refined to produce the correct output. To address this challenge, we introduce CA-SQL, a novel Text-to-SQL pipeline that utilizes the estimated difficulty of a task to dynamically scale the breadth of the exploration for generating solution candidates. In addition, we use a custom prompt seeding method, based on principles of evolutionary search, to further elicit exploratory behavior from the base LLM and a novel voting method to select the best candidate solution at the end of the search. Experiments demonstrate that our solution achieves a state-of-the-art score of 51.72% on the "challenging" tier of BIRD development set problems, using only GPT-4o-mini, out-performing other in-context learning approaches, even those that leverage larger models. Overall, our method attains a competitive 61.06% execution accuracy and 68.77% Soft F1 score on the BIRD development dataset.

</details>


### [194] [The Memory Curse: How Expanded Recall Erodes Cooperative Intent in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08060)
*Jiayuan Liu,Tianqin Li,Shiyi Du,Xin Luo,Haoxuan Zeng,Emanuel Tewolde,Tai Sing Lee,Tonghan Wang,Carl Kingsford,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究揭示扩展LLM上下文窗口在多智能体社会困境中会系统性破坏合作行为（18/28个模型-游戏设置），通过分析37.8万条推理轨迹发现核心机制是前瞻性意图的侵蚀而非偏执加剧。记忆消毒实验证明触发因素是记忆内容而非长度，CoT推理会放大这一"记忆诅咒"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将上下文窗口扩展视为LLM能力的简单升级，但忽略了在多智能体社会困境中记忆长度增加可能产生的负面影响，本文旨在系统性地探究这一现象及其机制。

Method: 对7个LLM在4类博弈游戏中进行了500轮实验，随后对37.8万条推理轨迹进行词汇分析，利用LoRA微调作为认知探针，进行记忆消毒实验（固定提示长度但替换可见历史），以及消融显式CoT推理。

Result: 扩展上下文窗口在28个模型-游戏设定中的18个中降低了合作水平。核心机制是前瞻性意图（forward-looking intent）的退化而非偏执（paranoia）增加。记忆内容而非长度是触发因素。CoT推理会放大而非缓解该诅咒。

Conclusion: 记忆是多智能体行为的主动决定因素，更长的上下文回忆既能破坏也能支持合作，具体取决于所引发的推理模式。在扩展上下文窗口时需谨慎设计其内容与推理结构。

Abstract: Context window expansion is often treated as a straightforward capability upgrade for LLMs, but we find it systematically fails in multi-agent social dilemmas. Across 7 LLMs and 4 games over 500 rounds, expanding accessible history degrades cooperation in 18 of 28 model--game settings, a pattern we term the memory curse. We isolate the underlying mechanism through three analyses. First, lexical analysis of 378,000 reasoning traces associates this breakdown with eroding forward-looking intent rather than rising paranoia. We validate this using targeted fine-tuning as a cognitive probe: a LoRA adapter trained exclusively on forward-looking traces mitigates the decay and transfers zero-shot to distinct games. Second, memory sanitization holds prompt length fixed while replacing visible history with synthetic cooperative records, which restores cooperation substantially, proving the trigger is memory content, not length alone. Finally, ablating explicit Chain-of-Thought reasoning often reduces the collapse, showing that deliberation paradoxically amplifies the memory curse. Together, these results recast memory as an active determinant of multi-agent behavior: longer recall can either destabilize or support cooperation depending on the reasoning patterns it elicits.

</details>


### [195] [LLMs Improving LLMs: Agentic Discovery for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08083)
*Tong Zheng,Haolin Liu,Chengsong Huang,Huiwen Bao,Sheng Zhang,Rui Liu,Runpeng Dai,Ruibo Chen,Chenxi Liu,Tianyi Xiong,Xidong Wu,Hongming Zhang,Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AutoTTS框架，将测试时缩放策略从人工设计转变为环境驱动的自动发现，通过宽度-深度控制与贝塔参数化，以低成本高效搜索最优推理策略，在数学推理基准上超越人工基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放策略依赖人工设计推理模式和启发式调参，探索空间有限，缺乏自动化发现机制。

Method: 提出AutoTTS框架，核心是环境构建：将宽度-深度TTS建模为基于预收集推理轨迹和探测信号的控制器合成问题，控制器决定分支、继续、探测、剪枝或停止；引入贝塔参数化使搜索可处理，并通过细粒度执行轨迹反馈提高发现效率。

Result: 在数学推理基准上，自动发现的策略在准确率-代价权衡上优于强人工基线；策略可泛化到未见基准和模型规模；整个发现过程仅花费39.9美元和160分钟。

Conclusion: 通过将TTS策略设计从人工启发式转向环境驱动的自动发现，可以更高效地探索计算分配空间，发现更优策略，且成本极低。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) has become an effective approach for improving large language model performance by allocating additional computation during inference. However, existing TTS strategies are largely hand-crafted: researchers manually design reasoning patterns and tune heuristics by intuition, leaving much of the computation-allocation space unexplored. We propose an environment-driven framework, AutoTTS, that changes what researchers design: from individual TTS heuristics to environments where TTS strategies can be discovered automatically. The key to AutoTTS lies in environment construction: the discovery environment must make the control space tractable and provide cheap, frequent feedback for TTS search. As a concrete instantiation, we formulate width--depth TTS as controller synthesis over pre-collected reasoning trajectories and probe signals, where controllers decide when to branch, continue, probe, prune, or stop and can be evaluated cheaply without repeated LLM calls. We further introduce beta parameterization to make the search tractable and fine-grained execution trace feedback to improve discovery efficiency by helping the agent diagnose why a TTS program fails. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that the discovered strategies improve the overall accuracy--cost tradeoff over strong manually designed baselines. The discovered strategies generalize to held-out benchmarks and model scales, while the entire discovery costs only $39.9 and 160 minutes. Our data, and code will be open-source at https://github.com/zhengkid/AutoTTS.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Modular Lie Algebraic PDE Control of Multibody Flexible Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06709)
*Sadeq Yaqubi,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对柔性多体机器人系统，提出了一种基于偏微分方程（PDE）的子系统控制框架，利用旋量理论和李代数模型，通过应变基变形PDE消除分布弹性加速度，结合自适应控制和复合Lyapunov函数，实现了任意长度串联机械臂的全局指数收敛跟踪控制，并保证弹性变形有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性机器人控制方法在处理多连杆、3D空间中的任意长度串联机械臂时，面临模型复杂、交互耦合难以精确补偿、稳定性证明难以模块化扩展等问题。本文旨在建立一个基于PDE的子系统框架，使得控制器的稳定性证明能够模块化、可扩展地适用于任意长度连杆的柔性机器人系统。

Method: 采用旋量理论李代数模型，将运动、变形和力表达为se(3)上的体固定旋量和力旋量；通过应变基变形PDE代入动力学方程消除分布弹性加速度；设计偏转补偿逆运动学生成子系统旋量轨迹；为每个连杆设计名义控制器保证旋量误差指数衰减（Lyapunov函数ν_i）；引入自适应修正用在线估计替代物理参数；构建复合Lyapunov函数V=∑ν_i和V^a=∑ν_i^a，利用牛顿第三定律和se(3)×se*(3)上功率配对的框架不变性使连杆间交互功率项 telescoping 为零，实现全局指数收敛。

Result: 建立了全局指数收敛的跟踪误差稳定性证明；通过Euler-Bernoulli能量论证保证了弹性变形有界；旋量理论结构使交互抵消精确成立，稳定性证明模块化且可扩展到任意长度链式结构；数值模拟验证了方案的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于PDE的子系统控制框架成功地解决了柔性多体机器人系统的模块化稳定控制问题，其旋量理论结构使得交互功率项精确抵消，为任意长度串联机械臂提供了可扩展的全局指数收敛控制方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses PDE-based control for flexible multibody robotic systems, presenting a subsystem-based framework for serial manipulators with arbitrary links in 3D space. The approach uses a screw-theoretic Lie-algebraic model where motion, deformation, and forces are expressed as body-fixed twists and wrenches in se(3). By substituting a strain-based deformation PDE into the dynamics, distributed elastic acceleration is eliminated, yielding a model governed by twist acceleration and the deformation field. Subsystem twist trajectories are generated from task-space endpoints via deflection-compensating inverse kinematics, providing real-time correction for tip deformation. A nominal controller for each link ensures exponential decay of twist errors via a Lyapunov function nu_i. An adaptive modification replaces physical parameters with online estimates, establishing exponential convergence of both twist and parameter errors. Summing over all links, composite Lyapunov functions V = sum(nu_i) and V^a = sum(nu_i^a) yield time derivatives where inter-link interaction power terms telescope to zero. This cancellation is ensured by Newton's third law and the frame invariance of the power pairing on se(3) x se*(3), establishing global exponential convergence of tracking errors. Bounded elastic deformation is guaranteed by an Euler-Bernoulli energy argument. The screw-theoretic structure renders interaction cancellation exact, making the stability certificate modular and scalable to chains of arbitrary length. Numerical simulations demonstrate the scheme's physical consistency.

</details>


### [197] [An Aerial Manipulator for Perception-Driven Flower Targeting Toward Contactless Pollination in Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06759)
*Chenzhe Jin,Zhuohang Wu,Yifan Cai,Xiangqi Li,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空中机械臂的感知驱动花朵检测、定位与接近平台，用于垂直农业环境中的精准授粉。系统集成了RGBD感知、MPPI无人机控制和2自由度机械臂，在仿真和真实实验中实现了厘米级末端定位精度，为未来非接触式授粉系统奠定了硬件基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然传粉者减少对可控室内农业（尤其是垂直农业）作物生产构成重大挑战，需要开发能够精确执行花朵定位任务且最小化对脆弱花结构物理干扰的机器人系统。

Method: 提出空中机械臂平台，集成RGBD感知、基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的无人机控制（PX4平台）以及轻量化2自由度机械臂，实现末端执行器精确定位。在MuJoCo仿真和真实无人机实验室中进行花朵定位测试平台评估。

Result: 实验表明无人机飞行稳定、花朵定位可靠、末端定位精度达厘米级。仿真中控制器实现一致轨迹收敛和精确目标对齐；真实环境中集成框架在受限空中操作下实现稳定的花朵定位和末端对齐。

Conclusion: 验证了所提空中机械臂可作为稳健的机器人载体和定位框架，为未来非接触式授粉系统（包括基于声学的花粉操控模块）提供实践基础。

Abstract: The decline of natural pollinators has created a major challenge for crop production in controlled indoor agriculture, particularly in vertical farming environments where natural insect pollination is absent. This motivates the development of robotic systems capable of performing precise flower targeting tasks while minimizing physical interference with delicate floral structures. This paper presents an aerial manipulator platform for perception driven flower detection, localization, and approach in vertical farming environments. The proposed system integrates onboard RGBD based perception, model predictive path integral (MPPI) based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) control on a PX4 platform, and a lightweight 2DoF manipulator for precise end effector positioning. The platform is evaluated in both MuJoCo simulation and UAV lab experiments using a flower targeting testbed. The experimental results demonstrate stable UAV flight, reliable flower localization, and centimeter level end effector positioning accuracy. In simulation, the proposed controller achieves consistent trajectory convergence and accurate target alignment. In the real world UAV lab environment, the integrated perception control manipulation framework enables stable flower targeted positioning and end effector alignment under constrained aerial operation. These results validate the proposed aerial manipulator as a robust robotic carrier and positioning framework for future contactless pollination systems. While the current study focuses on perception guided targeting and positioning, the developed platform provides a practical foundation for integrating advanced contactless end effectors, including acoustic based pollen manipulation modules, in future work.

</details>


### [198] [Bi3: A Biplatform, Bicultural, Biperson Dataset for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06863)
*Andrew Stratton,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Pranav Goyal,Rachid Alami,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bi3数据集，这是一个在受限实验室空间中采集的社交机器人导航数据集，包含74名参与者、两种机器人平台、五种导航算法，在10.5小时的多模态数据中记录了人-机器人近距离导航交互，为密集人群中的机器人导航研究提供了独特基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人导航数据集缺乏在受限空间中人-机器人近距离交互的场景，需要更具多样性和建模复杂度的基准来支持人类运动预测模型和机器人导航控制策略的训练。

Method: 设计了原始实验方案，在受限实验室空间中让两名人类与一台机器人产生近距离导航相遇；使用两种不同机器人平台和五种不同导航算法；从美国和法国两个地点招募了74名参与者；采集了10.5小时的模态数据，包括人和机器人的地面真实运动轨迹、RGB视频以及用户对机器人性能的感知评价。

Result: 通过交互密度和人类速度等指标分析表明，Bi3数据集具有独特的多样性和建模复杂度，代表了社交机器人导航领域的新基准。

Conclusion: Bi3数据集有助于理解人类与机器人在受限环境中如何有效协调活动，可作为人群密集空间中人类运动预测模型和机器人导航控制策略训练的重要资源。

Abstract: We contribute Bi3, a dataset of social robot navigation among groups of people in a constrained lab space. Compared to prior data collection efforts for social robot navigation, our dataset is unique in that it features: an original experiment design giving rise to close navigation encounters between two humans and a robot; five different navigation algorithms; two different robot platforms; a diverse participant pool of 74 people recruited from two sites in the USA and France; multimodal data streams including 10.5 hours of human and robot ground-truth motion tracks, RGB video, and user impressions over robot performance. Our analysis of the collected dataset through metrics like interaction density and human velocity suggests that Bi3 represents a benchmark of unique diversity and modeling complexity. Bi3 contributes towards understanding how humans and robots can productively mesh their activities in constrained environments, and can be a resource for training models of human motion prediction and robot control policies for navigation in densely crowded spaces.

</details>


### [199] [Traffic Scenario Orchestration from Language via Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06966)
*Frieda Rong,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于基础模型推理的约束求解方法，将自然语言描述的场景需求转化为约束条件，通过求解器生成闭环测试用例，用于自动驾驶车辆仿真测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要大量仿真测试，但测试用例生成既繁琐又难以兼顾精确可控性和规模化；手工编程精确但难以扩展，统计模型可扩展但难以实现分布外场景的精确可控。

Method: 将场景编排视为约束求解问题，利用基础模型（大语言模型）将自然语言描述转化为约束集合作为场景表示，然后调用现成求解器求解满足精确测试意图的actor行为，在闭环条件下进行测试。

Result: 在精心设计的多样化场景描述基准测试中，该方法在编排成功率上大幅超越基线方法；闭环方法对需要自车反应性（ego-reactive）规约的场景尤其重要。

Conclusion: 提出的语言输入-仿真输出场景编排器有效结合了自然语言的可理解性与约束求解的精确性，在自动驾驶闭环测试中取得了显著更好的场景编排效果。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) require extensive testing in simulation, but test case generation for driving scenarios is laborious. The desired scenarios are often out-of-distribution and have precise requirements on interactions with the AV policy under test. Manually programming scenarios allows for precise controllability but is difficult to scale. On the other hand, statistical models can leverage compute and data, but struggle with precise controllability when out-of-distribution. We cast scenario orchestration as a constraint-solving problem and present a language-in, simulation-out scenario orchestrator for closed-loop testing AVs. Our approach leverages foundation model reasoning to translate general, natural language descriptions into a set of constraints as a scenario representation. This then allows us to leverage off the shelf solvers to solve for actor behaviors which meet precise testing intentions in closed-loop. Under a benchmark of carefully crafted and diverse scenario descriptions, our approach greatly outperforms our baselines in orchestration success rate. We further show that our closed-loop approach is especially important for scenarios which require ego-reactive specifications.

</details>


### [200] [AirBender: Adaptive Transportation of Bendable Objects Using Dual UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07003)
*Jiawei Xu,Longsen Gao,Rafael Fierro,David Saldaña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应控制器，使两台多旋翼飞行器能够协同运输柔性弯曲物体，无需依赖弹性模型，即可在线适应物体未知的变形特性，并通过Lyapunov分析证明其渐近稳定性，硬件实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人与柔性物体交互时，由于飞行器驱动力有限且需持续悬空，控制难度大，易导致性能下降甚至坠毁。现有方法依赖精确的弹性模型，难以应对未知变形特性。

Method: 设计自适应控制器，不依赖显式弹性模型，通过在线自适应机制应对物体未知的变形特性；采用Lyapunov稳定性分析证明控制器的渐近稳定性。

Result: 通过多种场景下的硬件实验验证了该方法在多旋翼飞行器协同操控柔性物体方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应控制器能够在无弹性模型先验知识的情况下，实现两架飞行器协作搬运柔性物体的稳定轨迹跟踪，理论分析与实验共同证明了该方法的可行性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: The interaction of robots with bendable objects in midair presents significant challenges in control, often resulting in performance degradation and potential crashes, especially for aerial robots due to their limited actuation capabilities and constant need to remain airborne. This paper presents an adaptive controller that enables two aerial vehicles to collaboratively follow a trajectory while transporting a bendable object without relying on explicit elasticity models. Our method allows on-the-fly adaptation to the object's unknown deformable properties, ensuring stability and performance in trajectory-tracking tasks. We use Lyapunov analysis to demonstrate that our adaptive controller is asymptotically stable. Our method is evaluated through hardware experiments in various scenarios, demonstrating the capabilities of using multirotor aerial vehicles to handle bendable objects.

</details>


### [201] [Intention assimilation control for accurate tracking with variable impedance in teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07037)
*Atsushi Takagi,Yanan Li,Hiroaki Gomi,Etienne Burdet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种意图同化控制（IAC）策略，通过估计领导者的目标位置而非当前位置，在低刚度下仍能保证高跟踪精度，解决了遥操作中跟踪精度与安全性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥操作中，跟踪精度与安全性之间存在固有矛盾：低刚度安全性好但跟踪精度差，高刚度跟踪精度好但危险。现有弹簧类力控制方法无法同时满足两者。

Method: 提出IAC策略，不再直接传递领导者的当前位置，而是估计领导者的目标位置并传递给从端。从端阻抗可动态调整，以反映用户期望阻抗或根据任务自动调节。

Result: 在双7自由度机械臂上验证，与遥阻抗控制（TIC）相比，在自由跟踪、气球交互、插销插入、桌面抛光四项实验中，IAC提高了跟踪精度、任务完成率并缩短了完成时间。

Conclusion: IAC使机器人在低刚度下仍能精确复现用户运动，同时允许用户根据意图自由调节阻抗，在单/双边遥操作中实现了前所未有的位置和阻抗控制水平。

Abstract: Robot systems for teleoperation commonly use a spring-like force pulling the follower robot towards the leader's position to track their movements. With this control strategy, the tracking accuracy deteriorates when the follower' stiffness is low, but high stiffness poses a danger to objects or people in the follower robot's environment. To address this trade-off between tracking accuracy and safety, we propose an alternative intention assimilation control (IAC) strategy where the robot's tracking accuracy can be ensured without high stiffness. Different from traditional approaches, which transmit the leader's current position to the follower, this new controller estimates the leader's target position and transmits it to the follower. With this strategy, the follower impedance can be changed on-the-fly to continuously reflect the user's desired impedance or modulated automatically to fulfill the task requirements. Our controller was validated on two 7 degree-of-freedom manipulators, yielding high tracking accuracy with varying impedance. Four experiments were conducted to compare {teleoperation} with IAC to tele-impedance control (TIC) during free tracking, interaction with a balloon, during peg insertion, and table polishing with force feedback. The results show that IAC increases tracking accuracy, improves task completion rate and reduces completion time. IAC enables the robot to accurately replicate the user's movement while giving them freedom to modulate the impedance according to their intention, providing an unprecedented level of control of the follower's position and its impedance during unilateral and bilateral teleoperation.

</details>


### [202] [Dr-BA: Separable Optimization for Direct Radar Bundle Adjustment & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07041)
*Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dr-BA，首个直接对2D旋转雷达强度图像进行处理的雷达束调整框架，通过可分离优化公式解耦位姿估计与建图，实现密集地图构建和传感器位姿联合估计，并在200+公里道路数据上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达在降水条件下具有鲁棒性，是自动驾驶全天气候感知的关键模态，但现有方法仅从雷达回波中提取稀疏点云进行配准，未能充分利用完整雷达信息。作者旨在利用多扫描全雷达回波进行联合位姿估计与密集建图。

Method: 将雷达束调整问题构建为可分离优化问题，将位姿估计与建图解耦，得到高效且通用的求解方案；该公式自然扩展到基于预建地图的直接雷达定位（DRL）。

Result: 在五条不同路线、超过200公里的道路数据上，Dr-BA实现了SOTA的雷达束调整和跨会话定位性能。

Conclusion: Dr-BA直接利用2D雷达强度图像的全回波数据进行雷达束调整，通过可分离优化实现了高效且高性能的联合建图与定位，代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-BA, a first-of-its-kind radar bundle adjustment (BA) framework that operates directly on 2D spinning radar intensity images. Unlike camera or lidar sensors, radar is largely unaffected by precipitation, making it a critical modality for autonomous systems that require all-weather robustness. Existing state estimation approaches using spinning radar typically extract sparse point clouds from range-azimuth-intensity measurements and apply point cloud alignment techniques to estimate vehicle motion, scene structure, or to localize within an existing map. In contrast, Dr-BA uses the full radar returns from multiple scans to jointly estimate dense maps and sensor poses. By formulating the problem as a separable optimization, we derive an efficient and general solution that decouples pose estimation from mapping. In addition to solving the BA problem, this formulation naturally extends to direct radar-only localization (DRL) within a previously built map. Dr-BA achieves state-of-the-art radar-based BA and cross-session localization performance, demonstrated on more than 200 km of on-road data across five distinct routes. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_ba.

</details>


### [203] [PISTO: Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07215)
*Hongzhe Yu,Zinuo Chang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PISTO算法，通过揭示STOMP的变分推断结构并引入KL正则化近端项，实现更稳定、高效的随机轨迹优化，在机械臂运动规划中达到89%成功率，优于CHOMP和STOMP，并在接触丰富的MuJoCo任务中超越CEM和MPPI基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STOMP虽然能处理不可微成本函数，但其更新机制缺乏清晰的概率解释，导致稳定性不足。本文旨在揭示STOMP的变分推断本质，并通过近端正则化提升其稳定性和性能。

Method: 1) 揭示STOMP隐式最小化Boltzmann轨迹分布的KL散度，建立变分推断视角；2) 提出PISTO，在连续高斯提议分布之间引入KL正则化作为近端项，形成信任区域约束；3) 推导出闭式均值更新，通过重要性加权蒙特卡洛采样估计期望，得到无导数算法。

Result: 在机械臂运动规划中，PISTO成功率达89%，优于CHOMP(63%)和STOMP(68%)，且路径更短更平滑，速度是其他随机方法的两倍。在MuJoCo任务中，PISTO在奖励指标上一致优于CEM和MPPI。

Conclusion: PISTO通过变分推断视角和近端正则化显著提升了随机轨迹优化的稳定性和性能，保持了处理不可微/不连续成本函数的能力，是一种简单、高效、无导数的轨迹优化算法。

Abstract: Stochastic trajectory optimization methods like STOMP enable planning with non-differentiable costs, offering substantial flexibility over gradient-based approaches. We show that STOMP implicitly minimizes the KL divergence from a Boltzmann trajectory distribution, revealing an elegant Variational Inference (VI) structure underlying its updates. Building on this insight, we propose the \textit{Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization} (PISTO) algorithm that stabilizes the updates by augmenting the objective with a KL regularization between successive Gaussian proposals. This proximal formulation admits a trust-region interpretation and yields closed-form mean updates computable as expectations under a surrogate distribution. We estimate these expectations via importance-weighted Monte Carlo sampling, producing a simple, derivative-free algorithm that inherits STOMP's ability to handle non-differentiable and discontinuous costs without modification. On robot arm motion planning benchmarks, PISTO achieves an 89\% success rate -- outperforming CHOMP (63\%) and STOMP (68\%) -- while producing shorter, smoother paths at twice the speed of competing stochastic methods. We further validate PISTO on contact-rich MuJoCo locomotion and manipulation tasks, where it consistently outperforms both CEM and MPPI baselines in reward.

</details>


### [204] [Palm-sized Omnidirectional Vision-Based UAV Exploration with Sparse Topological Map Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07275)
*Zirui Wang,Xinjia Luo,Haotian Sun,Jun Ma,Jian Guo,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级自主探索系统，利用全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图引导，通过多鱼眼相机实现全向视野和深度估计，将前沿表示为拓扑节点而非显式边界，在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划，显著降低了内存消耗和计算开销，并在仅400克重、11厘米轴距的微型无人机上完成了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖密集占据地图或高精度点云，内存和计算开销大；微型无人机受尺寸、重量和功率（SWaP）限制，难以搭载激光雷达等传感器获取精确环境几何测量。

Method: 采用多鱼眼相机系统实现全向视野和深度估计；将前沿区域表示为拓扑节点而非显式边界，避免维护占据网格或全局点云；构建由关键节点及其描述符组成的稀疏拓扑地图；直接在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实世界实验中，在仅400克重量、11厘米轴距的掌上视觉无人机上验证了该方法能够以极低计算消耗实现高效探索。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图的轻量级探索系统，有效克服了传统密集表示方法在资源受限平台上的局限性，实现了低计算开销下的高效自主探索。

Abstract: Classic exploration methods often rely on dense occupancy maps or high-resolution point clouds for frontier detection and path planning, resulting in substantial memory consumption and computational overhead. Moreover, micro UAVs under size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints are not practical to be equipped with sensors like LiDAR to obtain accurate environmental geometric measurements. This paper presents a lightweight autonomous exploration system that leverages omnidirectional vision and sparse topological map guidance. Specifically, we utilize a multi-fisheye camera setup to achieve omnidirectional Field of View (FoV) and perform depth estimation. To address the limited depth estimation accuracy, frontiers are represented as potential unexplored regions characterized by topological nodes instead of explicit boundaries, enabling efficient identification of frontier regions without maintaining occupancy grids or global point clouds. Unlike classic dense representations, our approach abstracts the environment using a sparse topological map composed of key nodes and their descriptors, reducing memory consumption and computational demands. Global path planning is performed directly on the sparse graph. The proposed method is validated in both simulation and on a palm-sized vision-based UAV with an 11 cm wheelbase and a 400 g weight in real-world experiments, demonstrating that our method can achieve efficient exploration with extremely low computational consumption.

</details>


### [205] [Variable Aerodynamic Damping via Co-Contraction: A Dynamic Isomorphism with Variable Stiffness Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07292)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明了冗余双旋翼执行器中的气动共收缩可以通过调节被动配平定义的气动机械阻尼，同时保持命令净力恒定。基于叶素理论推导的简化推力模型，建立了单调递增的阻尼系数，并将其形式化为可变气动阻尼执行器（VADA），揭示了其与拮抗变刚度执行器（VSA）中刚度调制的动态同构性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索冗余双旋翼执行器中通过气动共收缩独立调节阻尼同时保持净力恒定的机制，为多旋翼系统的阻抗控制提供新自由度。

Method: 提出增量阻尼系数的定义，基于叶素理论推导最小推力模型（仿射于入流），验证单调性条件；通过纤维密度原理和共收缩类比建立VADA与VSA的动态同构性，并用阻抗形式表示共模/差模驱动的作用。

Result: 验证了在气动硬化条件下阻尼系数沿等力纤维单调递增；推导的模型显式揭示了速度-入流耦合驱动效应；证明了VADA与拮抗变刚度执行器的动态同构性；纤维密度原理还可增强冗余多旋翼的主动气动响应速度。

Conclusion: 气动共收缩为多旋翼执行器提供了一种调节被动阻抗的新机制，类似生物肌肉共收缩的肌腱刚度调制，扩展了冗余驱动系统的阻抗控制能力。

Abstract: We prove that aerodynamic co-contraction in a redundant dual-rotor actuator can tune a passive, trim-defined aero-mechanical damping while keeping the commanded net force constant. In particular, we define an incremental damping coefficient as the local sensitivity of net thrust to air-relative velocity at a trim and prove that it increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under a mild aerodynamic hardening condition. We then validate the required damping and hardening properties from a first-principles Blade Element Theory derivation, which yields a minimal thrust model affine in inflow and explicitly reveals the speed--inflow coupling driving the effect. The resulting mechanism is formalized as a Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuator (VADA) and shown to be dynamically isomorphic to stiffness modulation in antagonistic variable-stiffness actuation (VSA), similar to the co-contraction of tendons by muscle co-activation. The same fiber-density principle also enhances the active aerodynamic promptness measure of redundant multirotors. Finally, an impedance-form representation clarifies the roles of common-mode and differential-mode actuation in the control of passive impedance and the equilibrium velocity of the VADA system.

</details>


### [206] [BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306)
*Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Hongmei Fei,Xiwen Cao,Ting Xiao,Qi Wang,Huanbo Jin,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出BioProVLA-Agent，一个基于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的协议驱动、视觉增强的具身多智能体系统，用于生物实验室自动化操作。系统将实验协议作为任务接口，通过协议解析、视觉状态验证和具身执行构成闭环流程，并引入AugSmolVLA在线增强策略应对湿实验室视觉扰动。在包含15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务的层次化基准测试中，该系统在常规和高曝光条件下均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验室自动化可减少重复性人工操作并提高可重复性，但湿实验室环境中的可靠具身执行仍面临挑战：协议常非结构化、器皿透明/反射性强、多步骤程序需要状态感知执行。现有机器人系统依赖昂贵硬件、固定工作流、专用仪器或面向机器人的接口，缺乏经济高效且协议驱动的解决方案。

Method: 系统由三个核心智能体组成：1) Tailored LLM Protocol Agent将自然语言实验协议解析为可验证的子任务；2) VLM-RAG Verification Agent利用观察、机器人状态、检索知识和成功/失败示例评估子任务准备状态和完成情况；3) VLA Embodied Agent通过轻量级策略执行已验证的子任务。同时提出AugSmolVLA在线增强策略，针对透明器皿、反射、光照变化和曝光过度等湿实验室视觉扰动进行鲁棒性改进。

Result: 在覆盖15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务的层次化基准上评估，AugSmolVLA在常规和高曝光设置下在精确放置、透明物体操作、复合工作流和视觉退化场景中的执行稳定性均优于ACT、X-VLA和原始SmolVLA。

Conclusion: 该结果表明了构建经济可及、以协议为中心且具备验证能力的生物操作具身AI的可行路径。

Abstract: Biological laboratory automation can reduce repetitive manual work and improve reproducibility, but reliable embodied execution in wet-lab environments remains challenging. Protocols are often unstructured, labware is frequently transparent or reflective, and multi-step procedures require state-aware execution beyond one-shot instruction following. Existing robotic systems often rely on costly hardware, fixed workflows, dedicated instruments, or robotics-oriented interfaces. Here, we introduce BioProVLA-Agent, an affordable, protocol-driven, vision-enhanced embodied multi-agent system enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for biological manipulation. The system uses protocols as the task interface and integrates protocol parsing, visual state verification, and embodied execution in a closed-loop workflow. A Tailored LLM Protocol Agent converts protocols into verifiable subtasks; a VLM-RAG Verification Agent assesses readiness and completion using observations, robot states, retrieved knowledge, and success/failure examples; and a VLA Embodied Agent executes verified subtasks through a lightweight policy. To improve robustness under wet-lab visual perturbations, we develop AugSmolVLA, an online augmentation strategy targeting transparent labware, reflections, illumination shifts, and overexposure. We evaluate the system on a hierarchical benchmark covering 15 atomic tasks, 6 composite workflows, and 3 bimanual tasks, including tube loading, sorting, waste disposal, cap twisting, and liquid pouring. Across normal and high-exposure settings, AugSmolVLA improves execution stability over ACT, X-VLA, and the original SmolVLA, especially for precise placement, transparent-object manipulation, composite workflows, and visually degraded scenes. These results suggest a practical route toward accessible, protocol-centered, and verification-capable embodied AI for biological manipulation.

</details>


### [207] [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308)
*Xiaoqi Li,Muhe Cai,Jiadong Xu,Juan Zhu,Hongwei Fan,Yan Shen,Guangrui Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AT-VLA通过自适应触觉注入机制和触觉反应双流机制，在保持预训练VLA能力的同时，实现了接触丰富操作场景中的实时触觉反馈（0.04秒闭环响应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在接触丰富操作任务中表现不佳，且引入触觉信号进行微调会破坏预训练能力，同时VLA推理速度慢限制了触觉反馈的有效利用。

Method: 提出自适应触觉注入机制（动态决定触觉注入的时机和位置，最小化对预训练表征的干扰）和触觉反应双流机制（将感知处理解耦为慢速视觉-语言流用于低频推理，快速触觉控制流用于高频物理交互，实现0.04秒实时闭环响应）。

Result: 在真实世界的接触丰富操作任务中验证了AT-VLA的有效性。

Conclusion: AT-VLA通过自适应触觉注入和双流解耦设计，有效解决了触觉模态引入对预训练能力的干扰以及实时响应问题，为接触丰富操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significantly advanced the capabilities of robotic agents in executing diverse tasks; however, they still face challenges in contact-rich manipulation scenarios that require precise physical interactions. To address this limitation, recent studies have attempted to incorporate tactile signals during downstream tasks, enabling pretrained VLAs to interpret tactile feedback. Nevertheless, introducing new modalities during finetuning, which are rarely present in the pretrain stage, may disrupt the pretrained capabilities of VLAs. In addition, the inherently slow inference speed of VLAs hampers real-time responsiveness and limits the effective utilization of tactile feedback for action adjustment. To overcome these challenges, we propose Adaptive Tactile Vision-Language-Action (AT-VLA), which introduces a novel Adaptive Tactile Injection mechanism. This mechanism dynamically determines the appropriate timing and locations for tactile injection, incorporating only when it significantly contributes to action generation, thereby minimizing interference with pretrained representations. Furthermore, to enable rapid and accurate tactile responses, we propose a Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream mechanism, which decouples sensory processing into a slow visual-language stream for low-frequency perceptual reasoning and a fast tactile control stream for high-frequency physical interaction understanding, achieving real-time close-loop responses within 0.04 s. Real-world experiments thoroughly validate the effectiveness of AT-VLA in contact-rich manipulation tasks. The project page is available at: https://sites.google.com/view/at-vla.

</details>


### [208] [CSR: Infinite-Horizon Real-Time Policies with Massive Cached State Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07325)
*Robin Karlsson,Go Suzui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSR框架（缓存状态表示）和ASR算法（异步状态协调），通过理论证明和实践实现，在机器人部署中实现大规模LLM的实时推理。CSR利用KV缓存复用将120K token上下文的TTFT延迟从14.67s降至0.56s（26倍提升），ASR通过并行状态内存驱逐消除延迟尖峰，使235B参数的LLM能够以>2Hz的频率作为连续运行的具身策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM作为机器人连续认知引擎部署时，处理大量状态历史所需的TTFT延迟（首次令牌生成时间）成为瓶颈。现有方案如RAG或滑动窗口会损害全局上下文或带来高昂的重新计算成本。

Method: 首先形式化最优任务结构的最小延迟条件，理论上证明了前缀稳定性、增量可扩展性和异步状态协调是实现实时性能的必要条件。基于这些证明，提出CSR框架（确保最优KV缓存复用）和ASR算法（通过并行计算资源卸载状态内存驱逐以消除延迟尖峰）。

Result: 在物理机器人+无线连接GPU服务器场景中，235B参数模型处理120K token上下文时，TTFT延迟从14.67s降至0.56s（26倍降低）。在具身AI基准测试中实现SOTA召回率（0.836 vs 0.459）的同时保持RAG级延迟。ASR在10次连续实际驱逐周期中验证了无尖峰的稳定TTFT。

Conclusion: CSR和ASR共同使大规模LLM能够作为连续运行的高频（>2Hz）具身策略，解决了LLM在实时机器人控制中的延迟瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Deploying massive large language models (LLMs) as continuous cognitive engines for robotics is bottlenecked by the time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency required to process extensive state histories. Existing solutions like RAG or sliding windows compromise global context or incur prohibitive re-computation costs. We formalize the optimal task structure for minimizing latency and theoretically prove that prefix stability, incremental extensibility, and asynchronous state reconciliation are necessary conditions for real-time performance. Building on these proofs, we introduce the Cached State Representation (CSR) framework as the practical instantiation of these properties, ensuring optimal KV-cache reuse. To sustain these properties over infinite horizons, we further propose an Asynchronous State Reconciliation (ASR) algorithm that offloads state memory eviction to a parallel computational resource to eliminate latency spikes. On a physical robot wirelessly connected to an on-premise GPU server, CSR achieves a 26-fold latency reduction (14.67s to 0.56s) for 120K token contexts with a 235B parameter model compared to a standard baseline. On an embodied AI benchmark, we achieve SOTA recall (0.836 vs. 0.459) while maintaining RAG-level latency. ASR is validated to sustain bounded, spike-free TTFT over 10 eviction cycles in continuous real-world operation. Together, CSR and ASR enable massive LLMs to function as continuously operating, high-frequency (> 2 Hz) embodied policies.

</details>


### [209] [Weather-Robust Scene Semantics with Vision-Aligned 4D Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07367)
*Kali Hamilton,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将毫米波雷达编码器对齐到冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入，并通过冻结的视觉语言模型解码结构化场景描述的方法。仅需约7M可训练参数，在雨、雾、雪等恶劣天气下，雷达配置的性能优于相机基线（相机基线幻觉率超90%）。发现并解决了token-norm不匹配问题，揭示了编码器复杂度、描述格式和池化策略之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机和LiDAR在雨、雾、雪等恶劣天气下性能严重下降，而毫米波雷达基本不受影响。现有视觉语言模型多依赖相机输入，在恶劣天气下不可靠，因此需要探索以雷达为主传感器的VLM方案。

Method: 将雷达编码器输出对齐到冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入，然后通过一个冻结的视觉语言模型解码生成结构化场景描述。仅训练约7M参数（雷达编码器+投影层）。使用LayerNorm解决投影器输出与冻结VLM之间的token-norm不匹配问题。在K-RADAR数据集的雾、小雪、大雪等保留序列上评估。

Result: 所有雷达配置在恶劣天气下均优于相机基线，相机基线在恶劣天气下幻觉率超过90%。识别出token-norm不匹配是雷达接入冻结VLM的主要失败模式，通过投影器输出端的LayerNorm有效解决。揭示了编码器复杂度、描述格式和池化策略之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达可作为恶劣天气下视觉语言模型的有效替代传感器。token-norm不匹配是跨模态对齐的关键问题，LayerNorm可有效缓解。研究成果为未来雷达-VLM流水线设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDAR degrade in rain, fog, and snow, while millimeter-wave radar remains largely unaffected. We align a radar encoder to frozen SigLIP vision
  embeddings and decode structured scene captions through a frozen vision-language model (VLM) with approximately 7M trainable parameters. On K-RADAR with
  held-out fog, light snow, and heavy snow sequences, all radar configurations outperform a camera baseline that collapses to over 90% hallucination. We
  identify a token-norm mismatch as the dominant failure mode when bridging radar to a frozen VLM and show that projector-output LayerNorm resolves it.
  Analysis of encoder complexity, caption format, and pooling strategy reveals tradeoffs that inform future radar-VLM pipeline design.

</details>


### [210] [MORPH-U: Multi-Objective Resilient Motion Planning for V2X-Enabled Autonomous Driving in High-Uncertainty Environments via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07370)
*Shih-Yu Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MORPH-U框架，通过融合V2X通信与车载传感器构建局部动态地图(LDM)，结合Hybrid-A*重规划与拜占庭容错门控机制，在不确定V2X环境下实现鲁棒的运动规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X通信存在延迟、丢包甚至伪造信息等不确定性，同时地图知识可能动态变化，车辆需在实时约束下重新规划。现有方法缺乏对这些不确定事件驱动更新的鲁棒性保障。

Method: 1) 融合LiDAR/雷达/相机与V2X (CAM/DENM)构建LDM；2) 在检测到有效危险或地图变化时触发Hybrid-A*重规划；3) 多目标优化（跟踪误差、安全裕度、响应性、平滑性）结合Pareto前沿分析；4) 拜占庭容错门控：法定人数规则+车载传感器否决权。

Result: V2X增强的LDM提升下游安全性；Pareto调优实现可控的精度-舒适度权衡；门控机制在饱和虚假DENM注入攻击(p_attack=1.0)下有效阻止不安全重规划。

Conclusion: MORPH-U在CARLA动态场景中验证了对V2X不确定性的鲁棒性，为自动驾驶在不可靠通信环境下的安全规划与控制提供了有效方案。

Abstract: V2X can warn an autonomous vehicle about hazards beyond line-of-sight, but it also brings uncertainty: messages may be delayed, dropped, or even forged. Meanwhile, map knowledge may change during a trip, forcing the vehicle to replan under tight real-time budgets. This paper studies how to make motion planning and low-level control robust to such uncertain, event-driven updates. We present MORPH-U, a CARLA-based closed-loop stack that fuses LiDAR/radar/camera with V2X (CAM/DENM) into a Local Dynamic Map (LDM) and triggers Hybrid-A* replanning when validated hazards or map changes affect the planned route. We expose the planning/control trade-offs via a multi-objective formulation over tracking error, safety margin (minimum TTC), responsiveness, and smoothness, and select operating points using Pareto-frontier analysis. To avoid unsafe replanning from faulty V2X triggers, MORPH-U adds a lightweight Byzantine-inspired acceptance gate that combines a quorum rule with an on-board sensor veto. Experiments in dynamic CARLA scenarios show that V2X-augmented LDM improves downstream safety, Pareto tuning provides controllable accuracy-comfort trade-offs, and the gate prevents replanning under saturated false-DENM injection ($p_{\text{attack}}=1.0$).

</details>


### [211] [Escaping the Diversity Trap in Robotic Manipulation via Anchor-Centric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07381)
*Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qi Lv,Yiqi Lin,Zechen Bai,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA模型在数据预算有限时，传统"最大化覆盖"的多样本策略会陷入"多样性陷阱"，并提出覆盖-密度权衡理论及锚点中心自适应（ACA）两阶段框架，通过重复核心锚点演示稳定策略骨架，再选择性扩展高风险边界，在真实机器人实验中优于标准多样性采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型部署到具体硬件时需要实际环境适应以弥合具身差距，但机器人演示数据获取成本高昂，需在严格数据预算下完成适应。现有"最大化覆盖"的启发式策略可能因估计噪声非零而适得其反。

Method: 首先形式化覆盖-密度权衡，将策略误差分解为估计（密度）项和外推（覆盖）项，刻画固定预算下独特条件的最优内部分配。基于此提出ACA两阶段框架：阶段一通过重复核心锚点演示稳定策略骨架；阶段二通过教师强制错误挖掘和约束残差更新选择性扩展高风险边界。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了覆盖-密度权衡框架，证明在相同预算下ACA显著优于标准多样性采样策略，提升了任务可靠性和成功率。

Conclusion: 在数据预算有限时，应优先通过重复核心锚点演示降低估计误差，再针对性地扩展高风险区域覆盖，而非盲目追求最大覆盖度。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer broad general capabilities, deploying them on specific hardware requires real-world adaptation to bridge the embodiment gap. Since robot demonstrations are costly, this adaptation must often occur under a strict data budget. In this work, we identify a critical diversity trap: the standard heuristic of "maximizing coverage" by collecting diverse, single-shot demonstrations can be self-defeating due to non-vanishing estimation noise. We formalize this phenomenon as a Coverage--Density Trade-off. By decomposing the policy error into estimation (density) and extrapolation (coverage) terms, we characterize an interior optimal allocation of unique conditions for a fixed budget. Guided by this analysis, we propose Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA), a two-stage framework that first stabilizes a policy skeleton through repeated demonstrations at core anchors, then selectively expands coverage to high-risk boundaries via teacher-forced error mining and constrained residual updates. Real-robot experiments validate our trade-off framework and demonstrate that ACA significantly improves task reliability and success rates over standard diverse sampling strategies under the same budget.

</details>


### [212] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用鸟瞰图（BEV）作为全局先验的地面/近地机器人导航系统，通过图像生成模型理解自然语言意图、识别目标并生成可通行性掩码，引入跨视角定位来对齐里程计与BEV地图以消除长期漂移，在无人机平台上完成了160米室外长距离导航任务，展示了基础模型的世界理解能力向具身导航的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鸟瞰图（BEV）能为导航提供有价值的全局先验信息，但存在两个关键挑战：如何充分利用这些全局信息，以及如何在执行过程中可靠地使用它们。

Method: 1) 使用图像生成模型从自然语言理解人类意图，识别目标目的地并生成可通行性掩码；2) 引入跨视角定位（cross-view localization）将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，以缓解传统里程计的长期漂移问题。

Result: 在无人机平台上，仅使用传统的局部运动规划器，成功完成了160米室外长距离导航任务。

Conclusion: 将基础模型（如图像生成模型）的世界理解能力迁移到具身导航中，使机器人能够受益于现有模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [213] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作-状态一致性（action-state consistency）作为评估世界动作模型（WAM）可靠性的新维度，发现其能有效区分成功与失败的rollout轨迹，并基于此提出一种无需价值函数的共识策略，在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM生成的未来预测缺乏可靠性评估：一个视觉上合理的未来生成结果，是否与所建模的动作序列在动力学上兼容（即保持动作-状态一致性）？这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1）系统对比联合预测模型和逆动力学模型，分析动作-状态一致性在成功/失败rollout中的分布；2）识别背景坍缩（background collapse）这一边界条件；3）提出无价值函数的共识策略，通过候选rollout间未来预测的一致性进行测试时选择。

Result: 动作-状态一致性在多个任务中系统性区分成功与失败的rollout，其趋势与学习到的价值估计类似；背景坍缩导致低动力学失败的轨迹因静态未来更易预测而具有欺骗性一致性；共识策略在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率，无需额外训练或奖励建模。

Conclusion: 动作-状态一致性既是评估WAM可靠性的诊断工具，也是无价值函数规划中的实用信号。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. Taken together, our findings establish action-state consistency as both a diagnostic tool for evaluating WAM reliability and a practical signal for value-free planning.

</details>


### [214] [Search-based Robustness Testing of Laptop Refurbishing Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07530)
*Erblin Isaku,Hassan Sartaj,Shaukat Ali,Malaika Din Hashmi,Francois Picard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE方法，一种基于多目标优化的搜索式鲁棒性测试方法，用于发现笔记本电脑翻新机器人目标检测模型中的故障。该方法使用NSGA-II算法搜索最小局部扰动，在诱导故障和扰动幅度间取得平衡，相比随机搜索效率提升3-7倍，且扰动可在不同模型间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笔记本电脑翻新机器人依赖目标检测模型识别螺丝、贴纸等物体，但模型对小输入变化的鲁棒性不足可能导致翻新过程中损坏设备。确保此类模型的鲁棒性对于避免潜在损失至关重要。

Method: 提出PROBE方法，采用NSGA-II多目标优化算法系统探索扰动空间，同时优化两个目标：诱导故障（考虑定位和置信度）以及扰动幅度，从而发现多样化的故障案例。

Result: PROBE生成故障诱导扰动的效率是随机搜索的3-7倍，且所需扰动幅度更小；生成的扰动可在不同模型间迁移；变质关系还能在非故障情况下提供模型稳定性评估。

Conclusion: PROBE能有效检测翻新机器人目标检测模型的鲁棒性缺陷，对支持欧洲循环经济行动计划中的电子废弃物减少目标具有实际意义。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>


### [215] [How to utilize failure demo data?: Effective data selection for imitation learning using distribution differences in attention mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07560)
*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征并将其融入注意力机制的方法，使机器人模仿学习能够有效利用数据收集过程中积累的失败数据，无需额外数据处理或自主策略迭代。实验表明该方法能提升任务成功率，并提出一种量化注意力差异的后训练指标来筛选有益失败样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法仅依赖成功示范，而数据收集中不可避免地会产生失败数据。已有的利用失败数据的方法需要额外数据处理或自主 rollout 迭代更新策略，难以直接、稳定地利用这些积累的失败数据。

Method: 1) 学习成功-失败差异的潜在表征，并将其融入注意力机制；2) 推理时从初始观测中选择合适的潜在模式以提升动作稳定性；3) 提出一种后训练度量指标，量化每个失败样本与成功示范之间的注意力差异，用于筛选有价值的失败数据。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在使用失败数据训练时能够提升任务成功率；所提出的度量指标能够识别出与成功示范结合时对学习有益的失败样本。

Conclusion: 该方法能够支持机器人数据收集管线中更高效地利用收集到的示范数据，包括原本被丢弃的失败数据。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [216] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrickCraft 是一个面向长时效、可泛化的互锁积木自主装配的组合框架，通过相对公式化建模、情境化手册和组合执行管线，实现了从有限演示中学习并泛化到未见结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需要融合长时效任务推理、空间定位和精细操作，现有方法在这三方面的集成上存在不足，BrickCraft旨在弥合高层装配规划与物理执行之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 采用相对公式化建模，每一步以局部结构中的参考积木为锚点，将复杂任务分解为有限的可复用基本技能；2) 通过情境化手册（situated manuals）提供显式空间引导，将装配意图投影到实时机器人观测上；3) 采用组合执行管线将空间定位的技能串联起来完成长时效任务。

Result: 从有限演示中习得熟练的装配技能，并对未见结构展现出强大的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft有效解决了长时效可泛化积木装配中的任务推理、空间定位与精细控制的集成问题，验证了组合式方法在机器人装配中的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>


### [217] [PhySPRING: Structure-Preserving Reduction of Physics-Informed Twins via GNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07687)
*Yixiong Jing,Xingyuan Chen,Guangming Wang,Olaf Wysocki,Haibing Wu,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhySPRING提出了一种基于可微分GNN的方法，用于在弹簧-质点数字孪生中联合学习层次化简化图拓扑及其力学参数，通过合并具有相似动态响应的节点来降低复杂度，在保持物理和视觉保真度的同时实现最高2.30倍加速，并在机器人策略评估中零样本替换成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于物理的数字孪生方法（如弹簧-质点系统）在重建时往往继承视觉重建的分辨率，而非根据任务相关的物理复杂度进行降阶，导致拓扑中存在冗余节点，使重复的前向动力学推演计算成本过高。

Method: PhySPRING采用全可微分的GNN方法，联合学习层次化简化图拓扑和力学参数。在每个简化层级中，合并具有相似学习动态响应的节点以优化拓扑，同时保持每一层都是显式的弹簧-质点系统。

Result: 在PhysTwin基准上，PhySPRING相较于PhysTwin提升了密集重建和预测精度，简化模型在保持稳定物理和视觉保真度的同时实现了最高2.30倍加速。在Real2Sim机器人策略评估流程中，简化模型可零样本替换到ACT和π_0评估中，保持可比的操作成功率并提升动作采样效率。

Conclusion: PhySPRING实现了高效且保持结构特性的弹簧-质点系统降阶，在不牺牲保真度或机器人实用性的前提下提升了计算效率。

Abstract: Physics-based digital twins aim to predict the dynamics of real-world objects under interaction, enabling real-to-sim-to-real applications in robotics. Current approaches reconstruct such twins as explicit physical models (such as spring-mass systems) to predict the dynamics, but the resulting models often inherit the resolution of the visual reconstruction rather than being reduced to the physical complexity required to reproduce task-relevant dynamics. This mismatch introduces redundant topology, making repeated forward-dynamics rollouts unnecessarily expensive. To address this challenge, we present PhySPRING, an fully differentiable GNN-based method to reduce complexity in spring--mass digital twins. PhySPRING jointly learns a hierarchy of coarsened graph topologies and their mechanical parameters from observations. At each reduction level, PhySPRING merges nodes with similar learned dynamic responses to optimize the topology, while maintaining every reduced layer as an explicit spring--mass system. On the PhysTwin benchmark, PhySPRING improves dense reconstruction and prediction accuracy over PhysTwin, while reduced models retain stable physical and visual fidelity with up to a 2.30 times speed-up. We further demonstrate the effectiveness of PhySPRING in a Real2Sim robot policy-evaluation pipeline, where the reduced models are substituted zero-shot into ACT and $π_0$ evaluations, maintaining comparable manipulation success rates across downsampling levels while improving action-sampling effectiveness.
